--- START FILE: .adapters/mason_backend/run_qlora.py ---
# Auto-generated QLoRA training harness for role: mason_backend
import os
import torch
from datasets import load_dataset
from transformers import (
    AutoModelForCausalLM,
    AutoTokenizer,
    BitsAndBytesConfig,
    TrainingArguments,
)
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model, prepare_model_for_kbit_training
from trl import SFTTrainer

MODEL_ID = "meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct"
DATASET_PATH = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/oss-agent/.experience/datasets/mason_backend_sft.jsonl"
OUTPUT_DIR = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/oss-agent/.adapters/mason_backend"

print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Starting training for role 'mason_backend' on base model: {MODEL_ID}")

bnb_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(
    load_in_4bit=True,
    bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
    bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.float16,
    bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
)

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_ID, trust_remote_code=True)
if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
    tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token

device_map = "auto" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    MODEL_ID,
    quantization_config=bnb_config if torch.cuda.is_available() else None,
    device_map=device_map,
    trust_remote_code=True,
)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    model = prepare_model_for_kbit_training(model)

lora_config = LoraConfig(
    r=16,
    lora_alpha=32,
    target_modules=["q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"],
    lora_dropout=0.05,
    bias="none",
    task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
)
model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)
model.print_trainable_parameters()

training_args = TrainingArguments(
    output_dir=OUTPUT_DIR,
    per_device_train_batch_size=1,
    gradient_accumulation_steps=4,
    learning_rate=2e-4,
    lr_scheduler_type="cosine",
    warmup_ratio=0.03,
    num_train_epochs=3,
    logging_steps=5,
    save_strategy="epoch",
    fp16=torch.cuda.is_available(),
    report_to="none",
)

dataset = load_dataset("json", data_files=DATASET_PATH, split="train")

def format_prompts(batch):
    formatted = []
    for conv in batch["messages"]:
        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(conv, tokenize=False)
        formatted.append(text)
    return {"text": formatted}

dataset = dataset.map(format_prompts, batched=True)

trainer = SFTTrainer(
    model=model,
    train_dataset=dataset,
    dataset_text_field="text",
    max_seq_length=2048,
    tokenizer=tokenizer,
    args=training_args,
)

print("[QLoRA Engine] Executing optimizer steps...")
trainer.train()

final_adapter_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "final_adapter")
model.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
tokenizer.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Successfully saved adapter at: {final_adapter_path}")

--- END FILE: .adapters/mason_backend/run_qlora.py ---

import torch

    AutoModelForCausalLM,
    AutoTokenizer,
    BitsAndBytesConfig,
    TrainingArguments,
)

    load_in_4bit=True,
    bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
    bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.float16,
    bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,

    model=model,
    train_dataset=dataset,
    dataset_text_field="text",
    max_seq_length=2048,
    tokenizer=tokenizer,
    args=training_args,
)

model.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
tokenizer.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)

# Auto-generated QLoRA training harness for role: reviewer
import os
import torch
from datasets import load_dataset
from transformers import (
    AutoModelForCausalLM,
    AutoTokenizer,
    BitsAndBytesConfig,
    TrainingArguments,
)
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model, prepare_model_for_kbit_training
from trl import SFTTrainer

MODEL_ID = "meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct"
DATASET_PATH = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/oss-agent/.experience/datasets/reviewer_sft.jsonl"
OUTPUT_DIR = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/oss-agent/.adapters/reviewer"

print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Starting training for role 'reviewer' on base model: {MODEL_ID}")

bnb_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(
    load_in_4bit=True,
    bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
    bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.float16,
    bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
)

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_ID, trust_remote_code=True)
if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
    tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token

device_map = "auto" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    MODEL_ID,
    quantization_config=bnb_config if torch.cuda.is_available() else None,
    device_map=device_map,
    trust_remote_code=True,
)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    model = prepare_model_for_kbit_training(model)

lora_config = LoraConfig(
    r=16,
    lora_alpha=32,
    target_modules=["q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"],
    lora_dropout=0.05,
    bias="none",
    task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
)
model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)
model.print_trainable_parameters()

training_args = TrainingArguments(
    output_dir=OUTPUT_DIR,
    per_device_train_batch_size=1,
    gradient_accumulation_steps=4,
    learning_rate=2e-4,
    lr_scheduler_type="cosine",
    warmup_ratio=0.03,
    num_train_epochs=3,
    logging_steps=5,
    save_strategy="epoch",
    fp16=torch.cuda.is_available(),
    report_to="none",
)

dataset = load_dataset("json", data_files=DATASET_PATH, split="train")

def format_prompts(batch):
    formatted = []
    for conv in batch["messages"]:
        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(conv, tokenize=False)
        formatted.append(text)
    return {"text": formatted}

dataset = dataset.map(format_prompts, batched=True)

trainer = SFTTrainer(
    model=model,
    train_dataset=dataset,
    dataset_text_field="text",
    max_seq_length=2048,
    tokenizer=tokenizer,
    args=training_args,
)

print("[QLoRA Engine] Executing optimizer steps...")
trainer.train()

final_adapter_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "final_adapter")
model.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
tokenizer.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Successfully saved adapter at: {final_adapter_path}")

--- END FILE: .adapters/reviewer/run_qlora.py ---

--- START FILE: .adapters/test_worker/run_qlora.py ---
# Auto-generated QLoRA training harness for role: test_worker
import os
import torch
from datasets import load_dataset
from transformers import (
    AutoModelForCausalLM,
    AutoTokenizer,
    BitsAndBytesConfig,
    TrainingArguments,
)
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model, prepare_model_for_kbit_training
from trl import SFTTrainer

MODEL_ID = "meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct"
DATASET_PATH = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/oss-agent/.experience/datasets/test_worker_sft.jsonl"
OUTPUT_DIR = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/oss-agent/.adapters/test_worker"

print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Starting training for role 'test_worker' on base model: {MODEL_ID}")

bnb_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(
    load_in_4bit=True,
    bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
    bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.float16,
    bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
)

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_ID, trust_remote_code=True)
if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
    tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token

device_map = "auto" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    MODEL_ID,
    quantization_config=bnb_config if torch.cuda.is_available() else None,
    device_map=device_map,
    trust_remote_code=True,
)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    model = prepare_model_for_kbit_training(model)

lora_config = LoraConfig(
    r=16,
    lora_alpha=32,
    target_modules=["q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"],
    lora_dropout=0.05,
    bias="none",
    task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
)
model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)
model.print_trainable_parameters()

training_args = TrainingArguments(
    output_dir=OUTPUT_DIR,
    per_device_train_batch_size=1,
    gradient_accumulation_steps=4,
    learning_rate=2e-4,
    lr_scheduler_type="cosine",
    warmup_ratio=0.03,
    num_train_epochs=3,
    logging_steps=5,
    save_strategy="epoch",
    fp16=torch.cuda.is_available(),
    report_to="none",
)

dataset = load_dataset("json", data_files=DATASET_PATH, split="train")

def format_prompts(batch):
    formatted = []
    for conv in batch["messages"]:
        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(conv, tokenize=False)
        formatted.append(text)
    return {"text": formatted}

dataset = dataset.map(format_prompts, batched=True)

trainer = SFTTrainer(
    model=model,
    train_dataset=dataset,
    dataset_text_field="text",
    max_seq_length=2048,
    tokenizer=tokenizer,
    args=training_args,
)

print("[QLoRA Engine] Executing optimizer steps...")
trainer.train()

final_adapter_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "final_adapter")
model.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
tokenizer.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Successfully saved adapter at: {final_adapter_path}")

--- END FILE: .adapters/test_worker/run_qlora.py ---

--- START FILE: CONTRIBUTING.md ---
# Contributing

Thanks for considering contributing to OSS Contribution Agent.

## Before you start
- Read `SECURITY.md` — this project executes code from arbitrary
  repositories, and any change touching `tools/shell.py`,
  `tools/filesystem.py`, or `tools/git.py` needs extra scrutiny.
- Never weaken the human-approval gates in `agent/orchestrator.py`
  (issue selection, implementation, push, PR creation) without
  discussion first — they are a deliberate safety design, not
  leftover scaffolding.

## Setup
See `README.md` for environment setup (Termux/Linux/Mac/Windows-WSL).

## Development workflow
1. Make your change.
2. Test with `python main.py --dry-run --repo <owner/name>` first —
   this exercises the full pipeline without pushing or opening a PR.
3. Run against a real low-stakes repo only after the dry run looks
   correct.
4. Commit with a clear message describing the change and why.

## Code style
- Keep modules single-purpose (see the existing `agent/`, `tools/`,
  `github/`, `llm/` separation) — new functionality should extend an
  existing module or get its own, not get bolted onto an unrelated one.
- Any new dangerous shell pattern should be added to
  `DANGEROUS_PATTERNS` in `tools/shell.py`, with a comment explaining
  the risk it blocks.

## Reporting bugs
Open an issue with the command you ran, the full traceback, and
(if relevant) which repo/issue triggered it. Never include your
`.env` contents, tokens, or API keys in a bug report.

--- END FILE: CONTRIBUTING.md ---

--- START FILE: README.md ---
⚠️ Security note: this agent runs a cloned repository's tests and linter directly on your device (no sandbox/container in this version). Only point it at repositories you trust. See SECURITY.md for details.
OSS Contribution Agent
An autonomous agent that discovers GitHub issues, understands a repository, drafts a fix using LLMs via OpenRouter, tests it, gets it reviewed by a separate model, and — only after your explicit approval at every stage — pushes a branch and opens a pull request.
It never auto-merges. It never pushes without asking. It never runs a shell command without showing it to you first.
Why this exists
Most AI coding agents (SWE-agent, OpenHands, Aider, Devin) assume a developer laptop or cloud sandbox. This one is built to run entirely inside Termux on an Android device — no Docker, no GPU, minimal dependencies — while keeping the same human-in-the-loop safety model a security-conscious engineer would want.
What it does
Searches GitHub for repositories (or targets one you name directly)
Finds an open issue worth attempting
Forks the repo into your account and clones the fork
Reads the README/docs/structure to build context
Asks an LLM to write an implementation plan
[Gate] asks you to approve the plan
Writes the code change
Runs the test suite; if it fails, a debugging LLM gets limited retries
Runs lint
Sends the diff to a separate reviewer LLM
[Gate] asks you to approve pushing
[Gate] asks you to approve opening the PR
Every LLM call is routed through a fallback chain (see Architecture) — if one model fails or gives unusable output, it automatically tries the next model configured for that task.


Setup

Termux (Android)
pkg update && pkg upgrade -y
pkg install python git -y
git clone <your-repo-url>
cd oss-agent
export ANDROID_API_LEVEL=24   # needed for pydantic-core to build
pip install -r requirements.txt
cp .env.example .env
nano .env   # fill in OPENROUTER_API_KEY and GITHUB_TOKEN



Mac / Linux / Windows (WSL)
git clone <your-repo-url>
cd oss-agent
pip install -r requirements.txt
cp .env.example .env
# edit .env with your keys


Getting your keys
OpenRouter API key — https://openrouter.ai/keys (also add credits at https://openrouter.ai/settings/credits — the agent will fail with a 402 error on any model call if your balance is $0)
GitHub token — use a classic token (Settings → Developer settings → Tokens (classic) → Generate new token (classic)) with the repo scope. Fine-grained tokens have been unreliable for fork creation in testing.

Usage
# Interactive: prompts for a search query
python main.py

# Target a specific repo directly, skip search
python main.py --repo psf/requests

# Search with a query
python main.py --query "language:python stars:>1000 good-first-issues:>5"

# Dry run: full pipeline, stops before any push/PR (safe for testing)
python main.py --dry-run --repo psf/requests


Configuration (.env)

OPENROUTER_API_KEY=...
GITHUB_TOKEN=...

# Single-model fallback (used if a task's chain below isn't set)
OPENROUTER_CODING_MODEL=anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet
OPENROUTER_REVIEW_MODEL=anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet
OPENROUTER_ANALYSIS_MODEL=openai/gpt-4o-mini

# Optional: per-task fallback chains (comma-separated, tried in order)
MODELS_PLANNING=anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet,openai/gpt-4o
MODELS_CODING=anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet,qwen/qwen-2.5-coder-32b-instruct
MODELS_REVIEW=openai/gpt-4o,anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet
MODELS_DEBUG=deepseek/deepseek-coder,qwen/qwen-2.5-coder-32b-instruct
MODELS_ANALYSIS=openai/gpt-4o-mini,google/gemini-flash-1.5

AUTO_APPROVE_COMMANDS=false   # keep false unless you understand the risk
MAX_DEBUG_RETRIES=3
REPO_SELECTION_POOL_SIZE=5    # random pick from top N search results
WORKSPACE_DIR=./workspace
LOGS_DIR=./logs


Architecture

main.py
  → agent/orchestrator.py       (drives the whole pipeline + approval gates)
      → github/*.py             (repo search, issue search, forks, PRs)
      → llm/router.py           (per-task model fallback chains)
          → llm/openrouter.py   (raw OpenRouter API calls)
      → agent/coding_agent.py   (plan generation, patch generation)
      → agent/debugging_agent.py (fixes failing tests)
      → agent/reviewer_agent.py (independent review of the diff)
      → tools/*.py              (safe filesystem, shell, git, test runner)
      → tools/run_logger.py     (structured JSON log per run)

Full details in PROJECT-DOCUMENTATION.md.



Observability
Every run writes a structured log to logs/run-<id>.json and appends a summary line to logs/history.jsonl, including which model in each fallback chain actually succeeded and token usage per call.
Security
See SECURITY.md. In short: secrets are never sent to any LLM and are stripped from shell subprocess environments, but test/lint execution is not sandboxed — only run this against repositories you trust.
Contributing
See CONTRIBUTING.md.
Roadmap
Containerized/sandboxed test execution
CLI improvements (click, resuming a specific issue)
Web dashboard summarizing run history
Broader OpenRouter model catalog support



--- END FILE: README.md ---

--- START FILE: SECURITY.md ---
# Security Policy

## What this agent does with your credentials
- `GITHUB_TOKEN` and `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` are read from `.env` and never
  sent to any LLM prompt, logged to disk, or committed to git.
- Before running any shell command, both keys are stripped from the
  subprocess environment (see `tools/shell.py`), so a compromised or
  malicious repo cannot read them via `env` or similar.

## Known risk: test/lint execution is not sandboxed
This agent clones real, arbitrary GitHub repositories and runs their
test suite and linter via `subprocess` (e.g. `python -m pytest`,
`flake8 .`) directly on your device — there is **no container or VM
isolation** in this version.

This means:
- A malicious repository could include a test or setup file that
  executes arbitrary code on your machine when the test suite runs.
- Only run this agent against repositories you trust, or run it on a
  disposable device/environment (a spare VM, a throwaway cloud
  instance) if you plan to explore unfamiliar repositories.
- Containerized execution (Docker or similar) would close this gap
  but is intentionally out of scope for this version to keep the
  project dependency-light and runnable on Termux/mobile. Tracked as
  a future improvement — see ROADMAP.

## What is protected today
- All file operations are confined to the cloned repo's workspace
  folder — path traversal (`../`) is blocked (`tools/filesystem.py`).
- Shell commands are shown to you before execution and require
  manual confirmation unless `AUTO_APPROVE_COMMANDS=true` is set.
- A blocklist rejects known-dangerous command patterns (`rm -rf`,
  `sudo`, `curl | sh`, `.ssh` access, etc.) — this is a speed bump,
  **not a sandbox**, and should not be treated as a complete defense.
- The agent never auto-merges a pull request, and always requires
  explicit human approval before commit, push, or PR creation.

## Reporting a vulnerability
If you find a way to bypass the command blocklist, exfiltrate
secrets, or otherwise compromise the host running this agent, please
open a private security advisory on this repository (GitHub →
Security → Report a vulnerability) rather than a public issue.

--- END FILE: SECURITY.md ---

--- START FILE: agent/builder/builder_agent.py ---
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from tools.dashboard import dashboard


class BuilderAgent:
    """
    The General Contractor & Lead Architect.
    Deconstructs user instructions and GitHub issue contexts into clear
    architectural blueprints, designs file layouts, and allocates discrete
    work packages to specialized Masons (Frontend, Backend, DB, Config).
    """

    def __init__(self, router=None):
        self.router = router

    def plan_application_architecture(self, prompt: str, target_dir: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Synthesizes user requests into concrete project layout trees and work orders.
        """
        dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "THINKING"})
        dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": "Builder Agent outlining project topology..."})

        if not self.router:
            return {
                "project_name": target_dir.name,
                "framework": "fullstack-vanilla",
                "tasks": [
                    {"mason": "BACKEND_MASON", "file": "app.py", "action": "CREATE", "objective": "Main API server"},
                    {"mason": "FRONTEND_MASON", "file": "index.html", "action": "CREATE", "objective": "Frontend UI"},
                ],
            }

        blueprint_prompt = (
            f"You are the Lead Architect / Builder Agent.\n"
            f"The user wants to construct the following application:\n'{prompt}'\n\n"
            "Return a strictly valid JSON object matching this schema:\n"
            "{\n"
            '  "project_name": "short-app-name",\n'
            '  "stack": "tech stack description",\n'
            '  "files": [\n'
            '    {"file_path": "path/relative/to/root.ext", "mason": "FRONTEND_MASON"|"BACKEND_MASON"|"CONFIG_MASON", "description": "detailed purpose"}\n'
            "  ],\n"
            '  "execution_order": ["step 1", "step 2"]\n'
            "}\n"
            "Return ONLY raw JSON, no markdown backticks."
        )

        try:
            raw_blueprint, _ = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "planning",
                [
                    {"role": "system", "content": "You are a master software architect. Always output strictly valid JSON."},
                    {"role": "user", "content": blueprint_prompt},
                ],
            )
            clean_json = raw_blueprint.strip("` \n")
            if clean_json.startswith("json\n"):
                clean_json = clean_json[5:]
            blueprint = json.loads(clean_json)
        except Exception:
            blueprint = {
                "project_name": target_dir.name,
                "stack": "Standard Full-Stack",
                "files": [
                    {"file_path": "server.py", "mason": "BACKEND_MASON", "description": "Backend API Server"},
                    {"file_path": "static/index.html", "mason": "FRONTEND_MASON", "description": "Interactive Web View"},
                ],
                "execution_order": ["Setup backend server", "Build interface view"],
            }

        for f in blueprint.get("files", []):
            f_path = f.get("file_path", "")
            if f_path:
                dashboard.emit("AST_NODE_DISCOVERED", {
                    "id": f_path,
                    "label": Path(f_path).name,
                    "mason": f.get("mason", "BACKEND_MASON")
                })

        return blueprint

--- END FILE: agent/builder/builder_agent.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/chat_interface.py ---
from config import settings
import sys
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from llm.router import LLMRouter
from tools.agent_memory import UserProfileMemory, ConversationMemory, EpisodicExecutionMemory
from tools.agent_bus import blackboard
from agent.security_agent import SecurityAgent
from agent.reviewer_agent import ReviewerAgent

class AutonomousChatBot:
    """
    Primary conversational agent.
    Runs locally, maintains user personality & context across sessions,
    and coordinates autonomous subagents via the Blackboard.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.router = LLMRouter(settings.get_task_chains())
        self.user_mem = UserProfileMemory()
        self.conv_mem = ConversationMemory()
        self.ep_mem = EpisodicExecutionMemory()
        self.security = SecurityAgent(self.router)
        self.reviewer = ReviewerAgent(self.router)

    def _build_system_prompt(self, user_query: str) -> str:
        base_prompt = (
            "You are OSS-Agent, an elite autonomous software engineer and pair programmer.\n"
            "You run completely self-contained and reason step-by-step.\n"
            "Keep your responses direct, highly technical, and minimal without conversational fluff."
        )

        # 1. Inject persistent user traits
        user_context = self.user_mem.get_system_prompt_context()

        # 2. Inject RAG context from prior sessions if relevant
        recalled_chats = self.conv_mem.search_past_topics(user_query, limit=2)
        history_context = ""
        if recalled_chats:
            history_context = "\n[Relevant Past Context]\n" + "\n".join(recalled_chats) + "\n"

        return f"{base_prompt}\n{user_context}\n{history_context}"

    def reply(self, user_input: str) -> str:
        # Save user message to persistent DB
        self.conv_mem.log_message("user", user_input)

        # Retrieve recent conversation turns
        recent_turns = self.conv_mem.get_recent_history(limit=6)

        messages = [{"role": "system", "content": self._build_system_prompt(user_input)}]
        messages.extend(recent_turns)

        # Complete via local model (or configured fallback)
        response_text, meta = self.router.complete_with_fallback("chat", messages)

        # Log assistant response to persistent DB
        self.conv_mem.log_message("assistant", response_text)
        return response_text

    def start_cli(self) -> None:
        user_name = self.user_mem.profile.get("user_name", "Developer")
        print(f"\n[OSS-Agent Online] Logged in as: {user_name}")
        print("Type 'exit' or 'quit' to stop.\n" + "-" * 40)

        while True:
            try:
                user_msg = input(f"\n{user_name} > ").strip()
                if not user_msg:
                    continue
                if user_msg.lower() in ("exit", "quit"):
                    print("Shutting down OSS-Agent session.")
                    break

                response = self.reply(user_msg)
                print(f"\nOSS-Agent > {response}")

            except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
                print("\nSession ended.")
                break

--- END FILE: agent/chat_interface.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/coding_agent.py ---
def _get_issue_field(issue, field, default=""):
    if isinstance(issue, dict):
        return issue.get(field, default)
    return getattr(issue, field, default)

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Optional
from llm.router import ModelRouter, AllModelsFailedError
from llm.validators import is_valid_json_object, is_non_empty_code_block
from llm.prompts import SYSTEM_PLANNING_PROMPT, SYSTEM_CODING_PROMPT
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem
from tools.search import CodeSearcher
from models.schemas import IssueInfo, ImplementationPlan
from tools.dashboard import dashboard

# Modular Mason imports & backward-compatible re-exports
from agent.masons import (
    BaseMason,
    FrontendMason,
    BackendMason,
    ConfigMason,
    extract_code_block as _extract_code_block,
)


def _extract_json(text: str) -> str:
    match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.DOTALL)
    return match.group(0) if match else text


class CodingAgent:
    """
    The Masons Pool Manager.
    Routes tasks to specialized Mason personas (Frontend, Backend, Config)
    and synchronizes shared code across file boundaries.
    """

    def __init__(self, router: ModelRouter, fs: SafeFileSystem):
        self.router = router
        self.fs = fs
        self.searcher = CodeSearcher(fs)

        # Modular Mason persona instances
        self.frontend_mason = FrontendMason(router, fs)
        self.backend_mason = BackendMason(router, fs)
        self.config_mason = ConfigMason(router, fs)

    def _select_mason(self, file_path: str) -> BaseMason:
        if FrontendMason.can_handle(file_path):
            return self.frontend_mason
        if ConfigMason.can_handle(file_path):
            return self.config_mason
        return self.backend_mason

    def _find_referencing_files(self, file_path: str, current_content: str, max_snippets: int = 3) -> str:
        names = re.findall(r'^\s*(?:def|class)\s+(\w+)', current_content, re.MULTILINE)
        names = list(dict.fromkeys(names))[:5]
        if not names:
            return ""

        found_snippets = []
        seen_files = set()

        for name in names:
            if len(found_snippets) >= max_snippets:
                break
            hits = self.searcher.search_symbol_or_text(name, max_results=10)
            for hit in hits:
                hit_file = hit["file"]
                if hit_file == file_path or hit_file in seen_files:
                    continue
                if hit_file.startswith(".git/") or hit_file.startswith("workspace/"):
                    continue
                seen_files.add(hit_file)
                found_snippets.append(
                    f"--- {hit_file} references '{name}' (line {hit['line']}): {hit['content']} ---"
                )
                if len(found_snippets) >= max_snippets:
                    break

        if not found_snippets:
            return ""

        return (
            "\n\nOther files reference symbols defined in this file. Keep them consistent:\n"
            + "\n".join(found_snippets)
            + "\n"
        )

    def create_plan(self, issue, context=""):
        self._current_issue = issue
        return self.generate_plan(issue, context)

    def execute_plan(self, plan):
        issue = getattr(self, "_current_issue", None)
        return self.apply_plan(issue, plan)

    def generate_plan(self, issue: IssueInfo, context: str) -> ImplementationPlan:
        title = _get_issue_field(issue, 'title', "No title")
        body = _get_issue_field(issue, 'body', "No description")
        prompt = f"Issue Title: {title}\nIssue Body: {body}\n\nCodebase Context:\n{context}"
        try:
            res, model_used = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "planning",
                [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PLANNING_PROMPT}, {"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                validate_fn=is_valid_json_object,
            )
            print(f"[CodingAgent] plan generated by {model_used}")
        except AllModelsFailedError as e:
            print(f"[CodingAgent] planning failed on every model: {e}")
            return ImplementationPlan(summary="Planning failed — no models available.", files_to_modify=[], steps=[])

        try:
            data = json.loads(_extract_json(res))
            return ImplementationPlan(**data)
        except Exception:
            return ImplementationPlan(summary=res, files_to_modify=[], steps=[])

    def generate_file_patch(
        self, issue: IssueInfo, plan: ImplementationPlan, file_path: str, already_modified: dict = None
    ) -> str:
        try:
            current_content = self.fs.read_file(file_path)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            current_content = "(file does not exist yet — create it)"

        sibling_context = ""
        if already_modified:
            sections = []
            for other_path, other_content in already_modified.items():
                truncated = other_content[:2000]
                suffix = "\n... (truncated)" if len(other_content) > 2000 else ""
                sections.append(f"--- already modified: {other_path} ---\n{truncated}{suffix}")
            sibling_context = "\n\n".join(sections)

        ref_context = self._find_referencing_files(file_path, current_content)
        task_context = (
            f"Goal: {_get_issue_field(issue, 'title')}\n{_get_issue_field(issue, 'body')}\n\n"
            f"Plan Summary: {plan.summary}\n"
            f"Steps: {plan.steps}\n"
            f"Current file content:\n{current_content}\n"
            f"{ref_context}"
        )

        mason = self._select_mason(file_path)
        return mason.craft_file(file_path, task_context, sibling_context)

    def revise_file_patch(
        self, issue: IssueInfo, plan: ImplementationPlan, file_path: str, review_feedback: str
    ) -> str:
        try:
            current_content = self.fs.read_file(file_path)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            current_content = "(file does not exist)"

        prompt = (
            f"Goal: {_get_issue_field(issue, 'title')}\n{_get_issue_field(issue, 'body')}\n"
            f"Plan Summary: {plan.summary}\n\n"
            f"Reviewer rejected with feedback:\n{review_feedback}\n\n"
            f"File: {file_path}\nContent:\n{current_content}\n\n"
            "Revise and resolve all feedback points directly. Return code inside fences."
        )

        mason = self._select_mason(file_path)
        try:
            res, model_used = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "coding",
                [{"role": "system", "content": mason.role_prompt}, {"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                validate_fn=is_non_empty_code_block,
            )
            print(f"[{mason.domain}] Revised {file_path} per reviewer notes via {model_used}")
            return _extract_code_block(res)
        except AllModelsFailedError:
            return current_content

    def apply_plan(self, issue: IssueInfo, plan: ImplementationPlan) -> None:
        already_modified: dict = {}
        for file_path in plan.files_to_modify:
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "WORKING"})
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"Mason building {Path(file_path).name}..."})
            new_content = self.generate_file_patch(issue, plan, file_path, already_modified)
            self.fs.write_file(file_path, new_content)
            already_modified[file_path] = new_content
            print(f"[CodingAgent] wrote {file_path} ({len(new_content)} chars)")

--- END FILE: agent/coding_agent.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/git_bridge.py ---
import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

class GitBridge:
    """Manages git operations for autonomous issue reproduction, branching, and PR submissions."""

    def __init__(self, workspace_path: Path):
        self.workspace_path = workspace_path

    def _run_git(self, cmd: list) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        return subprocess.run(
            ["git"] + cmd,
            cwd=str(self.workspace_path),
            text=True,
            capture_output=True
        )

    def is_git_repo(self) -> bool:
        res = self._run_git(["rev-parse", "--is-inside-work-tree"])
        return res.returncode == 0 and res.stdout.strip() == "true"

    def init_repo(self) -> bool:
        res = self._run_git(["init"])
        return res.returncode == 0

    def checkout_branch(self, branch_name: str) -> bool:
        res = self._run_git(["checkout", "-B", branch_name])
        return res.returncode == 0

    def stage_and_commit(self, message: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        self._run_git(["add", "-A"])
        res = self._run_git(["commit", "-m", message])
        return {
            "success": res.returncode == 0,
            "output": res.stdout.strip() if res.returncode == 0 else res.stderr.strip()
        }

    def generate_diff(self) -> str:
        res = self._run_git(["diff", "HEAD~1"])
        if res.returncode != 0:
            res = self._run_git(["diff"])
        return res.stdout

    def create_pull_request(self, title: str, body: str, branch: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Submits PR using the GitHub CLI (gh) if installed and authenticated."""
        gh_path = subprocess.run(["which", "gh"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
        if not gh_path:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "error": "GitHub CLI ('gh') is not installed. Install with 'pkg install gh'."
            }

        cmd = [
            "gh", "pr", "create",
            "--title", title,
            "--body", body,
            "--head", branch
        ]
        res = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=str(self.workspace_path), text=True, capture_output=True)
        if res.returncode == 0:
            return {"success": True, "url": res.stdout.strip()}
        return {"success": False, "error": res.stderr.strip()}

--- END FILE: agent/git_bridge.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/guardrails/prm_verifier.py ---
import ast
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
from agent.memory.codex_store import codex_ledger


@dataclass
class PRMStepResult:
    valid: bool
    score: float
    error_message: Optional[str] = None
    suggested_repair_prompt: Optional[str] = None


class ProcessRewardGuard:
    """
    Step-Level Process Verifier (PRM). Intercepts every code block and
    tool execution in sub-100ms before context corruption or disk writes occur.
    """

    def __init__(self, auto_record_failures_to_ledger: bool = True):
        self.auto_record = auto_record_failures_to_ledger

    def verify_python_code(self, code: str, filepath: str = "snippet.py") -> PRMStepResult:
        """Validates AST correctness, syntax boundaries, and unclosed brackets."""
        if not code or not code.strip():
            return PRMStepResult(
                valid=False,
                score=-0.5,
                error_message="Empty code buffer emitted.",
                suggested_repair_prompt="The generated code was blank. Provide complete file contents.",
            )

        # 1. AST Syntax Parse (<10ms)
        try:
            tree = ast.parse(code, filename=filepath)
        except SyntaxError as e:
            error_details = f"SyntaxError at line {e.lineno}, col {e.offset}: {e.msg}"
            if e.text:
                error_details += f"\nCode context: {e.text.strip()}"

            if self.auto_record:
                codex_ledger.record_failed_hypothesis(f"Syntax break in {filepath}: {error_details}")

            return PRMStepResult(
                valid=False,
                score=-0.8,
                error_message=error_details,
                suggested_repair_prompt=(
                    f"AST Parsing failed with {error_details}. "
                    f"Fix indentation, missing colons, or mismatched brackets before returning code."
                ),
            )

        # 2. Structural Sanity Checks
        num_nodes = len(list(ast.walk(tree)))
        if num_nodes < 3:
            return PRMStepResult(
                valid=False,
                score=-0.3,
                error_message="Code contains no functional statements or expressions.",
                suggested_repair_prompt="Ensure the implementation contains actual logic, not just comments.",
            )

        return PRMStepResult(valid=True, score=1.0)

    def verify_shell_execution(self, command: str, returncode: int, stderr: str, stdout: str) -> PRMStepResult:
        """Evaluates shell execution results immediately to prevent compounding terminal errors."""
        if returncode != 0:
            err_snip = stderr.strip() if stderr else stdout.strip()
            summary = f"Command '{command}' exited with code {returncode}. Error snippet: {err_snip[:250]}"

            if self.auto_record:
                codex_ledger.record_failed_hypothesis(summary)

            return PRMStepResult(
                valid=False,
                score=-0.6,
                error_message=summary,
                suggested_repair_prompt=f"Command failed with code {returncode}. Trace: {err_snip[:400]}",
            )

        return PRMStepResult(valid=True, score=0.5)


# Singleton instance ready for import across Masons and Orchestrator
prm_guard = ProcessRewardGuard()

--- END FILE: agent/guardrails/prm_verifier.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/hardware/runtime_adapter.py ---
import os
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

try:
    # Ingest existing environment/hardware detection module
    from llm.hardware_engine import get_hardware_profile
except ImportError:
    try:
        from agent.hardware_engine import get_hardware_profile
    except ImportError:
        def get_hardware_profile() -> Dict[str, Any]:
            is_termux = os.path.exists("/data/data/com.termux")
            return {
                "tier": "low" if is_termux else "high",
                "is_mobile": is_termux,
                "ram_gb": 4 if is_termux else 16,
                "has_gpu": False if is_termux else True,
                "has_ollama": False if is_termux else True,
            }


@dataclass
class AdaptiveRuntimeConfig:
    tier: str  # "low" | "mid" | "high"
    is_mobile: bool
    memory_strategy: str      # "sqlite_fts5_bm25" (low) vs "hybrid_dense_vector" (high)
    verification_strategy: str  # "pure_ast" (low) vs "full_typecheck_lsp" (high)
    debate_strategy: str        # "single_pass_self_critique" (low) vs "multi_agent_consensus" (high)
    inference_provider: str     # "openrouter" (low) vs "ollama" (high)


class HardwareRuntimeAdapter:
    """Translates hardware audit outputs into operational execution strategies."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.profile = get_hardware_profile()
        self.config = self._resolve_config(self.profile)

    def _resolve_config(self, profile: Dict[str, Any]) -> AdaptiveRuntimeConfig:
        tier = profile.get("tier", "low").lower()
        is_mobile = profile.get("is_mobile", False) or os.path.exists("/data/data/com.termux")

        if is_mobile or tier == "low":
            return AdaptiveRuntimeConfig(
                tier="low",
                is_mobile=True,
                memory_strategy="sqlite_fts5_bm25",
                verification_strategy="pure_ast",
                debate_strategy="single_pass_self_critique",
                inference_provider="openrouter",
            )
        elif tier == "mid":
            return AdaptiveRuntimeConfig(
                tier="mid",
                is_mobile=False,
                memory_strategy="sqlite_fts5_bm25",
                verification_strategy="pure_ast_with_linter",
                debate_strategy="multi_agent_consensus",
                inference_provider="hybrid",
            )
        else:
            return AdaptiveRuntimeConfig(
                tier="high",
                is_mobile=False,
                memory_strategy="hybrid_dense_vector",
                verification_strategy="full_typecheck_lsp",
                debate_strategy="multi_agent_consensus",
                inference_provider="ollama",
            )

    def route_debate(self, prompt: str, mason_output: str, reviewer_callback: Any, mason_callback: Any) -> str:
        """
        Executes adversarial validation adaptively:
        - Low Spec / Mobile: Single-pass self-critique (saves 50% tokens and latency).
        - High Spec: Full multi-agent turn-based debate.
        """
        if self.config.debate_strategy == "single_pass_self_critique":
            # Mobile path: Have the Mason critique and patch its own draft in one follow-up
            critique_prompt = (
                f"Perform a strict review of your code for syntax, imports, and security boundaries:\n"
                f"{mason_output}\n"
                f"Emit only the final corrected code wrapped in standard markdown fences."
            )
            return mason_callback(critique_prompt)
        else:
            # High-spec path: Mason vs Reviewer dynamic consensus
            critique = reviewer_callback(mason_output)
            repair_prompt = f"Address this reviewer critique and fix the patch:\n{critique}\nOriginal:\n{mason_output}"
            return mason_callback(repair_prompt)


# Global adapter instance
runtime_adapter = HardwareRuntimeAdapter()

--- END FILE: agent/hardware/runtime_adapter.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/harness.py ---
import asyncio
from typing import Any
from agent.memory.codex_store import PersistentCodexLedger, codex_ledger


async def agent_orchestration_step(
    agent: Any,
    user_query: str,
    ledger: PersistentCodexLedger = codex_ledger,
    run_tool_fn: Any = None,
    token_reset_threshold: int = 40_000,
):
    """
    Autonomous ReAct execution harness governed by persistent Codex state
    and active context-window page turns.
    """
    # Turn 0: Initialize Task & Core Milestones
    ledger.update_objective(user_query)
    ledger.add_pending_milestone("Audit codebase for target bug")
    ledger.add_pending_milestone("Implement patch in isolation")
    ledger.add_pending_milestone("Run pytest suite to verify fix")

    # Ingest ledger state into agent instructions
    agent.system_prompt = f"System Rules...\n\n{ledger.render_rehydration_prompt()}"

    active_tokens = 0

    while getattr(ledger.state, "status", "IN_PROGRESS") == "IN_PROGRESS":
        # 1. Predict next step via cognitive model
        action = await agent.predict_next_step()

        # 2. Intercept and execute tools
        if getattr(action, "type", None) == "TOOL_EXECUTE":
            tool_name = getattr(action, "name", "")
            tool_args = getattr(action, "args", {})

            tool_output = ""
            if run_tool_fn:
                tool_output = await run_tool_fn(tool_name, tool_args)

            # Archive execution payload and result to disk
            ledger.archive_turn(tool_name, str(tool_args), str(tool_output))

            # Track affected files
            if tool_name in ["write_file", "apply_patch"] and "filepath" in tool_args:
                ledger.mark_file_modified(tool_args["filepath"])

        # 3. Check for context rollover boundary
        consumed = getattr(action, "consumed_tokens", 0)
        active_tokens += consumed

        if active_tokens > token_reset_threshold:
            print("[Harness] Approaching context boundary. Executing Page-Turn...")

            rehydration_context = ledger.turn_the_page()

            if hasattr(agent, "reset_context_window"):
                agent.reset_context_window()

            agent.system_prompt = rehydration_context
            active_tokens = 0
            break

--- END FILE: agent/harness.py ---

"""

--- START FILE: agent/masons/base_mason.py ---
import ast
import re
from llm.router import ModelRouter, AllModelsFailedError
from llm.validators import is_non_empty_code_block
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem
from tools.dashboard import dashboard
from tools.repo_map import RepoMapGenerator
from agent.training import experience_buffer


def extract_code_block(text: str) -> str:
    """Extract code from markdown code fences or strip extra whitespace."""
    match = re.search(r"```(?:\w+)?\n(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)
    return match.group(1) if match else text.strip("` \n")


def is_valid_python(code: str) -> bool:
    """Checks if generated Python code compiles cleanly without syntax errors."""
    try:
        ast.parse(code)
        return True
    except SyntaxError:
        return False


class BaseMason:
    """Base technical specialist worker for code generation."""

    def __init__(self, domain: str, role_prompt: str, router: ModelRouter, fs: SafeFileSystem):
        self.domain = domain
        self.role_prompt = role_prompt
        self.router = router
        self.fs = fs

    def craft_file(self, file_path: str, task_context: str, sibling_context: str) -> str:
        # Dynamically inject codebase symbol map to prevent import hallucinations
        try:
            workspace_root = getattr(self.fs, "workspace_root", None)
            repo_skeleton = RepoMapGenerator(workspace_root=workspace_root).generate_map(max_chars=2500)
        except Exception:
            repo_skeleton = "[Codebase skeleton unavailable]"

        prompt = (
            f"{self.role_prompt}\n\n"
            f"--- CODEBASE SKELETON (AST REPO-MAP) ---\n{repo_skeleton}\n\n"
            f"--- TASK CONTEXT ---\n{task_context}\n\n"
            f"--- SIBLING CODE & CONTRACTS ---\n{sibling_context}\n\n"
            f"File to create/modify: {file_path}\n"
            "Return complete, functional, high-quality production code enclosed in markdown code fences."
        )
        try:
            res, model_used = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "coding",
                [{"role": "system", "content": self.role_prompt}, {"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                validate_fn=is_non_empty_code_block,
            )
            print(f"[{self.domain}] {file_path} generated by {model_used}")
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"{self.domain} generated {file_path} via [{model_used}]"})
            extracted = extract_code_block(res)

            # Assign reward based on AST syntax check for Python files
            reward = 1.0
            is_py = file_path.endswith(".py")
            if is_py and not is_valid_python(extracted):
                reward = -0.5
                print(f"[{self.domain}] Syntax validation failed for {file_path}. Penalizing trajectory.")

            experience_buffer.record_trajectory(
                agent_role=f"mason_{self.domain.lower()}",
                task_type="code_generation",
                input_context=prompt,
                action_output=extracted,
                outcome_reward=reward,
                metadata={"file_path": file_path, "model_used": model_used, "domain": self.domain, "syntax_valid": reward > 0},
            )

            return extracted

        except AllModelsFailedError as e:
            print(f"[{self.domain}] Generation failed for {file_path}: {e}")

            experience_buffer.record_trajectory(
                agent_role=f"mason_{self.domain.lower()}",
                task_type="code_generation",
                input_context=prompt,
                action_output=str(e),
                outcome_reward=-1.0,
                metadata={"file_path": file_path, "status": "failed", "domain": self.domain},
            )

            return ""

--- END FILE: agent/masons/base_mason.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/masons/config_mason.py ---
from pathlib import Path
from llm.router import ModelRouter
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem
from agent.masons.base_mason import BaseMason

CONFIG_ROLE_PROMPT = (
    "You are a DevOps & Tooling Specialist. Build valid configuration manifests "
    "(package.json, requirements.txt, configs, Dockerfiles). Ensure dependency pins and environments are clean."
)

class ConfigMason(BaseMason):
    """Specialist responsible for dependencies, deployment configurations, and project manifests."""

    def __init__(self, router: ModelRouter, fs: SafeFileSystem):
        super().__init__(
            domain="Config Mason",
            role_prompt=CONFIG_ROLE_PROMPT,
            router=router,
            fs=fs,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def can_handle(file_path: str) -> bool:
        name = Path(file_path).name.lower()
        return name in [
            "package.json",
            "requirements.txt",
            "dockerfile",
            ".env.example",
            "cargo.toml",
            "setup.py",
            "pyproject.toml",
        ]

--- START FILE: agent/masons/devops_mason.py ---
from agent.masons.base_mason import BaseMason
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem
from llm.router import ModelRouter

DEVOPS_ROLE_PROMPT = """You are the DevOps Mason for oss-agent.
Your domain is CI/CD infrastructure, Docker packaging, shell automation, and deployment configurations.
Rules:
1. Write minimal, multi-stage, rootless Dockerfiles and reproducible CI scripts.
2. Handle environment variable isolation, caching, and healthchecks cleanly.
3. Return complete, production-ready code enclosed in markdown code fences.
"""

class DevOpsMason(BaseMason):
    def __init__(self, router: ModelRouter, fs: SafeFileSystem):
        super().__init__(
            domain="DevOps",
            role_prompt=DEVOPS_ROLE_PROMPT,
            router=router,
            fs=fs,
        )

--- START FILE: agent/memory/codex_store.py ---
from agent.memory.turn_retriever import turn_retriever
import json
import os
import time
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Union


@dataclass
class TaskLedgerState:
    task_slug: str
    objective: str
    status: str = "IN_PROGRESS"  # IN_PROGRESS | BLOCKED | COMPLETED
    invariants: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    failed_hypotheses: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    verified_assumptions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    unconfirmed_hypotheses: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    modified_files: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    completed_milestones: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pending_milestones: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    window_id: int = 1
    last_updated: float = field(default_factory=time.time)


class PersistentCodexLedger:
    """Production-grade working memory engine with dual JSON/Markdown persistence."""

    def __init__(self, workspace_root: Optional[Union[str, Path]] = None, task_slug: str = "main"):
        self.workspace_root = Path(workspace_root) if workspace_root else Path.cwd()
        self.task_slug = task_slug
        self.codex_dir = self.workspace_root / ".codex"
        self.ledger_dir = self.codex_dir / "ledger"
        self.history_dir = self.codex_dir / "history"

        self.ledger_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.history_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        self.ledger_md_path = self.ledger_dir / f"{self.task_slug}-MEMORY.md"
        self.state_json_path = self.ledger_dir / f"{self.task_slug}-state.json"

        self.state = self._load_or_initialize()

    def _load_or_initialize(self) -> TaskLedgerState:
        """Loads state from disk if present; otherwise initializes a clean state."""
        if self.state_json_path.exists():
            try:
                with open(self.state_json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                valid_keys = {field_name for field_name in TaskLedgerState.__dataclass_fields__}
                filtered_data = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in valid_keys}
                return TaskLedgerState(**filtered_data)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return TaskLedgerState(task_slug=self.task_slug, objective="")

    def _atomic_write(self, target_path: Path, content: str) -> None:
        """Writes to a temporary file and atomically replaces destination to prevent corruption."""
        temp_file = target_path.with_name(f"{target_path.name}.tmp_{os.getpid()}_{int(time.time()*1000)}")
        with open(temp_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(content)
        temp_file.replace(target_path)

    def update_objective(self, objective: str):
        self.state.objective = objective
        self.sync_to_disk()

    def set_status(self, status: str):
        self.state.status = status
        self.sync_to_disk()

    def record_decision(self, invariant: str):
        """Records an architectural invariant or rule (e.g. 'Use Python standard library only')."""
        if invariant not in self.state.invariants:
            self.state.invariants.append(invariant)
            self.sync_to_disk()

    def record_failed_hypothesis(self, failure: str):
        """Records a failed patch or broken approach to prevent repetitive loops."""
        if failure not in self.state.failed_hypotheses:
            self.state.failed_hypotheses.append(failure)
            self.sync_to_disk()

    def record_verified_assumption(self, assumption: str):
        """Records a proven fact (e.g. 'FastAPI 0.110 running on port 8000')."""
        if assumption not in self.state.verified_assumptions:
            self.state.verified_assumptions.append(assumption)
            self.sync_to_disk()

    def mark_file_modified(self, filepath: Union[str, Path]):
        try:
            rel_path = os.path.relpath(filepath, self.workspace_root)
        except ValueError:
            rel_path = str(filepath)
        if rel_path not in self.state.modified_files:
            self.state.modified_files.append(rel_path)
            self.sync_to_disk()

    def complete_milestone(self, milestone: str):
        if milestone in self.state.pending_milestones:
            self.state.pending_milestones.remove(milestone)
        if milestone not in self.state.completed_milestones:
            self.state.completed_milestones.append(milestone)
        self.sync_to_disk()

    def add_pending_milestone(self, milestone: str):
        if milestone not in self.state.pending_milestones and milestone not in self.state.completed_milestones:
            self.state.pending_milestones.append(milestone)
            self.sync_to_disk()

    def archive_turn(self, tool_name: str, payload: str, result: str):
        """Appends tool and compiler outputs into an indexed history log with artifact spilling."""
        log_file = self.history_dir / f"window_{self.state.window_id}.jsonl"
        entry: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "timestamp": time.time(),
            "window_id": self.state.window_id,
            "tool": tool_name,
            "payload": payload,
            "result_summary": result[:1000] if len(result) > 1000 else result,
            "full_result_path": None,
        }

        # Spill large stdout/stderr payloads to dedicated artifact logs
        if len(result) > 2000:
            artifact_name = f"artifact_{int(time.time()*1000)}.log"
            artifact_path = self.history_dir / artifact_name
            with open(artifact_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as af:
                af.write(result)
            entry["full_result_path"] = str(artifact_path)
            entry["result_summary"] = result[:300] + f"... [Truncated. Full output in {artifact_name}]"

        with open(log_file, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(entry) + "\n")
        try:
            turn_retriever.index_turn(tool_name, payload, entry['result_summary'], self.state.window_id, entry.get('full_result_path'))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def turn_the_page(self) -> str:
        """Increments context window ID, flushes history, and emits Turn 0 rehydration prompt."""
        self.state.window_id += 1
        self.sync_to_disk()
        return self.render_rehydration_prompt()

    def sync_to_disk(self):
        """Atomically persists structured JSON and human-readable Markdown representations."""
        self.state.last_updated = time.time()
        self._atomic_write(self.state_json_path, json.dumps(asdict(self.state), indent=2))
        self._atomic_write(self.ledger_md_path, self._render_markdown())

    def _render_markdown(self) -> str:
        """Generates canonical Markdown ingested by LLM prompts."""
        return f"""# CODEX LEDGER: {self.task_slug.upper()}
> **Status**: {self.state.status} | **Active Window**: {self.state.window_id}

### Objective
{self.state.objective or "No objective set."}

### Architectural Invariants & Rules
{self._format_list(self.state.invariants, "- [INVARIANT]")}

### Discarded Approaches & Failed Fixes
{self._format_list(self.state.failed_hypotheses, "- [DO NOT REPEAT]")}

### Verification State
* **Confirmed Facts:**
{self._format_list(self.state.verified_assumptions, "  -")}
* **Unconfirmed Hypotheses:**
{self._format_list(self.state.unconfirmed_hypotheses, "  - [UNCONFIRMED]")}

### Milestones & Progress
{self._format_list(self.state.completed_milestones, "- [x]")}
{self._format_list(self.state.pending_milestones, "- [ ]")}

### Modified Artifacts
{self._format_list(self.state.modified_files, "- `") + ("`" if self.state.modified_files else "")}
"""

    def render_context_block(self, max_chars: int = 2500) -> str:
        """Renders an immutable, anti-amnesia context block for agent prompts."""
        md = self._render_markdown()
        return md if len(md) <= max_chars else md[:max_chars] + "\n...[Codex Ledger Truncated]"

    def render_rehydration_prompt(self) -> str:
        """Returns context string injected at the start of a rolled-over session."""
        return f"""[SYSTEM MEMORY RESTORATION]
You are resuming task: {self.task_slug}.
A context rollover occurred. Do not re-explore files already marked verified.

{self._render_markdown()}
Continue execution from the next pending milestone."""

    @staticmethod
    def _format_list(items: List[str], prefix: str) -> str:
        if not items:
            return "  *(None)*"
        return "\n".join(f"{prefix} {item}" for item in items)


# Default workspace singleton
codex_ledger = PersistentCodexLedger()

--- END FILE: agent/memory/codex_store.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/memory/conflict_resolver.py ---
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional

try:
    from agent.hardware.runtime_adapter import detect_hardware_tier, RuntimeTier
except ImportError:
    class RuntimeTier:
        LOW_SPEC_MOBILE = "low_spec_mobile"
        MID_SPEC = "mid_spec"
        HIGH_SPEC = "high_spec"

    def detect_hardware_tier():
        is_android = "ANDROID_ROOT" in os.environ or "TERMUX_VERSION" in os.environ
        return RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE if is_android else RuntimeTier.MID_SPEC


# Role hierarchy matrix for deterministic conflict resolution
ROLE_HIERARCHY: Dict[str, int] = {
    "queen": 100,
    "reviewer": 80,
    "tester": 60,
    "builder": 40,
    "researcher": 20,
    "worker": 10
}


@dataclass
class ConflictResolutionReport:
    accepted_invariants: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    rejected_invariants: List[Tuple[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list)  # (invariant, reason)
    resolved_milestones: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    conflicted_milestones: List[Tuple[str, str]] = field(default_factory=list) # (milestone, reason)


class AdaptiveConflictResolver:
    def __init__(self):
        self.tier = detect_hardware_tier()
        self.negation_patterns = [
            (r"\bnever\b", r"\balways\b"),
            (r"\bdo not\b", r"\bmust\b"),
            (r"\bprohibit\b", r"\ballow\b"),
            (r"\bdisallow\b", r"\benable\b"),
            (r"\bdeprecated\b", r"\brequired\b")
        ]

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Milestone Resolution: State Monotonicity & Role Precedence
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    def resolve_milestone_transition(
        self,
        worker_role: str,
        milestone: str,
        action: str,  # "complete" | "add"
        completed_in_root: List[str],
        pending_in_root: List[str],
        root_milestone_authors: Dict[str, str]
    ) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """
        Prevents race conditions where a lower-tier agent revives or alters
        a milestone already closed or owned by a higher-tier agent.
        """
        # Rule A: Monotonic Completion (Once completed, a milestone cannot be reopened without higher rank)
        if milestone in completed_in_root and action == "add":
            prior_role = root_milestone_authors.get(milestone, "worker")
            if ROLE_HIERARCHY.get(worker_role, 0) <= ROLE_HIERARCHY.get(prior_role, 0):
                return False, f"Milestone '{milestone}' was marked completed by higher/equal role '{prior_role}'."

        # Rule B: Duplicate Complete (Idempotent success)
        if milestone in completed_in_root and action == "complete":
            return True, "Already completed in root (Idempotent merge)."

        # Rule C: Completing a milestone that was never pending
        if milestone not in pending_in_root and action == "complete":
            if ROLE_HIERARCHY.get(worker_role, 0) < ROLE_HIERARCHY.get("tester", 60):
                return False, f"Role '{worker_role}' cannot complete unregistered milestone '{milestone}'."

        return True, "Approved"

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Invariant Resolution: Polarity Collision & Overlap Analysis
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    def detect_invariant_collision(self, candidate: str, existing: str) -> bool:
        """
        Deterministic opposition heuristic.
        Runs with zero RAM overhead on mobile and catches direct philosophical splits.
        """
        cand_lower = candidate.lower()
        exist_lower = existing.lower()

        # Tokenize content words (>3 chars)
        cand_words = set(re.findall(r"\b\w{4,}\b", cand_lower))
        exist_words = set(re.findall(r"\b\w{4,}\b", exist_lower))
        shared = cand_words.intersection(exist_words)

        # Require topical overlap (at least 2 substantial subject words match)
        if len(shared) < 2:
            return False

        # Polarity flip check
        for neg, pos in self.negation_patterns:
            c_has_neg = bool(re.search(neg, cand_lower))
            c_has_pos = bool(re.search(pos, cand_lower))
            e_has_neg = bool(re.search(neg, exist_lower))
            e_has_pos = bool(re.search(pos, exist_lower))

            if (c_has_neg and e_has_pos) or (c_has_pos and e_has_neg):
                return True

        return False

    def arbitrate_invariants(
        self,
        worker_role: str,
        candidates: List[str],
        existing_invariants: List[str],
        invariant_authors: Dict[str, str]
    ) -> Tuple[List[str], List[Tuple[str, str]]]:
        """
        Arbitrates invariants.
        If a collision is detected:
        - Higher rank wins outright.
        - Lower/Equal rank is rejected or held in worker scratchpad.
        """
        accepted = []
        rejected = []

        for cand in candidates:
            collided = False
            for exist in existing_invariants:
                if self.detect_invariant_collision(cand, exist):
                    author_role = invariant_authors.get(exist, "queen")
                    worker_weight = ROLE_HIERARCHY.get(worker_role, 0)
                    existing_weight = ROLE_HIERARCHY.get(author_role, 0)

                    if worker_weight > existing_weight:
                        # Replace prior invariant (Authoritative supersede)
                        accepted.append(cand)
                    else:
                        rejected.append((cand, f"Contradicts invariant established by '{author_role}': '{exist}'"))
                    collided = True
                    break

            if not collided and cand not in existing_invariants:
                accepted.append(cand)

        return accepted, rejected

--- END FILE: agent/memory/conflict_resolver.py ---
