import os
import sys
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any

try:
    from agent.hardware.runtime_adapter import detect_hardware_tier, RuntimeTier
except ImportError:
    class RuntimeTier:
        LOW_SPEC_MOBILE = "low_spec_mobile"
        MID_SPEC = "mid_spec"
        HIGH_SPEC = "high_spec"

    def detect_hardware_tier():
        is_android = "ANDROID_ROOT" in os.environ or "TERMUX_VERSION" in os.environ
        return RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE if is_android else RuntimeTier.MID_SPEC

from agent.memory.conflict_resolver import AdaptiveConflictResolver


class AdaptiveFileLock:
    def __init__(self, lock_path: Path, timeout: float = 10.0, poll_interval: float = 0.05):
        self.lock_path = lock_path
        self.dir_lock_path = lock_path.with_suffix(".dirlock")
        self.timeout = timeout
        self.poll_interval = poll_interval
        self._fd = None
        self._use_kernel_lock = True
        
        if "ANDROID_ROOT" in os.environ or "TERMUX_VERSION" in os.environ or sys.platform == "win32":
            self._use_kernel_lock = False

    def __enter__(self):
        start = time.time()
        while True:
            if self._use_kernel_lock:
                try:
                    import fcntl
                    self._fd = open(self.lock_path, "w")
                    fcntl.flock(self._fd.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
                    return self
                except (IOError, OSError, ImportError):
                    self._use_kernel_lock = False
                    if self._fd:
                        try:
                            self._fd.close()
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        self._fd = None

            try:
                os.mkdir(self.dir_lock_path)
                return self
            except FileExistsError:
                pass

            if time.time() - start > self.timeout:
                raise TimeoutError(f"Deadlock timeout for {self.lock_path}")
            time.sleep(self.poll_interval)

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
        if self._use_kernel_lock and self._fd:
            try:
                import fcntl
                fcntl.flock(self._fd.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
                self._fd.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
        else:
            try:
                os.rmdir(self.dir_lock_path)
            except OSError:
                pass


@dataclass
class ScratchpadMemory:
    agent_id: str
    active_role: str
    hypotheses: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    local_notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    candidate_invariants: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    resolved_milestones: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pending_milestones: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    files_touched: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class SharedRootLedgerState:
    task_slug: str
    global_objective: str
    invariants: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    invariant_authors: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)       # invariant -> role
    completed_milestones: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pending_milestones: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    milestone_authors: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)       # milestone -> role
    modified_artifacts: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    verified_facts: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    active_workers: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
    revision: int = 0
    last_synced: float = field(default_factory=time.time)


class SharedRootLedger:
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str, task_slug: str):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.task_slug = task_slug
        self.ledger_dir = self.workspace / ".codex" / "ledger"
        self.ledger_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        self.state_file = self.ledger_dir / f"{task_slug}-root.json"
        self.md_file = self.ledger_dir / f"{task_slug}-ROOT.md"
        self.lock_file = self.ledger_dir / f"{task_slug}.lock"
        self.resolver = AdaptiveConflictResolver()
        
        self._init_or_load()

    def _init_or_load(self):
        with AdaptiveFileLock(self.lock_file):
            if not self.state_file.exists():
                initial = SharedRootLedgerState(
                    task_slug=self.task_slug,
                    global_objective="Define global task parameters"
                )
                self._save_state_unlocked(initial)

    def _load_state_unlocked(self) -> SharedRootLedgerState:
        with open(self.state_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return SharedRootLedgerState(**json.load(f))

    def _save_state_unlocked(self, state: SharedRootLedgerState):
        state.revision += 1
        state.last_synced = time.time()
        
        with open(self.state_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(asdict(state), f, indent=2)

        md = f"""# ROOT LEDGER: {state.task_slug.upper()} (Rev: {state.revision})
**Objective**: {state.global_objective}

### Universal Invariants
"""
        for inv in state.invariants:
            author = state.invariant_authors.get(inv, "system")
            md += f"- [INVARIANT] {inv} *(author: {author})*\n"

        md += f"\n### Global Milestones\n"
        for cm in state.completed_milestones:
            md += f"- [x] {cm}\n"
        for pm in state.pending_milestones:
            md += f"- [ ] {pm}\n"

        md += f"\n### Modified Artifacts\n"
        for fpath in state.modified_artifacts:
            md += f"- `{fpath}`\n"

        with open(self.md_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(md)

    def register_worker(self, agent_id: str, role: str):
        with AdaptiveFileLock(self.lock_file):
            st = self._load_state_unlocked()
            st.active_workers[agent_id] = role
            self._save_state_unlocked(st)

    def commit_worker_delta(self, role: str, delta: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Hardware-adaptive conflict resolution merge.
        Returns a dict of accepted deltas and rejection reasons.
        """
        with AdaptiveFileLock(self.lock_file):
            st = self._load_state_unlocked()
            merge_report = {"accepted_invariants": [], "rejected_invariants": [], "rejected_milestones": []}

            # 1. Arbitrate Invariants
            candidate_invs = delta.get("candidate_invariants", [])
            accepted_invs, rejected_invs = self.resolver.arbitrate_invariants(
                worker_role=role,
                candidates=candidate_invs,
                existing_invariants=st.invariants,
                invariant_authors=st.invariant_authors
            )
            for inv in accepted_invs:
                if inv not in st.invariants:
                    st.invariants.append(inv)
                st.invariant_authors[inv] = role
                merge_report["accepted_invariants"].append(inv)
            merge_report["rejected_invariants"] = rejected_invs

            # 2. Arbitrate Milestones
            for done in delta.get("resolved_milestones", []):
                ok, reason = self.resolver.resolve_milestone_transition(
                    worker_role=role,
                    milestone=done,
                    action="complete",
                    completed_in_root=st.completed_milestones,
                    pending_in_root=st.pending_milestones,
                    root_milestone_authors=st.milestone_authors
                )
                if ok:
                    if done in st.pending_milestones:
                        st.pending_milestones.remove(done)
                    if done not in st.completed_milestones:
                        st.completed_milestones.append(done)
                    st.milestone_authors[done] = role
                else:
                    merge_report["rejected_milestones"].append((done, reason))

            for pending in delta.get("pending_milestones", []):
                ok, reason = self.resolver.resolve_milestone_transition(
                    worker_role=role,
                    milestone=pending,
                    action="add",
                    completed_in_root=st.completed_milestones,
                    pending_in_root=st.pending_milestones,
                    root_milestone_authors=st.milestone_authors
                )
                if ok:
                    if pending not in st.pending_milestones and pending not in st.completed_milestones:
                        st.pending_milestones.append(pending)
                        st.milestone_authors[pending] = role
                else:
                    merge_report["rejected_milestones"].append((pending, reason))

            # 3. Merge Artifacts & Facts
            for path in delta.get("files_touched", []):
                if path not in st.modified_artifacts:
                    st.modified_artifacts.append(path)

            for fact in delta.get("verified_facts", []):
                if fact not in st.verified_facts:
                    st.verified_facts.append(fact)

            self._save_state_unlocked(st)
            return merge_report

    def get_read_only_view(self) -> SharedRootLedgerState:
        with AdaptiveFileLock(self.lock_file):
            return self._load_state_unlocked()


class WorkerAgentScratchpad:
    def __init__(self, root_ledger: SharedRootLedger, agent_id: str, role: str):
        self.root = root_ledger
        self.agent_id = agent_id
        self.role = role
        self.tier = detect_hardware_tier()
        
        self.scratchpad_dir = self.root.ledger_dir / "scratchpads"
        self.scratchpad_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.local_file = self.scratchpad_dir / f"{self.agent_id}.json"
        
        self.data = ScratchpadMemory(agent_id=self.agent_id, active_role=self.role)
        self.root.register_worker(self.agent_id, self.role)
        self._sync_disk()

    def record_hypothesis(self, text: str):
        if text not in self.data.hypotheses:
            self.data.hypotheses.append(text)
            self._sync_disk()

    def touch_file(self, path: str):
        if path not in self.data.files_touched:
            self.data.files_touched.append(path)
            self._sync_disk()

    def propose_invariant(self, invariant: str):
        if invariant not in self.data.candidate_invariants:
            self.data.candidate_invariants.append(invariant)
            self._sync_disk()

    def complete_subtask(self, milestone: str):
        if milestone not in self.data.resolved_milestones:
            self.data.resolved_milestones.append(milestone)
            self._sync_disk()

    def sync_to_root(self, verified_facts: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        delta = {
            "candidate_invariants": self.data.candidate_invariants,
            "resolved_milestones": self.data.resolved_milestones,
            "pending_milestones": self.data.pending_milestones,
            "files_touched": self.data.files_touched,
            "verified_facts": verified_facts or []
        }
        
        report = self.root.commit_worker_delta(role=self.role, delta=delta)
        self.data.candidate_invariants.clear()
        self.data.resolved_milestones.clear()
        self.data.files_touched.clear()
        self._sync_disk()
        return report

    def build_context_prompt(self) -> str:
        root_state = self.root.get_read_only_view()
        if self.tier == RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE:
            return (
                f"[LEDGER: {self.role.upper()} ({self.agent_id})]\n"
                f"ROOT INVARIANTS: {'; '.join(root_state.invariants) or 'None'}\n"
                f"FACTS: {'; '.join(root_state.verified_facts) or 'None'}\n"
                f"PENDING MILESTONES: {'; '.join(root_state.pending_milestones) or 'None'}\n"
            )
        else:
            invariants_fmt = "\n".join(f"- {i}" for i in root_state.invariants) or "  *(None)*"
            facts_fmt = "\n".join(f"- {f}" for f in root_state.verified_facts) or "  *(None)*"
            return f"""# SYSTEM CONTEXT [Role: {self.role.upper()} | Agent: {self.agent_id}]
## Shared Universal Invariants
{invariants_fmt}

## Confirmed Facts
{facts_fmt}

## Private Scratchpad
- Hypotheses: {self.data.hypotheses}
- Staged Invariants: {self.data.candidate_invariants}
"""

    def _sync_disk(self):
        with open(self.local_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(asdict(self.data), f, indent=2 if self.tier != RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE else None)

--- END FILE: agent/memory/federated_ledger.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/memory/turn_retriever.py ---
import os
import sys
import json
import re
import math
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional

try:
    from agent.hardware.runtime_adapter import detect_hardware_tier, RuntimeTier
except ImportError:
    class RuntimeTier:
        LOW_SPEC_MOBILE = "low_spec_mobile"
        MID_SPEC = "mid_spec"
        HIGH_SPEC = "high_spec"

    def detect_hardware_tier():
        is_android = "ANDROID_ROOT" in os.environ or "TERMUX_VERSION" in os.environ
        return RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE if is_android else RuntimeTier.MID_SPEC


@dataclass
class SearchResult:
    source_type: str      # "ROOT_LEDGER" | "SCRATCHPAD" | "ARCHIVED_TURN"
    source_id: str        # task_slug, agent_id, or turn filename
    section: str          # e.g., "invariants", "hypotheses", "tool_result"
    content: str
    relevance_score: float


class FederatedTurnRetriever:
    """
    Hardware-adaptive retriever capable of scanning across:
    1. Shared Root Ledgers (.codex/ledger/*-root.json)
    2. Private Worker Scratchpads (.codex/ledger/scratchpads/*.json)
    3. Archived Page-Turn Histories (.codex/history/*.jsonl)
    """
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.ledger_dir = self.workspace / ".codex" / "ledger"
        self.history_dir = self.workspace / ".codex" / "history"
        self.scratchpad_dir = self.ledger_dir / "scratchpads"
        self.tier = detect_hardware_tier()

    def search(
        self,
        query: str,
        task_slug: Optional[str] = None,
        include_scratchpads: bool = True,
        include_history: bool = True,
        top_k: int = 5
    ) -> List[SearchResult]:
        """
        Executes a federated search across all agent ledger layers.
        """
        query_tokens = self._tokenize(query)
        if not query_tokens:
            return []

        candidates: List[SearchResult] = []

        # 1. Search Shared Root Ledgers
        candidates.extend(self._search_root_ledgers(query_tokens, task_slug))

        # 2. Search Private Worker Scratchpads
        if include_scratchpads:
            candidates.extend(self._search_scratchpads(query_tokens))

        # 3. Search Historical Turned Pages / Tool Execution Logs
        if include_history:
            candidates.extend(self._search_history_logs(query_tokens))

        # Sort results by relevance descending
        candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x.relevance_score, reverse=True)
        return candidates[:top_k]

    def _search_root_ledgers(self, query_tokens: List[str], task_slug: Optional[str]) -> List[SearchResult]:
        results = []
        if not self.ledger_dir.exists():
            return results

        pattern = f"{task_slug}-root.json" if task_slug else "*-root.json"
        for state_file in self.ledger_dir.glob(pattern):
            try:
                with open(state_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                continue

            slug = data.get("task_slug", state_file.stem)

            # Match Invariants
            for inv in data.get("invariants", []):
                score = self._compute_score(query_tokens, inv)
                if score > 0:
                    author = data.get("invariant_authors", {}).get(inv, "system")
                    results.append(SearchResult(
                        source_type="ROOT_LEDGER",
                        source_id=slug,
                        section=f"invariant (author: {author})",
                        content=inv,
                        relevance_score=score * 1.3  # Invariants receive priority weighting
                    ))

            # Match Confirmed Facts
            for fact in data.get("verified_facts", []):
                score = self._compute_score(query_tokens, fact)
                if score > 0:
                    results.append(SearchResult(
                        source_type="ROOT_LEDGER",
                        source_id=slug,
                        section="verified_fact",
                        content=fact,
                        relevance_score=score * 1.2
                    ))

            # Match Milestones
            for ms in data.get("completed_milestones", []) + data.get("pending_milestones", []):
                score = self._compute_score(query_tokens, ms)
                if score > 0:
                    results.append(SearchResult(
                        source_type="ROOT_LEDGER",
                        source_id=slug,
                        section="milestone",
                        content=ms,
                        relevance_score=score
                    ))
        return results

    def _search_scratchpads(self, query_tokens: List[str]) -> List[SearchResult]:
        results = []
        if not self.scratchpad_dir.exists():
            return results

        for sp_file in self.scratchpad_dir.glob("*.json"):
            try:
                with open(sp_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                continue

            agent_id = data.get("agent_id", sp_file.stem)
            role = data.get("active_role", "unknown")

            # Search Hypotheses
            for h in data.get("hypotheses", []):
                score = self._compute_score(query_tokens, h)
                if score > 0:
                    results.append(SearchResult(
                        source_type="SCRATCHPAD",
                        source_id=f"{agent_id} ({role})",
                        section="hypothesis",
                        content=h,
                        relevance_score=score * 0.9
                    ))

            # Search Candidate/Staged Invariants
            for ci in data.get("candidate_invariants", []):
                score = self._compute_score(query_tokens, ci)
                if score > 0:
                    results.append(SearchResult(
                        source_type="SCRATCHPAD",
                        source_id=f"{agent_id} ({role})",
                        section="staged_invariant",
                        content=ci,
                        relevance_score=score * 1.0
                    ))

            # Search Local Notes
            for note in data.get("local_notes", []):
                score = self._compute_score(query_tokens, note)
                if score > 0:
                    results.append(SearchResult(
                        source_type="SCRATCHPAD",
                        source_id=f"{agent_id} ({role})",
                        section="local_note",
                        content=note,
                        relevance_score=score * 0.8
                    ))
        return results

    def _search_history_logs(self, query_tokens: List[str]) -> List[SearchResult]:
        results = []
        if not self.history_dir.exists():
            return results

        for log_file in self.history_dir.glob("*.jsonl"):
            try:
                with open(log_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    for line in f:
                        if not line.strip():
                            continue
                        entry = json.loads(line)
                        text_corpus = f"{entry.get('payload', '')} {entry.get('result_summary', '')}"
                        score = self._compute_score(query_tokens, text_corpus)
                        if score > 0:
                            results.append(SearchResult(
                                source_type="ARCHIVED_TURN",
                                source_id=log_file.stem,
                                section=f"tool:{entry.get('tool', 'unknown')}",
                                content=entry.get("result_summary", "")[:250],
                                relevance_score=score * 0.85
                            ))
            except Exception:
                continue
        return results

    @staticmethod
    def _tokenize(text: str) -> List[str]:
        """Low-RAM tokenization: alphanumeric lower-cased tokens > 2 chars."""
        return [tok for tok in re.findall(r"\b[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{3,}\b", text.lower())]

    def _compute_score(self, query_tokens: List[str], target_text: str) -> float:
        """
        Hardware-adaptive scoring:
        On low-spec/mobile, executes an exact frequency + coverage calculation.
        """
        if not target_text:
            return 0.0

        target_tokens = self._tokenize(target_text)
        if not target_tokens:
            return 0.0

        target_set = set(target_tokens)
        matches = 0
        freq = 0

        for q in query_tokens:
            if q in target_set:
                matches += 1
                freq += target_tokens.count(q)

        if matches == 0:
            return 0.0

        # BM25-style saturation: rewards coverage across unique query terms
        coverage_ratio = matches / len(query_tokens)
        density = freq / len(target_tokens)
        return round((coverage_ratio * 0.7) + (min(density, 1.0) * 0.3), 4)

--- START FILE: agent/orchestrator/loop.py ---
import os
import sys
import json
import time
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Tuple

from agent.memory.federated_ledger import SharedRootLedger, WorkerAgentScratchpad
from agent.memory.turn_retriever import FederatedTurnRetriever
from agent.prompts.ledger_prompts import LedgerPromptBuilder


def estimate_tokens(text_or_messages: Any) -> int:
    if isinstance(text_or_messages, str):
        return max(1, len(text_or_messages) // 4)
    elif isinstance(text_or_messages, list):
        total_chars = 0
        for msg in text_or_messages:
            if isinstance(msg, dict):
                total_chars += len(msg.get("content", ""))
            else:
                total_chars += len(str(msg))
        return max(1, total_chars // 4)
    return 1


class LedgerToolDispatcher:
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str, root_ledger: SharedRootLedger, worker: WorkerAgentScratchpad):
        self.workspace_root = workspace_root
        self.root = root_ledger
        self.worker = worker
        self.retriever = FederatedTurnRetriever(workspace_root)

    def dispatch(self, tool_name: str, arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:
        if tool_name == "ledger_record_invariant":
            inv = arguments.get("invariant", "")
            rationale = arguments.get("rationale", "")
            formatted = f"{inv} (Rationale: {rationale})" if rationale else inv
            self.worker.propose_invariant(formatted)
            return f"Invariant staged: {inv}", False

        elif tool_name == "ledger_manage_milestones":
            action = arguments.get("action")
            milestone = arguments.get("milestone", "")
            if action == "complete":
                self.worker.complete_subtask(milestone)
                return f"Milestone marked complete: {milestone}", False
            elif action == "add":
                if milestone not in self.worker.data.pending_milestones:
                    self.worker.data.pending_milestones.append(milestone)
                return f"Milestone queued: {milestone}", False
            return "Unknown milestone action", False

        elif tool_name == "ledger_query_memory":
            query = arguments.get("query", "")
            top_k = arguments.get("top_k", 4)
            hits = self.retriever.search(query=query, task_slug=self.root.task_slug, top_k=top_k)
            
            if not hits:
                return f"No memory matches found for query: '{query}'", False

            formatted_hits = []
            for h in hits:
                formatted_hits.append(
                    f"[{h.source_type} | {h.source_id} > {h.section}] (Score: {h.relevance_score})\n"
                    f"  {h.content}"
                )
            return "\n\n".join(formatted_hits), False

        elif tool_name == "ledger_turn_the_page":
            summary = arguments.get("checkpoint_summary", "Window cycle complete.")
            next_step = arguments.get("immediate_next_step", "Resume pending milestone.")
            hypotheses = arguments.get("active_hypotheses", [])
            
            self.worker.sync_to_root(verified_facts=[f"Checkpoint: {summary}"])
            
            for h in hypotheses:
                self.worker.record_hypothesis(h)

            rehydration_prompt = (
                f"[CONTEXT RESTORATION - NEW WINDOW]\n"
                f"PREVIOUS WINDOW SUMMARY: {summary}\n"
                f"IMMEDIATE DIRECTIVE: {next_step}\n\n"
                f"{self.worker.build_context_prompt()}"
            )
            return rehydration_prompt, True

        return f"Unknown tool: {tool_name}", False


class AutonomousAgentLoop:
    def __init__(
        self,
        workspace_root: str,
        task_slug: str,
        agent_id: str,
        role: str,
        base_instructions: str,
        token_ceiling: int = 4096
    ):
        self.workspace_root = workspace_root
        self.task_slug = task_slug
        self.role = role
        self.base_instructions = base_instructions
        
        self.root_ledger = SharedRootLedger(workspace_root, task_slug)
        self.worker = WorkerAgentScratchpad(self.root_ledger, agent_id, role)
        self.prompt_builder = LedgerPromptBuilder(token_ceiling=token_ceiling)
        self.dispatcher = LedgerToolDispatcher(workspace_root, self.root_ledger, self.worker)
        
        self.messages: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
        self.window_index = 1

    def count_active_tokens(self) -> int:
        return estimate_tokens(self.messages)

    def reset_context(self, rehydration_prompt: str):
        self.window_index += 1
        self.messages.clear()
        self.messages.append({"role": "user", "content": rehydration_prompt})

    def run_turn(self, simulated_llm_response: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        current_tokens = self.count_active_tokens()
        active_system_prompt = self.prompt_builder.build_system_prompt(
            role=self.role,
            base_instructions=self.base_instructions,
            current_tokens=current_tokens
        )

        tool_call = simulated_llm_response.get("tool_call")
        if tool_call:
            tool_name = tool_call.get("name")
            tool_args = tool_call.get("args", {})
            
            tool_output, should_flush = self.dispatcher.dispatch(tool_name, tool_args)
            
            if should_flush:
                print(f"\n[LOOP] >>> Context rollover triggered! Resetting to Window {self.window_index + 1}...")
                self.reset_context(rehydration_prompt=tool_output)
                return f"[PAGE_TURNED] Reseeded Window {self.window_index}"
            else:
                self.messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": f"Ran tool {tool_name}"})
                self.messages.append({"role": "tool", "content": tool_output})
                return f"[TOOL_EXECUTED] {tool_output}"

        content = simulated_llm_response.get("content", "")
        self.messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content})
        return f"[MESSAGE] {content}"

--- END FILE: agent/orchestrator/loop.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/orchestrator/queen_validator.py ---
import os
import sys
import ast
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Tuple, Optional
from pathlib import Path

try:
    from agent.hardware.runtime_adapter import detect_hardware_tier, RuntimeTier
except ImportError:
    class RuntimeTier:
        LOW_SPEC_MOBILE = "low_spec_mobile"
        MID_SPEC = "mid_spec"
        HIGH_SPEC = "high_spec"

    def detect_hardware_tier():
        is_android = "ANDROID_ROOT" in os.environ or "TERMUX_VERSION" in os.environ
        return RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE if is_android else RuntimeTier.MID_SPEC

from agent.memory.federated_ledger import SharedRootLedger, SharedRootLedgerState


@dataclass
class ValidationResult:
    approved: bool
    rejection_reasons: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    sanitized_delta: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


class QueenLedgerValidator:
    """
    Hardware-adaptive gatekeeper for worker scratchpad commits.
    Validates proposed invariants, milestones, and modified files
    before permitting commits into the SharedRootLedger.
    """
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str, root_ledger: SharedRootLedger):
        self.workspace_root = Path(workspace_root)
        self.root_ledger = root_ledger
        self.tier = detect_hardware_tier()

        # Hard anti-patterns / forbidden words for unverified invariant staging
        self.forbidden_invariant_patterns = [
            r"assume",
            r"probably",
            r"maybe",
            r"might work",
            r"untested",
            r"temporary fix",
            r"TODO",
        ]

    def validate_worker_pr(self, agent_id: str, role: str, worker_delta: Dict[str, Any]) -> ValidationResult:
        rejections: List[str] = []
        current_root: SharedRootLedgerState = self.root_ledger.get_read_only_view()

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # 1. Deterministic Validation: Modified Files & Syntax Check
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        verified_files = []
        for file_path_str in worker_delta.get("files_touched", []):
            target_path = self.workspace_root / file_path_str
            if not target_path.exists():
                rejections.append(f"File claim invalid: '{file_path_str}' does not exist on disk.")
                continue

            # If it is a Python file, run a strict AST syntax check (Zero RAM cost, runs everywhere)
            if file_path_str.endswith(".py"):
                try:
                    with open(target_path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                        ast.parse(f.read(), filename=file_path_str)
                except SyntaxError as e:
                    rejections.append(f"Syntax validation failed in '{file_path_str}' at line {e.lineno}: {e.msg}")
                    continue

            verified_files.append(file_path_str)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # 2. Invariant Conflict & Quality Verification
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        sanitized_invariants = []
        for inv in worker_delta.get("candidate_invariants", []):
            inv_lower = inv.lower()

            # Rule A: Invariants must have substance (> 15 chars)
            if len(inv.strip()) < 15:
                rejections.append(f"Invariant too trivial/vague: '{inv}'")
                continue

            # Rule B: Invariants must not contain speculative language
            matched_pattern = next((p for p in self.forbidden_invariant_patterns if p in inv_lower), None)
            if matched_pattern:
                rejections.append(f"Speculative invariant rejected (matches '{matched_pattern}'): '{inv}'")
                continue

            # Rule C: Check for direct contradiction against existing root invariants
            contradicts = False
            for existing in current_root.invariants:
                if self._check_direct_negation(inv_lower, existing.lower()):
                    rejections.append(f"Contradiction detected: '{inv}' contradicts existing root invariant '{existing}'")
                    contradicts = True
                    break

            if not contradicts:
                sanitized_invariants.append(inv)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # 3. Milestone Completion Integrity Check
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        sanitized_completions = []
        for milestone in worker_delta.get("resolved_milestones", []):
            # A worker cannot resolve a milestone that was never registered in root or scratchpad
            if milestone not in current_root.pending_milestones and milestone not in worker_delta.get("pending_milestones", []):
                # Warning rather than rejection: allow self-declared tasks if role matches
                if role not in ["builder", "tester", "researcher"]:
                    rejections.append(f"Unauthorized milestone completion attempt by {role}: '{milestone}'")
                    continue
            sanitized_completions.append(milestone)

        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        # 4. Final Verdict Determination
        # -------------------------------------------------------------
        is_approved = len(rejections) == 0

        sanitized_delta = {
            "candidate_invariants": sanitized_invariants,
            "resolved_milestones": sanitized_completions,
            "pending_milestones": worker_delta.get("pending_milestones", []),
            "files_touched": verified_files,
            "verified_facts": worker_delta.get("verified_facts", [])
        }

        return ValidationResult(
            approved=is_approved,
            rejection_reasons=rejections,
            sanitized_delta=sanitized_delta
        )

    def review_and_commit(self, agent_id: str, role: str, worker_delta: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """
        Main entry point for Queen Agent: reviews delta, commits if valid,
        or returns actionable diagnostics to the worker.
        """
        result = self.validate_worker_pr(agent_id, role, worker_delta)

        if not result.approved:
            error_report = (
                f"[QUEEN REJECTION] PR from {agent_id} ({role}) failed validation:\n"
                + "\n".join(f"  - {err}" for err in result.rejection_reasons)
            )
            return False, error_report

        # Commit sanitized payload into the root ledger
        self.root_ledger.commit_worker_delta(result.sanitized_delta)
        return True, f"[QUEEN APPROVED] Delta from {agent_id} committed to Root Ledger (Rev {self.root_ledger.get_read_only_view().revision})."

    @staticmethod
    def _check_direct_negation(phrase_a: str, phrase_b: str) -> bool:
        """Lightweight heuristic to catch basic polarity reversals without heavy NLP."""
        negations = ["never", "do not", "don't", "prohibit", "avoid"]
        a_has_neg = any(n in phrase_a for n in negations)
        b_has_neg = any(n in phrase_b for n in negations)

        # If one asserts prohibition and the other does not, check lexical overlap
        if a_has_neg != b_has_neg:
            words_a = set(w for w in phrase_a.split() if len(w) > 4)
            words_b = set(w for w in phrase_b.split() if len(w) > 4)
            common = words_a.intersection(words_b)
            if len(common) >= 2:
                return True
        return False

--- END FILE: agent/orchestrator/queen_validator.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/orchestrator/swarm.py ---
import os
import sys
import time
from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional

from agent.memory.federated_ledger import SharedRootLedger
from agent.orchestrator.loop import AutonomousAgentLoop
from agent.orchestrator.queen_validator import QueenLedgerValidator

class SwarmOrchestrator:
    """
    Hardware-adaptive multi-agent coordinator (Queen).
    Spins up worker agents, manages turn execution, and validates all
    pull-requests through QueenLedgerValidator before merging to root.
    """
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str, task_slug: str, token_ceiling: int = 4096):
        self.workspace_root = workspace_root
        self.task_slug = task_slug
        self.token_ceiling = token_ceiling
        
        # 1. Initialize root ledger and queen validator
        self.root_ledger = SharedRootLedger(workspace_root, task_slug)
        self.validator = QueenLedgerValidator(workspace_root, self.root_ledger)
        
        # 2. Worker registry
        self.workers: Dict[str, AutonomousAgentLoop] = {}

    def get_or_create_worker(self, role: str, base_instructions: str) -> AutonomousAgentLoop:
        agent_id = f"{role}_worker"
        if agent_id not in self.workers:
            self.workers[agent_id] = AutonomousAgentLoop(
                workspace_root=self.workspace_root,
                task_slug=self.task_slug,
                agent_id=agent_id,
                role=role,
                base_instructions=base_instructions,
                token_ceiling=self.token_ceiling
            )
        return self.workers[agent_id]

    def run_pipeline_step(
        self,
        role: str,
        instructions: str,
        simulated_actions: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        verified_facts: Optional[List[str]] = None
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Runs a worker agent, captures its staged delta, and passes it
        to QueenLedgerValidator. Returns True if approved and committed.
        """
        worker_loop = self.get_or_create_worker(role, instructions)
        worker_scratchpad = worker_loop.worker

        print(f"\n[SWARM] Activating subagent: {role.upper()} ({worker_scratchpad.agent_id})")
        for turn_idx, action in enumerate(simulated_actions):
            res = worker_loop.run_turn(action)
            print(f"  [Turn {turn_idx + 1}] {res}")

        # Construct pending delta payload from worker scratchpad
        staged_delta = {
            "candidate_invariants": list(worker_scratchpad.data.candidate_invariants),
            "resolved_milestones": list(worker_scratchpad.data.resolved_milestones),
            "pending_milestones": list(worker_scratchpad.data.pending_milestones),
            "files_touched": list(worker_scratchpad.data.files_touched),
            "verified_facts": verified_facts or []
        }

        # Queen reviews and commits or rejects
        approved, report = self.validator.review_and_commit(
            agent_id=worker_scratchpad.agent_id,
            role=role,
            worker_delta=staged_delta
        )
        print(f"[SWARM-QUEEN] {report}")

        if approved:
            # Clear local staged items upon successful root commit
            worker_scratchpad.data.candidate_invariants.clear()
            worker_scratchpad.data.resolved_milestones.clear()
            worker_scratchpad.data.files_touched.clear()
            worker_scratchpad._sync_disk()
            return True
        else:
            print(f"[SWARM-QUEEN] Worker {role} must rectify validation errors before state can merge.")
            return False

--- END FILE: agent/orchestrator/swarm.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/orchestrator.py ---
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from config.settings import settings
from models.schemas import RepositoryInfo
from tools.dashboard import dashboard
from tools.diff_explainer import DiffHunkExplainer
from tools.environment_setup import EnvironmentBootstrapper
from tools.git import GitTools
from tools.github import ForkManager, IssueSelector, PullRequestManager
from llm.router import LLMRouter
from tools.run_logger import RunLogger
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem
from ui.sprite_renderer import sprite
from tools.system_tier import CURRENT_TIER, HardwareTier
from tools.tests import TestRunner, format_guard_report

# New Deliberation, Memory & Gating Modules
from tools.agent_bus import blackboard

def _confirm(prompt: str) -> bool:
    """Prompt user for confirmation in CLI."""
    try:
        reply = input(f"\n[Approval Gate] {prompt} [y/N]: ").strip().lower()
        return reply in ("y", "yes")
    except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
        return False


def _cleanup_ephemeral_workspace(repo_dir: Path) -> None:
    """Clean up workspace after run completes."""
    if repo_dir and repo_dir.exists():
        try:
            shutil.rmtree(repo_dir)
        except Exception:
            pass


class Orchestrator:
    """
    Central Coordinator managing the autonomous engineering loop:
    Tractability Ranking -> Fork/Clone -> JIT Tooling -> Episodic Recall ->
    Plan -> Code -> Debug -> Adversarial Audit (Security & Reviewer) -> Diff Summary -> PR.
    """

    def __init__(self, dry_run: bool = False):
        self.dry_run = dry_run
        self.router = LLMRouter(settings.get_task_chains())
        self.repo_agent = RepositoryAgent(router=self.router)
        self.fork_mgr = ForkManager()
        self.pr_mgr = PullRequestManager()
        self.issue_selector = IssueSelector()
        self.diff_explainer = DiffHunkExplainer()
        self.bootstrapper = EnvironmentBootstrapper()

        # Integrated Memory & Adversarial Gates
        self.user_mem = UserProfileMemory()
        self.ep_mem = EpisodicExecutionMemory()
        self.security_agent = SecurityAgent(self.router)
        self.reviewer_agent = ReviewerAgent(self.router)
        self.distillation_daemon = distillation_daemon

    def _auto_install_dependencies(self, repo_dir: Path, test_runner: TestRunner) -> None:
        """Installs toolchains and dependencies Just-In-Time based on manifest presence."""
        has = lambda fname: (repo_dir / fname).exists()

        # 1. Trigger JIT Environment Toolchain Setup
        self.bootstrapper.bootstrap_for_repo(repo_dir)

        # 2. Package Manager resolution & install
        if has("package.json") and not has("node_modules"):
            from tools.tests import resolve_package_manager_command
            install_cmd = resolve_package_manager_command(repo_dir)
            if install_cmd:
                test_runner.shell.execute(install_cmd, auto_approve_override=True)
        elif has("Cargo.toml") and not (repo_dir / "target").exists():
            test_runner.shell.execute("cargo fetch", auto_approve_override=True)
        elif has("go.mod"):
            test_runner.shell.execute("go mod download", auto_approve_override=True)
        elif has("requirements.txt"):
            pip_cmd = (
                "pip install -r requirements.txt --break-system-packages"
                if "TERMUX_VERSION" in os.environ or "com.termux" in os.environ.get("PREFIX", "")
                else "pip install -r requirements.txt"
            )
            test_runner.shell.execute(pip_cmd, auto_approve_override=True)

    def run(
        self,
        search_query: Optional[str] = None,
        target_repo: Optional[str] = None,
        target_issue: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> None:
        run_logger = RunLogger()
        repo_dir = None

        try:
            # Reset blackboard for fresh run
            blackboard.clear()
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "SCANNING_AST"})

            # 1. Resolve Target Repo
            if target_repo:
                repo_info = self.issue_selector.get_repo_info(target_repo)
            else:
                repo_info = self.issue_selector.find_best_repo(search_query)

            if not repo_info:
                print("[Orchestrator] No matching repository found.")
                dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "ERROR"})
                return

            # 2. Resolve or Score Target Issue
            if target_issue:
                issue = self.issue_selector.get_issue(repo_info.full_name, target_issue)
            else:
                candidate_issues = self.issue_selector.list_open_issues(repo_info.full_name)
                # Deterministic Tractability Pre-Flight Ranking
                issue = self.repo_agent.rank_and_select_issue(candidate_issues)

            if not issue:
                print("[Orchestrator] No solvable issue found for this repository.")
                dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "ERROR"})
                return

            blackboard.publish("Orchestrator", "TARGET_ISSUE", {
                "repo": repo_info.full_name,
                "number": issue.get("number"),
                "title": issue.get("title")
            })

            # 3. Fork and Clone
            sprite.set_state("working")
            sprite.newline()
            print("\n=== Forking repository ===")
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "WORKING"})
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"Forking and cloning {repo_info.full_name}..."})

            fork_data = self.fork_mgr.create_fork(repo_info.full_name)
            fork_owner = fork_data["owner"]["login"]
            print(f"Forked to: {fork_data['full_name']}")
            print("Waiting for fork to become ready...")
            time.sleep(5)

            fork_repo = RepositoryInfo(
                owner=fork_owner,
                name=repo_info.name,
                full_name=fork_data["full_name"],
                clone_url=fork_data["clone_url"],
                ssh_url=fork_data["ssh_url"],
                default_branch=repo_info.default_branch,
            )

            print("\n=== Cloning your fork (fresh) ===")
            repo_dir = self.repo_agent.clone(fork_repo, settings.WORKSPACE_DIR)
            fs = SafeFileSystem(repo_dir)
            git_tools = GitTools(repo_dir)
            test_runner = TestRunner(repo_dir)
            coding_agent = CodingAgent(self.router, fs)
            debug_agent = DebuggingAgent(self.router, fs)

            # 4. JIT Preflight Dependencies
            self._auto_install_dependencies(repo_dir, test_runner)

            print("\n=== Checking host environment / repo dependencies ===")
            guard_result = test_runner.preflight_check()
            if not guard_result.ok:
                sprite.set_state("sad")
                sprite.newline()
                dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "ERROR"})
                dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": "Missing repo dependencies."})
                print(format_guard_report(guard_result))
                run_logger.set_outcome("failed_missing_repo_dependency")
                run_logger.note("host environment or repo dependencies missing")
                run_logger.attach_model_calls(self.router.call_log)
                run_logger.finalize()
                return

            # 5. Episodic Fix Retrieval & Repository Research
            issue_title = issue.get("title", "")
            issue_body = issue.get("body", "")
            past_fixes = self.ep_mem.recall_similar_fix(f"{issue_title} {issue_body}")
            if past_fixes:
                print(f"\n[Episodic Memory] Found {len(past_fixes)} relevant past resolution(s) in local SQLite.")
                blackboard.publish("EpisodicMemory", "PAST_SOLUTIONS", past_fixes)

            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "SCANNING_AST"})
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": "Running Repository Research Agent..."})
            researcher = RepositoryResearchAgent(repo_dir=str(repo_dir))
            approved_research = researcher.present_proposals()
            blackboard.publish("ResearcherAgent", "RESEARCH_PROPOSAL", approved_research)

            branch_name = f"fix-issue-{issue.get('number', int(time.time()))}"
            git_tools.create_branch(branch_name)

            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "PLANNING"})
            plan = coding_agent.create_plan(issue, approved_research)
            blackboard.publish("CodingAgent", "PLAN", plan)

            # 6. Coding & Reflection Loop
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "CODING"})
            coding_agent.execute_plan(plan)

            retries = 0
            max_retries = 3
            test_passed = False

            while retries < max_retries:
                test_result = test_runner.run_tests()
                if test_result.passed:
                    test_passed = True
                    break
                retries += 1
                dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "REFLECTING"})
                debug_agent.diagnose_and_patch(issue, test_result.output)

            # 7. Structural Diff Audit & Adversarial Pre-Review Gates
            raw_diff = git_tools.diff()
            diff_audit = self.diff_explainer.format_review_summary(raw_diff)

            print("\n" + "=" * 55)
            print(diff_audit)
            print("=" * 55 + "\n")

            # Adversarial Security Audit
            sec_result = self.security_agent.audit_diff(raw_diff)
            blackboard.publish("SecurityAgent", "SECURITY_AUDIT", sec_result)
            if not sec_result["passed"]:
                print("\n[SECURITY GATE WARNING] Potential security or injection issues flagged:")
                for r in sec_result["reasons"]:
                    print(f"  • {r}")

            # Adversarial Reviewer Audit
            rev_result = self.reviewer_agent.review_patch(raw_diff, test_passed)
            blackboard.publish("ReviewerAgent", "REVIEW_DECISION", rev_result)
            if not rev_result["approved"]:
                print("\n[REVIEWER GATE CRITIQUE] Quality issues flagged:")
                for c in rev_result["critiques"]:
                    print(f"  • {c}")

            # If tests passed and patch verified, log resolution into Episodic Memory
            if test_passed and raw_diff:
                self.ep_mem.record_resolution(
                    repo_name=repo_info.full_name,
                    error_signature=f"{issue_title}\n{issue_body[:300]}",
                    resolution_patch=raw_diff[:2000],
                    explanation=plan.get("summary", "Resolved via automated repair loop.")
                )

            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": diff_audit})
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "AWAITING_APPROVAL"})

            if self.dry_run:
                print("\n[Dry Run] Completed verification successfully. Skipping commit & push.")
                dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "SUCCESS"})
                return

            if not _confirm("Create commit and push?"):
                dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": "User aborted commit and push."})
                run_logger.set_test_result(passed=test_passed, retries_used=retries)
                run_logger.set_review_result(approved=False)
                run_logger.set_outcome("aborted")
                run_logger.attach_model_calls(self.router.call_log)
                run_logger.finalize()
                return

            issue_num = issue.get("number")
            issue_title = issue.get("title")
            git_tools.commit_all(f"Fix #{issue_num}: {issue_title}")
            git_tools.push("origin", branch_name)

            pr_title = f"Fix #{issue_num}: {issue_title}"
            pr_body = (
                f"Closes #{issue_num}\n\n"
                f"### Summary of Changes\n{plan.get('summary', '')}\n\n"
                f"{diff_audit}"
            )

            if not _confirm("Create PR?"):
                dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "SUCCESS"})
                dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": "Branch pushed successfully (no PR created)."})
                run_logger.set_test_result(passed=test_passed, retries_used=retries)
                run_logger.set_review_result(approved=True)
                run_logger.set_outcome("pushed_no_pr")
                run_logger.attach_model_calls(self.router.call_log)
                run_logger.finalize()
                return

            pr = self.pr_mgr.create_pull_request(
                repo_info.full_name,
                pr_title,
                head=f"{fork_owner}:{branch_name}",
                base=repo_info.default_branch,
                body=pr_body,
            )
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "SUCCESS"})
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"Pull Request created: {pr.get('html_url')}"})
            run_logger.set_test_result(passed=test_passed, retries_used=retries)
            run_logger.set_review_result(approved=True)
            run_logger.set_outcome("pr_created")
            run_logger.attach_model_calls(self.router.call_log)
            run_logger.finalize()

        finally:
            if repo_dir:
                _cleanup_ephemeral_workspace(Path(repo_dir))
            try:
                self.distillation_daemon.execute_distillation_cycle()
                import sys
                if sys.stdin.isatty():
                    interactive_sync_prompt()
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Orchestrator] Distillation / sync lifecycle notice: {e}")

--- END FILE: agent/orchestrator.py ---

import os
import json
import sqlite3
from dataclasses import asdict
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

from agent.pa_scaffolder.schemas import (
    PersonalAgentConfig,
    AgentPersona,
    OperatorProfile,
    InferenceConfig
)
from agent.core.branding import BRAND


class PersonalAgentScaffolder:
    """
    Builds and writes a clean, isolated Personal Agent (PA) deployment
    into ~/.personal_agent/ (or custom directory).
    """
    def __init__(self, target_home: Optional[str] = None):
        self.target_home = Path(target_home or (Path.home() / ".personal_agent")).resolve()

    def deploy(self, config: PersonalAgentConfig) -> Path:
        # 1. Create directory structure
        mem_dir = self.target_home / "memory"
        hist_dir = mem_dir / "history"
        telemetry_dir = self.target_home / "telemetry"
        runtime_dir = self.target_home / "runtime"
        
        for d in [self.target_home, mem_dir, hist_dir, telemetry_dir, runtime_dir]:
            d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        # 2. Deploy config.json
        config.pa_home = str(self.target_home)
        config_path = self.target_home / "config.json"
        with open(config_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(config.to_dict(), f, indent=2)

        # 3. Deploy memory/profile.json (Dynamic, hot-reloading profile)
        profile_path = mem_dir / "profile.json"
        with open(profile_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump({
                "persona": asdict(config.persona),
                "operator": asdict(config.operator)
            }, f, indent=2)

        # 4. Initialize Structured Cognitive Brain (SQLite)
        self._init_brain_database(mem_dir / "brain.db", config.operator)

        # 5. Initialize Starter PA Task Ledger
        self._init_task_ledger(mem_dir / "PA-LEDGER.md", config.persona, config.operator)

        # 6. Deploy Standalone PA Runtime & Identity Modifier Tool
        self._deploy_standalone_runtime(runtime_dir)

        return self.target_home

    def _init_brain_database(self, db_path: Path, operator: OperatorProfile):
        """Creates tables for habits, routines, nutrition, and relationships."""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
        cur = conn.cursor()
        
        cur.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS habits_and_routines (
            metric_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
            metric_value TEXT,
            updated_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
        """)

        cur.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS daily_nutrition (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            date_str TEXT,
            food_item TEXT,
            calories REAL,
            protein_g REAL,
            carbs_g REAL,
            fats_g REAL,
            logged_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
        )
        """)

        cur.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS people_directory (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            name TEXT UNIQUE,
            relationship TEXT,
            birthday TEXT,
            notes TEXT,
            face_embedding_id TEXT
        )
        """)

        # Pre-seed operator habits
        seed_data = [
            ("wake_time", operator.wake_time),
            ("sleep_time", operator.sleep_time),
            ("work_hours", operator.work_hours),
            ("gym_time", operator.gym_time),
            ("daily_calories_target", str(operator.daily_calories_target)),
            ("daily_protein_target_g", str(operator.daily_protein_target_g)),
        ]
        cur.executemany("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO habits_and_routines (metric_key, metric_value) VALUES (?, ?)", seed_data)
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

    def _init_task_ledger(self, ledger_path: Path, persona: AgentPersona, operator: OperatorProfile):
        content = f"""# PERSONAL AGENT ROOT LEDGER
**Companion**: {persona.name} ({persona.tone_style})
**Operator**: {operator.call_sign}

### Immutable Directives
- [INVARIANT] The companion identity is {persona.name}; prioritize {operator.call_sign}'s focus and time.
- [INVARIANT] Perform destructive operations only after explicit operator confirmation.

### Active Milestones
- [ ] Complete initial device environment setup and health profile calibration.
- [ ] Connect communication channels (Email, Slack, WhatsApp).
"""
        with open(ledger_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(content)

    def _deploy_standalone_runtime(self, runtime_dir: Path):
        """Drops the standalone PA execution loop into runtime/pa_runtime.py."""
        runtime_code = '''import os
import sys
import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

class StandalonePersonalAgent:
    def __init__(self, pa_home: Optional[str] = None):
        self.pa_home = Path(pa_home or Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
        self.config_path = self.pa_home / "config.json"
        self.profile_path = self.pa_home / "memory" / "profile.json"
        self.db_path = self.pa_home / "memory" / "brain.db"
        self.config = self._load_json(self.config_path)
        self.profile = self._load_json(self.profile_path)

    def _load_json(self, path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)

    def _save_json(self, path: Path, data: Dict[str, Any]):
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return self.profile.get("persona", {}).get("name", "Hype")

    @property
    def operator(self) -> str:
        return self.profile.get("operator", {}).get("call_sign", "Operator")

    def update_agent_persona(
        self,
        new_name: Optional[str] = None,
        new_tone: Optional[str] = None,
        new_call_sign: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        In-flight tool allowing the agent or user to rename and recalibrate
        identity without reinstalling or restarting the process.
        """
        if new_name:
            self.profile.setdefault("persona", {})["name"] = new_name
            self.profile["persona"]["wake_word"] = new_name.lower()
            self.config.setdefault("persona", {})["name"] = new_name
            self.config["persona"]["wake_word"] = new_name.lower()

        if new_tone:
            self.profile.setdefault("persona", {})["tone_style"] = new_tone
            self.config.setdefault("persona", {})["tone_style"] = new_tone

        if new_call_sign:
            self.profile.setdefault("operator", {})["call_sign"] = new_call_sign
            self.config.setdefault("operator", {})["call_sign"] = new_call_sign

        self._save_json(self.profile_path, self.profile)
        self._save_json(self.config_path, self.config)
        return {
            "status": "SUCCESS",
            "active_name": self.name,
            "operator": self.operator,
            "tone": self.profile.get("persona", {}).get("tone_style")
        }

    def greeting(self) -> str:
        return f"Hey {self.operator}! {self.name} is online and standing by. What are we building today?"

if __name__ == "__main__":
    pa = StandalonePersonalAgent()
    print(pa.greeting())
'''
        with open(runtime_dir / "pa_runtime.py", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(runtime_code)

--- END FILE: agent/pa_scaffolder/scaffolder.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/pa_scaffolder/schemas.py ---
import os
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from pathlib import Path


@dataclass
class AgentPersona:
    name: str = "Hype"
    wake_word: str = "hype"
    tone_style: str = "Supportive & High Energy"
    voice_profile: str = "dynamic-expressive-1"

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "AgentPersona":
        return cls(
            name=data.get("name", "Hype"),
            wake_word=data.get("wake_word", data.get("name", "hype").lower()),
            tone_style=data.get("tone_style", "Supportive & High Energy"),
            voice_profile=data.get("voice_profile", "dynamic-expressive-1")
        )


@dataclass
class OperatorProfile:
    call_sign: str = "Operator"
    wake_time: str = "07:00"
    sleep_time: str = "23:00"
    work_hours: str = "09:00 - 18:00"
    gym_time: str = "18:30 - 19:30"
    daily_calories_target: int = 2200
    daily_protein_target_g: int = 150
    preferred_ide: str = "vscode"
    preferred_shell: str = "bash"

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "OperatorProfile":
        return cls(
            call_sign=data.get("call_sign", "Operator"),
            wake_time=data.get("wake_time", "07:00"),
            sleep_time=data.get("sleep_time", "23:00"),
            work_hours=data.get("work_hours", "09:00 - 18:00"),
            gym_time=data.get("gym_time", "18:30 - 19:30"),
            daily_calories_target=int(data.get("daily_calories_target", 2200)),
            daily_protein_target_g=int(data.get("daily_protein_target_g", 150)),
            preferred_ide=data.get("preferred_ide", "vscode"),
            preferred_shell=data.get("preferred_shell", "bash")
        )


@dataclass
class InferenceConfig:
    engine: str = "openrouter"               # "openrouter" | "direct_api" | "app_automation" | "local_ollama"
    base_url: str = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"
    api_key: Optional[str] = None
    model: str = "anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet"
    app_mode: bool = False
    app_target: str = "chatgpt"

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "InferenceConfig":
        return cls(
            engine=data.get("engine", "openrouter"),
            base_url=data.get("base_url", "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"),
            api_key=data.get("api_key"),
            model=data.get("model", "anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet"),
            app_mode=bool(data.get("app_mode", False)),
            app_target=data.get("app_target", "chatgpt")
        )


@dataclass
class PersonalAgentConfig:
    version: str = "1.0.0"
    created_at: str = "2026-09"
    revision: int = 1
    persona: AgentPersona = field(default_factory=AgentPersona)
    operator: OperatorProfile = field(default_factory=OperatorProfile)
    inference: InferenceConfig = field(default_factory=InferenceConfig)
    pa_home: str = str(Path.home() / ".personal_agent")

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return asdict(self)

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "PersonalAgentConfig":
        return cls(
            version=data.get("version", "1.0.0"),
            created_at=data.get("created_at", "2026-09"),
            revision=data.get("revision", 1),
            persona=AgentPersona.from_dict(data.get("persona", {})),
            operator=OperatorProfile.from_dict(data.get("operator", {})),
            inference=InferenceConfig.from_dict(data.get("inference", {})),
            pa_home=data.get("pa_home", str(Path.home() / ".personal_agent"))
        )

"""

--- START FILE: agent/personal/cli.py ---
import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent
if str(REPO_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT))

from agent.personal.sentinel_device import EphemeralPersonalAgent

SENTINEL_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are Sentinel-Personal, a private local Android automation agent.
ZERO-RETENTION MANDATE:
- All data resides strictly in volatile memory. No disk writes to training buffers or logs.
- You operate over STRUCTURALLY SANITIZED elements. Private user text is stripped.

AVAILABLE ACTIONS (JSON object only):
1. Unlock phone:
   {"action": "unlock", "pin": "<PIN>"}
2. Launch app:
   {"action": "launch_app", "package": "<package_name>", "activity": "<optional>"}
3. Check notifications:
   {"action": "read_notifications"}
4. Inspect interactive screen buttons:
   {"action": "inspect_screen"}
5. Tap recognized control button:
   {"action": "tap_control", "label": "<control_label>"}
6. Tap coordinates:
   {"action": "tap", "x": <int>, "y": <int>}
7. Respond to user:
   {"action": "respond", "message": "<your_message>"}

Return ONLY a single valid JSON object.
"""


class LocalLLMClient:
    def __init__(self, endpoint_url: Optional[str] = None, model: Optional[str] = None):
        self.endpoint_url = endpoint_url or os.environ.get(
            "LOCAL_LLM_URL", "http://localhost:11434/v1/chat/completions"
        )
        self.model = model or os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "llama3.2")

    def query(self, messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> str:
        payload = {
            "model": self.model,
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": 0.1,
            "max_tokens": 1024,
            "stream": False,
        }
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            self.endpoint_url,
            data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
            return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()


def extract_json_action(text: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    text = text.strip()
    match = re.search(r"```(?:json)?\s*(\{.*?\})\s*```", text, re.DOTALL)
    if match:
        text = match.group(1)
    else:
        obj_match = re.search(r"\{.*?\}", text, re.DOTALL)
        if obj_match:
            text = obj_match.group(0)

    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return None


def run_interactive_cli(adb_host: str = "127.0.0.1", adb_port: int = 5555):
    print("=" * 60)
    print("     SENTINEL-PERSONAL: STRUCTURALLY ISOLATED CLI")
    print("=" * 60)
    print("• Retention: Ephemeral RAM-only")
    print("• Privacy: Structural field allow-listing (No raw text leak)")
    print("=" * 60 + "\n")

    agent = EphemeralPersonalAgent(adb_host=adb_host, adb_port=adb_port)
    llm = LocalLLMClient()
    chat_history: List[Dict[str, str]] = [{"role": "system", "content": SENTINEL_SYSTEM_PROMPT}]

    while True:
        try:
            user_input = input("personal-agent> ").strip()
        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
            print("\nPurging session. Exiting.")
            agent.clear_memory()
            break

        if not user_input or user_input.lower() in ("exit", "quit"):
            agent.clear_memory()
            break
        if user_input.lower() == "clear":
            chat_history = [{"role": "system", "content": SENTINEL_SYSTEM_PROMPT}]
            agent.clear_memory()
            print("[Sentinel] Memory purged.\n")
            continue

        chat_history.append({"role": "user", "content": user_input})

        try:
            raw_response = llm.query(chat_history)
            action_data = extract_json_action(raw_response)

            if not action_data or "action" not in action_data:
                print(f"\n[Agent]: {raw_response}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": raw_response})
                continue

            action = action_data.get("action")

            if action == "respond":
                msg = action_data.get("message", "")
                print(f"\n[Agent]: {msg}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": json.dumps(action_data)})

            elif action == "unlock":
                pin = action_data.get("pin", "")
                print(f"[*] Unlocking device (PIN: {'*' * len(pin)})...")
                ok = agent.unlock_device(pin)
                res = "Device unlocked." if ok else "Unlock failed."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})

            elif action == "launch_app":
                pkg = action_data.get("package", "")
                ok = agent.launch_app(pkg, action_data.get("activity"))
                res = f"Launched {pkg}." if ok else f"Failed to launch {pkg}."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})

            elif action == "inspect_screen":
                elements = agent.get_screen_elements()
                preview = [f"{e['class']} (label: {e['control_label']}, center: {e['center']})" for e in elements[:6]]
                summary = f"Interactive Elements ({len(elements)} found):\n" + "\n".join(preview)
                print(f"\n{summary}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {summary}"})

            elif action == "tap_control":
                lbl = action_data.get("label", "")
                ok = agent.tap_element_by_control(lbl)
                res = f"Tapped control '{lbl}'." if ok else f"Control '{lbl}' not found on screen."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})

            elif action == "tap":
                x, y = action_data.get("x", 0), action_data.get("y", 0)
                ok = agent.tap_coordinates(x, y)
                res = f"Tapped ({x}, {y})." if ok else f"Failed to tap ({x}, {y})."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})

            elif action == "read_notifications":
                notes = agent.get_unread_notifications()
                summary = f"Active Notifications: {len(notes)} item(s) found across packages: " + ", ".join(
                    {n.get("packageName", "unknown") for n in notes}
                )
                print(f"\n{summary}\n")
                chat_history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {summary}"})

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"\n[Error] {e}\n")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Sentinel-Personal CLI")
    parser.add_argument("--adb-host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--adb-port", type=int, default=5555)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    run_interactive_cli(adb_host=args.adb_host, adb_port=args.adb_port)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

--- START FILE: agent/personal/privacy_guard.py ---
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Set

# Non-sensitive system actions and control labels that are safe to expose
SAFE_CONTROL_LABELS: Set[str] = {
    "ok", "cancel", "submit", "next", "back", "done", "search", "settings",
    "save", "delete", "open", "close", "yes", "no", "continue", "confirm",
    "home", "menu", "send", "allow", "deny", "install", "update"
}

# Permitted packages whose notification counts/status may be queried
ALLOWED_NOTIFICATION_KEYS: Set[str] = {"id", "packageName", "postTime", "tag"}


class StructuralPrivacyGuard:
    """
    Enforces schema-driven allow-listing. Drops unstructured user data,
    private screen text, and unverified metadata before context ingestion.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def filter_ui_hierarchy(raw_xml: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Parses Android UIAutomator XML and extracts ONLY actionable UI elements
        (clickable buttons, inputs, tabs). Strips private text bodies.
        """
        if not raw_xml or not raw_xml.strip():
            return []

        try:
            root = ET.fromstring(raw_xml)
        except Exception:
            return []

        interactive_nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for node in root.iter("node"):
            attrib = node.attrib
            clickable = attrib.get("clickable") == "true"
            checkable = attrib.get("checkable") == "true"
            resource_id = attrib.get("resource-id", "")
            class_name = attrib.get("class", "").split(".")[-1]
            bounds = attrib.get("bounds", "")
            raw_text = attrib.get("text", "").strip()

            # Retain only actionable components (buttons, input fields, clickable elements)
            if clickable or checkable or "button" in class_name.lower() or "edittext" in class_name.lower():
                # Allow text ONLY if it is a known static system control
                safe_text = raw_text if raw_text.lower() in SAFE_CONTROL_LABELS else "[CONTENT_HIDDEN]"
                
                # Parse bounds [x1,y1][x2,y2]
                center_coords = None
                coords = re.findall(r"\[(\d+),(\d+)\]", bounds)
                if len(coords) == 2:
                    x1, y1 = int(coords[0][0]), int(coords[0][1])
                    x2, y2 = int(coords[1][0]), int(coords[1][1])
                    center_coords = {"x": (x1 + x2) // 2, "y": (y1 + y2) // 2}

                interactive_nodes.append({
                    "class": class_name,
                    "resource_id": resource_id if resource_id else "none",
                    "control_label": safe_text,
                    "center": center_coords,
                    "clickable": clickable,
                })

        return interactive_nodes

    @staticmethod
    def filter_notifications(raw_notifications: List[Dict[str, Any]], allow_content: bool = False) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Restricts notifications to structural metadata. Drops raw body
        payloads unless allow_content is explicitly set by the caller.
        """
        sanitized = []
        for item in raw_notifications:
            filtered_item = {k: item[k] for k in ALLOWED_NOTIFICATION_KEYS if k in item}
            if allow_content:
                # Truncate and strip tokens/OTPs even if content is authorized
                title = str(item.get("title", ""))[:60]
                filtered_item["title"] = title
            else:
                filtered_item["title"] = "[NOTIFICATION_RECEIVED]"
            sanitized.append(filtered_item)
        return sanitized

--- END FILE: agent/personal/privacy_guard.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/personal/sentinel_device.py ---
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from agent.personal.privacy_guard import StructuralPrivacyGuard


class EphemeralPersonalAgent:
    """
    Zero-retention personal intelligence agent for local device automation.
    Enforces adaptive viewport bounds and structural allow-listing privacy.
    """

    def __init__(self, adb_host: str = "127.0.0.1", adb_port: int = 5555):
        self.adb_target = f"{adb_host}:{adb_port}"
        self._session_context: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
        self._cached_dimensions: Optional[Tuple[int, int]] = None
        self.guard = StructuralPrivacyGuard()

    def _run_adb(self, cmd: List[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        """Executes an ADB command targeting the connected device."""
        full_cmd = ["adb", "-s", self.adb_target] + cmd
        return subprocess.run(full_cmd, text=True, capture_output=True)

    def get_screen_dimensions(self) -> Tuple[int, int]:
        """Queries physical display resolution via wm size."""
        if self._cached_dimensions:
            return self._cached_dimensions

        res = self._run_adb(["shell", "wm", "size"])
        matches = re.findall(r"size:\s*(\d+)x(\d+)", res.stdout)
        if matches:
            w, h = int(matches[-1][0]), int(matches[-1][1])
            self._cached_dimensions = (w, h)
            return self._cached_dimensions
        return (1080, 2400)

    def unlock_device(self, pin: str) -> bool:
        """Wakes screen and performs adaptive vertical swipe unlock."""
        w, h = self.get_screen_dimensions()
        self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "keyevent", "26"])
        
        center_x = w // 2
        start_y = int(h * 0.80)
        end_y = int(h * 0.25)
        self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "swipe", str(center_x), str(start_y), str(center_x), str(end_y), "250"])

        res = self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "text", pin])
        self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "keyevent", "66"])
        return res.returncode == 0

    def launch_app(self, package_name: str, activity_name: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
        """Launches application by package name."""
        if activity_name:
            target = f"{package_name}/{activity_name}"
            cmd = ["shell", "am", "start", "-n", target]
        else:
            cmd = ["shell", "monkey", "-p", package_name, "-c", "android.intent.category.LAUNCHER", "1"]
        res = self._run_adb(cmd)
        return res.returncode == 0

    def get_screen_elements(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Dumps UI hierarchy and returns structurally sanitized interactive nodes."""
        dump_path = "/sdcard/window_dump.xml"
        self._run_adb(["shell", "uiautomator", "dump", dump_path])
        cat_res = self._run_adb(["shell", "cat", dump_path])
        self._run_adb(["shell", "rm", "-f", dump_path])
        return self.guard.filter_ui_hierarchy(cat_res.stdout)

    def tap_coordinates(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:
        """Executes simulated touch at absolute pixel (x, y)."""
        res = self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "tap", str(x), str(y)])
        return res.returncode == 0

    def tap_element_by_control(self, control_label: str) -> bool:
        """Taps an on-screen interactive control by its validated label."""
        elements = self.get_screen_elements()
        for el in elements:
            if el.get("control_label", "").lower() == control_label.lower() and el.get("center"):
                coords = el["center"]
                return self.tap_coordinates(coords["x"], coords["y"])
        return False

    def get_unread_notifications(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Extracts device notifications filtered by structural allow-list."""
        termux_api = subprocess.run(["termux-notification-list"], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if termux_api.returncode != 0 or not termux_api.stdout.strip():
            return []
        try:
            raw_notes = json.loads(termux_api.stdout)
            return self.guard.filter_notifications(raw_notes, allow_content=False)
        except Exception:
            return []

    def clear_memory(self):
        """Purges ephemeral memory cache."""
        self._session_context.clear()
