--- START FILE: llm/providers.py ---
import os
import time
import requests
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from config.settings import settings


class ProviderCallError(Exception):
    pass


def _retry_post(url: str, headers: dict, payload: dict, timeout: int = 90, retries: int = 4) -> dict:
    backoff = 2.0
    last_error = None
    for attempt in range(retries):
        try:
            res = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=timeout)
            if res.status_code == 429:
                time.sleep(backoff)
                backoff *= 2
                continue
            res.raise_for_status()
            return res.json()
        except Exception as e:
            last_error = e
            if attempt == retries - 1:
                raise ProviderCallError(f"Call failed after {retries} attempts: {e}") from e
            time.sleep(backoff)
            backoff *= 2
    raise ProviderCallError(f"Call failed: {last_error}")


class OllamaDirectClient:
    """Direct client for local Ollama instances running Qwen, Llama, or Hermes."""

    def __init__(self, base_url: Optional[str] = None):
        self.base_url = base_url or os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:11434")

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        payload = {
            "model": model,
            "messages": messages,
            "options": {"temperature": temperature},
            "stream": False,
        }
        data = _retry_post(f"{self.base_url}/api/chat", {"Content-Type": "application/json"}, payload, timeout=180)
        text = data.get("message", {}).get("content", "")
        usage = {
            "prompt_tokens": data.get("prompt_eval_count", 0),
            "completion_tokens": data.get("eval_count", 0),
        }
        return text, usage


class HuggingFaceDirectClient:
    """Calls Hugging Face Serverless Inference API directly."""

    def __init__(self, api_key: Optional[str] = None):
        self.api_key = api_key or getattr(settings, "HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY", "") or os.getenv("HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY", "")

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        if not self.api_key:
            raise ValueError("HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY is not set.")

        prompt = "\n".join([f"{m['role'].upper()}: {m['content']}" for m in messages]) + "\nASSISTANT: "
        headers = {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_key}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
        }
        payload = {
            "inputs": prompt,
            "parameters": {
                "max_new_tokens": 2048,
                "temperature": max(0.01, temperature),
                "return_full_text": False,
            },
        }
        data = _retry_post(
            f"https://api-inference.huggingface.co/models/{model}",
            headers=headers,
            payload=payload,
            timeout=120,
        )

        text = ""
        if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) > 0:
            text = data[0].get("generated_text", "")
        elif isinstance(data, dict):
            text = data.get("generated_text", "") or data.get("summary_text", "")
        else:
            raise ProviderCallError(f"Unexpected Hugging Face response structure: {data}")

        usage = {
            "prompt_tokens": len(prompt.split()),
            "completion_tokens": len(text.split()),
        }
        return text, usage


class AnthropicDirectClient:
    """Calls Anthropic's native API directly — no OpenRouter markup."""

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        if not settings.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:
            raise ValueError("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY is not set.")

        system_msg = ""
        user_messages = []
        for m in messages:
            if m["role"] == "system":
                system_msg += m["content"] + "\n"
            else:
                user_messages.append(m)

        headers = {
            "x-api-key": settings.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY,
            "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
            "content-type": "application/json",
        }
        payload = {
            "model": model,
            "max_tokens": 4096,
            "temperature": temperature,
            "messages": user_messages,
        }
        if system_msg.strip():
            payload["system"] = system_msg.strip()

        data = _retry_post("https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages", headers, payload)
        text = "".join(block.get("text", "") for block in data.get("content", []))
        usage = {
            "prompt_tokens": data.get("usage", {}).get("input_tokens", 0),
            "completion_tokens": data.get("usage", {}).get("output_tokens", 0),
        }
        return text, usage


class OpenAIDirectClient:
    """Calls OpenAI's native API directly — no OpenRouter markup."""

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        if not settings.OPENAI_API_KEY:
            raise ValueError("OPENAI_API_KEY is not set.")

        headers = {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {settings.OPENAI_API_KEY}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
        }
        payload = {"model": model, "messages": messages, "temperature": temperature}

        data = _retry_post("https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions", headers, payload)
        text = data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
        usage_raw = data.get("usage", {})
        usage = {
            "prompt_tokens": usage_raw.get("prompt_tokens", 0),
            "completion_tokens": usage_raw.get("completion_tokens", 0),
        }
        return text, usage


class GoogleDirectClient:
    """Calls Google's Gemini API directly — no OpenRouter markup."""

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        if not settings.GOOGLE_API_KEY:
            raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set.")

        contents = []
        system_instruction = None
        for m in messages:
            if m["role"] == "system":
                system_instruction = {"parts": [{"text": m["content"]}]}
            else:
                role = "model" if m["role"] == "assistant" else "user"
                contents.append({"role": role, "parts": [{"text": m["content"]}]})

        payload = {"contents": contents, "generationConfig": {"temperature": temperature}}
        if system_instruction:
            payload["systemInstruction"] = system_instruction

        url = f"https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent?key={settings.GOOGLE_API_KEY}"
        data = _retry_post(url, {"Content-Type": "application/json"}, payload)

        candidates = data.get("candidates", [])
        text = ""
        if candidates:
            parts = candidates[0].get("content", {}).get("parts", [])
            text = "".join(p.get("text", "") for p in parts)

        usage_raw = data.get("usageMetadata", {})
        usage = {
            "prompt_tokens": usage_raw.get("promptTokenCount", 0),
            "completion_tokens": usage_raw.get("candidatesTokenCount", 0),
        }
        return text, usage

import time
import requests
from typing import Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from config.settings import settings
from llm.openrouter import OpenRouterClient
from models.schemas import ModelCallLog
from tools.scrubber import sanitize_messages
from tools.system_tier import CURRENT_TIER, HardwareTier


class AllModelsFailedError(Exception):
    """Raised when every model — static chain and dynamic fallback — has failed."""
    pass


class HardwareSpecDetector:
    """
    Detects system hardware tier for dashboard display and routing
    decisions. Delegates the actual tier calculation to
    tools/system_tier.py, which is the single source of truth for tier
    names used across this codebase (issue_scorer, security_agent,
    subagent_react, repomap, diff_explainer all import from there) —
    this class exists only as a stable, familiar interface for callers
    that expect it, not a second implementation.
    """

    @staticmethod
    def detect_tier() -> str:
        return CURRENT_TIER

    @staticmethod
    def get_system_ram_gb() -> float:
        try:
            import psutil
            return round(psutil.virtual_memory().total / (1024 ** 3), 2)
        except Exception:
            from tools.system_info import _read_ram_fallback
            return _read_ram_fallback()


class LocalOllamaClient:
    """
    OpenAI-compatible client for local Ollama execution. Uses requests
    (already a project dependency via OpenRouterClient) rather than
    urllib, for consistency with every other HTTP call in this codebase.
    """

    def __init__(self, base_url: str = None):
        self.base_url = base_url or __import__("os").getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:11434/v1")

    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        try:
            res = requests.get(f"{self.base_url.replace('/v1', '')}/api/tags", timeout=1.5)
            return res.status_code == 200
        except Exception:
            return False

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        payload = {"model": model, "messages": messages, "temperature": temperature}
        res = requests.post(f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions", json=payload, timeout=180)
        res.raise_for_status()
        data = res.json()
        content = data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
        usage = data.get("usage", {})
        return content, usage


class HuggingFaceDirectClient:
    """Direct Serverless Inference client for Hugging Face open models."""

    def __init__(self, api_key: Optional[str] = None):
        self.api_key = api_key or getattr(settings, "HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY", "") or __import__("os").getenv("HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY", "")

    def complete(self, model: str, messages: List[Dict[str, str]], temperature: float = 0.2) -> Tuple[str, Dict]:
        if not self.api_key:
            raise ValueError("HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY is not set.")

        prompt = "\n".join([f"{m['role'].upper()}: {m['content']}" for m in messages]) + "\nASSISTANT: "
        url = f"https://api-inference.huggingface.co/models/{model}"
        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {self.api_key}"}
        payload = {"inputs": prompt, "parameters": {"max_new_tokens": 2048, "temperature": max(0.01, temperature)}}
        res = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=120)
        res.raise_for_status()
        data = res.json()

        if isinstance(data, list) and len(data) > 0:
            generated_text = data[0].get("generated_text", "")
            if "ASSISTANT: " in generated_text:
                generated_text = generated_text.split("ASSISTANT: ", 1)[1]
            return generated_text, {"prompt_tokens": len(prompt.split()), "completion_tokens": len(generated_text.split())}

        raise ValueError(f"Unexpected HF response format: {data}")


class ModelRouter:
    """
    Tier-aware, credit-aware model router. Tries a configured chain of
    models per task; falls through to the next on failure or invalid
    output; can query OpenRouter's live catalog for further fallback
    candidates; supports local Ollama, Hugging Face, and direct
    provider (Anthropic/OpenAI/Google) routing alongside OpenRouter.

    'ModelRouter' is the canonical name used throughout this codebase —
    do not rename this class or alias it under a different name
    elsewhere (a prior version introduced an 'LLMRouter' alias in
    tools/llm_router.py; that file has been removed as unused/orphaned).
    """

    def __init__(self, task_chains: Optional[Dict[str, List[str]]] = None, client: Optional[OpenRouterClient] = None, mode: str = "openrouter"):
        if task_chains is None:
            try:
                from config.settings import settings
                task_chains = settings.get_task_chains() if hasattr(settings, "get_task_chains") else getattr(settings, "TASK_CHAINS", {})
            except Exception:
                task_chains = {
                    "planning": ["qwen2.5-coder:1.5b"],
                    "coding": ["qwen2.5-coder:1.5b"],
                    "debugging": ["qwen2.5-coder:1.5b"],
                }
        self.task_chains = task_chains
        self.openrouter_client = client or OpenRouterClient()
        self.local_client = LocalOllamaClient()
        self.hf_client = HuggingFaceDirectClient()
        self.mode = mode
        self.hardware_tier = HardwareSpecDetector.detect_tier()
        self.last_used_model: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.call_log: List[ModelCallLog] = []
        self._dynamic_model_cache: Optional[Tuple[float, list]] = None
        self._direct_clients = {}

    def set_mode(self, mode: str):
        """Modes: 'auto', 'local_llama', 'openrouter'."""
        self.mode = mode

    def get_account_balance(self) -> float:
        """Queries OpenRouter credits. Defaults safely to 0.0 on any
        error or missing data — never assumes abundance."""
        import os
        api_key = getattr(settings, "OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "") or os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "")
        if not api_key:
            return 0.0

        headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}"}
        try:
            res = requests.get("https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/credits", headers=headers, timeout=8)
            if res.status_code == 200:
                data = res.json().get("data", {})
                total_credits = float(data.get("total_credits", 0.0))
                total_usage = float(data.get("total_usage", 0.0))
                return max(0.0, total_credits - total_usage)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            res = requests.get("https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/key", headers=headers, timeout=8)
            if res.status_code == 200:
                data = res.json().get("data", {})
                limit = data.get("limit")
                usage = float(data.get("usage", 0.0))
                if limit is None or data.get("is_free_tier", False) is False:
                    return max(0.0, float(data.get("limit_remaining", 0.0)))
                return max(0.0, float(limit) - usage)
        except Exception:
            pass

        return 0.0

    def _resolve_client_and_model(self, model: str):
        if model.startswith("local/") or model.startswith("ollama/"):
            return self.local_client, model.split("/", 1)[1]

        if model.startswith("hf/") or model.startswith("huggingface/"):
            return self.hf_client, model.split("/", 1)[1]

        if model.startswith("anthropic-direct/"):
            if "anthropic" not in self._direct_clients:
                from llm.providers import AnthropicDirectClient
                self._direct_clients["anthropic"] = AnthropicDirectClient()
            return self._direct_clients["anthropic"], model.split("/", 1)[1]

        if model.startswith("openai-direct/"):
            if "openai" not in self._direct_clients:
                from llm.providers import OpenAIDirectClient
                self._direct_clients["openai"] = OpenAIDirectClient()
            return self._direct_clients["openai"], model.split("/", 1)[1]

        if model.startswith("google-direct/"):
            if "google" not in self._direct_clients:
                from llm.providers import GoogleDirectClient
                self._direct_clients["google"] = GoogleDirectClient()
            return self._direct_clients["google"], model.split("/", 1)[1]

        return self.openrouter_client, model

    def _get_chain_for_task(self, task: str, balance: float) -> List[str]:
        """Determines model priority chain based on mode, hardware tier, and balance."""
        chain = self.task_chains.get(task, [])
        ollama_ready = self.local_client.is_available()
        is_mason_task = any(k in task.lower() for k in ["code", "mason", "debug", "test"])

        # Explicit "local_llama" mode must route locally whenever Ollama
        # is reachable — this previously only checked for "auto" mode,
        # silently ignoring an explicit local_llama selection. Fixed.
        wants_local = self.mode in ("auto", "local_llama")

        if wants_local and ollama_ready:
            if self.hardware_tier == HardwareTier.TIER_HIGH:
                if is_mason_task:
                    return ["local/qwen2.5-coder:14b", "local/qwen2.5-coder:7b"] + chain
                return ["local/llama3.3:70b", "local/nous-hermes2:latest"] + chain
            elif self.hardware_tier == HardwareTier.TIER_MID and is_mason_task:
                return ["local/qwen2.5-coder:7b", "local/qwen2.5-coder:1.5b"] + chain
            elif self.mode == "local_llama":
                return ["local/qwen2.5-coder:1.5b"] + chain
        elif self.mode == "local_llama" and not ollama_ready:
            print("[Router] Local mode selected but Ollama is not reachable. Falling back to cloud...")

        if balance >= 1.00:
            return chain if chain else ["anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet", "openai/gpt-4o"]
        elif balance > 0.0:
            return [
                "qwen/qwen-2.5-coder-32b-instruct",
                "anthropic/claude-3.5-haiku",
                "openai/gpt-4o-mini",
                "deepseek/deepseek-chat",
            ]
        else:
            free_models = [m for m in chain if ":free" in m]
            if not free_models:
                free_models = ["qwen/qwen3-coder:free", "deepseek/deepseek-r1:free"]
            hf_key = getattr(settings, "HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY", "") or __import__("os").getenv("HUGGINGFACE_API_KEY", "")
            if hf_key:
                free_models.append("hf/Qwen/Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct")
            return free_models

    
    def _get_adapter_override(self, task_name: str) -> Optional[dict]:
        """Checks if a fine-tuned adapter exists for the task role."""
        try:
            from agent.training.adapter_registry import adapter_registry
            role_mapping = {
                "coding": "mason_backend_mason",
                "review": "reviewer",
                "testing": "tester",
            }
            target_role = role_mapping.get(task_name)
            if not target_role:
                return None

            active = adapter_registry.get_active_adapter(target_role)
            if active and "repo_id" in active.get("metadata", {}):
                return {
                    "model": active["metadata"]["repo_id"],
                    "provider": "huggingface",
                    "base_model": active.get("base_model"),
                }
        except Exception:
            pass
        return None

    
    def _detect_local_endpoint(self) -> Optional[dict]:
        """Detects whether Ollama or llama.cpp is listening on localhost."""
        import os, urllib.request

        custom_url = os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_URL")
        candidates = []
        if custom_url:
            candidates.append({"url": custom_url, "provider": "custom", "model": os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "default")})

        candidates.extend([
            {"url": "http://localhost:11434/v1/chat/completions", "provider": "ollama", "model": os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "llama3.2")},
            {"url": "http://127.0.0.1:11434/v1/chat/completions", "provider": "ollama", "model": os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "llama3.2")},
            {"url": "http://localhost:8080/v1/chat/completions", "provider": "llama.cpp", "model": os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "default")},
            {"url": "http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/chat/completions", "provider": "llama.cpp", "model": os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "default")},
        ])

        for c in candidates:
            try:
                base_health = c["url"].replace("/v1/chat/completions", "/v1/models")
                req = urllib.request.Request(base_health, headers={"User-Agent": "oss-agent"})
                with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=1.5) as resp:
                    if resp.status in (200, 204):
                        return c
            except Exception:
                continue
        return None

    def _call_local_server(self, messages: list, endpoint_info: dict) -> str:
        """Executes inference against local llama.cpp or Ollama OpenAI-compatible endpoint."""
        import json, urllib.request

        payload = {
            "model": endpoint_info.get("model", "llama3.2"),
            "messages": messages,
            "max_tokens": 2048,
            "temperature": 0.2,
            "stream": False
        }

        req = urllib.request.Request(
            endpoint_info["url"],
            data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}
        )

        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=180) as resp:
            data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
            return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]

    def complete_with_fallback(
        self,
        task: str,
        messages: List[Dict[str, str]],
        validate_fn: Optional[Callable[[str], bool]] = None,
        temperature: float = 0.2,
    ) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        chain = self.task_chains.get(task)
        if chain is None and task not in self.task_chains:
            raise ValueError(f"No model chain configured for task '{task}'")

        messages = sanitize_messages(messages)
        balance = self.get_account_balance()
        resolved_chain = self._get_chain_for_task(task, balance)

        errors = []
        attempted_models = set()

        for model in resolved_chain:
            attempted_models.add(model)
            result = self._try_model(task, model, messages, validate_fn, temperature, errors, was_dynamic=False)
            if result is not None:
                return result

        if settings.ENABLE_DYNAMIC_MODEL_FALLBACK or balance == 0.0:
            print(f"[Router] '{task}' — static chain exhausted, querying dynamic fallback...")
            try:
                candidates = self._get_dynamic_candidates(task, attempted_models, zero_cost_only=(balance == 0.0), max_budget=balance)
            except Exception as e:
                errors.append(f"dynamic discovery failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
                candidates = []

            for model in candidates:
                result = self._try_model(task, model, messages, validate_fn, temperature, errors, was_dynamic=True)
                if result is not None:
                    return result

                # Offline Fallback: Check for local Ollama / llama.cpp
        local_ep = self._detect_local_endpoint()
        if local_ep:
            try:
                print(f"[Router] Remote providers exhausted/offline. Trying local {local_ep['provider']} at {local_ep['url']}...")
                content = self._call_local_server(messages, local_ep)
                if validate_fn is None or validate_fn(content):
                    return content, f"local_{local_ep['provider']}"
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[Router] Local {local_ep['provider']} inference failed: {e}")
        raise AllModelsFailedError(f"All models failed for task '{task}' under tier {self.hardware_tier}:\n" + "\n".join(errors))

    def _try_model(self, task, model, messages, validate_fn, temperature, errors, was_dynamic) -> Optional[Tuple[str, str]]:
        tag = "[dynamic]" if was_dynamic else "[static]"
        try:
            client, resolved_model = self._resolve_client_and_model(model)
            result, usage = client.complete(resolved_model, messages, temperature=temperature)
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append(f"{model} {tag}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}")
            self.call_log.append(ModelCallLog(task=task, model_used=model, succeeded=False, error=str(e), was_dynamic=was_dynamic))
            print(f"[Router] '{task}' — {model} {tag} failed, trying next model...")
            return None

        if validate_fn and not validate_fn(result):
            errors.append(f"{model} {tag}: output failed validation")
            self.call_log.append(ModelCallLog(
                task=task, model_used=model, succeeded=False, error="output_failed_validation",
                prompt_tokens=usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0), completion_tokens=usage.get("completion_tokens", 0),
                was_dynamic=was_dynamic,
            ))
            print(f"[Router] '{task}' — {model} {tag} returned invalid output, trying next model...")
            return None

        self.last_used_model[task] = model
        self.call_log.append(ModelCallLog(
            task=task, model_used=model, succeeded=True,
            prompt_tokens=usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0), completion_tokens=usage.get("completion_tokens", 0),
            was_dynamic=was_dynamic,
        ))
        if was_dynamic:
            print(f"[Router] '{task}' — succeeded via dynamic fallback: {model}")
        return result, model

    def _get_dynamic_candidates(self, task: str, attempted_models: set, zero_cost_only: bool = False, max_budget: float = 0.0) -> List[str]:
        models = self._fetch_model_catalog()
        candidates = []
        for m in models:
            model_id = m.get("id")
            if not model_id or model_id in attempted_models:
                continue
            pricing = m.get("pricing", {}) or {}
            try:
                p_price = float(pricing.get("prompt", "999"))
                c_price = float(pricing.get("completion", "999"))
            except (TypeError, ValueError):
                continue
            if zero_cost_only:
                if p_price != 0.0 or c_price != 0.0:
                    continue
            else:
                price_per_m = p_price * 1_000_000
                if price_per_m > settings.MAX_DYNAMIC_MODEL_PRICE_PER_MILLION:
                    continue
                if max_budget < 0.50 and price_per_m > 2.0:
                    continue
            if (m.get("context_length", 0) or 0) < 4000:
                continue
            candidates.append(model_id)
            if len(candidates) >= 5:
                break
        return candidates

    def _fetch_model_catalog(self) -> list:
        import os
        now = time.time()
        if self._dynamic_model_cache is not None:
            fetched_at, cached_models = self._dynamic_model_cache
            if now - fetched_at < getattr(settings, "DYNAMIC_MODEL_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS", 300):
                return cached_models

        headers = {}
        api_key = getattr(settings, "OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "") or os.getenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "")
        if api_key:
            headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {api_key}"

        res = requests.get("https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/models", headers=headers, timeout=15)
        res.raise_for_status()
        models = res.json().get("data", [])
        self._dynamic_model_cache = (now, models)
        return models
       
# Both names are canonical in this codebase — ModelRouter is used by
# RunLogger/orchestrator's original design, LLMRouter is used by
# reviewer_agent, security_agent, chat_interface, and subagent_react.
# Rather than pick one and edit every call site, both names point at
# the exact same class.
LLMRouter = ModelRouter

--- START FILE: llm/validators.py ---
import json
import re


def is_valid_json_object(text: str) -> bool:
    """Used for planning/review outputs, which must be parseable JSON."""
    match = re.search(r"\{.*\}", text, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        return False
    try:
        json.loads(match.group(0))
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False


def is_non_empty_code_block(text: str) -> bool:
    """Used for coding/debug outputs, which must contain a real code block."""
    match = re.search(r"```(?:\w+)?\n(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)
    if not match:
        return False
    return len(match.group(1).strip()) > 0

--- END FILE: llm/validators.py ---

--- START FILE: main.py ---
import time
import click
from config.settings import validate_settings, settings
from agent.orchestrator import Orchestrator
from tools.environment_setup import EnvironmentBootstrapper
from tools.dashboard import dashboard, generate_dashboard


@click.command()
@click.option("--query", default=None, help="GitHub repo search query, e.g. 'language:python stars:>500'")
@click.option("--repo", default=None, help="Target a specific repo directly, e.g. 'psf/requests'")
@click.option("--issue", default=None, type=int, help="Target a specific issue number directly (requires --repo)")
@click.option("--dry-run", is_flag=True, default=False, help="Run the full pipeline but stop before any push")
@click.option("--server-only", is_flag=True, default=False, help="Launch the interactive UI companion workstation without running CLI tasks")
def run(query, repo, issue, dry_run, server_only):
    """OSS-Agent — Autonomous Multi-Agent Engineering Workstation."""
    # 1. Start live non-blocking Glass-Box Dashboard server
    dashboard.start()
    dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "IDLE"})
    dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": "OSS-Agent companion initialized and standing by."})

    validate_settings()
    settings.validate_task_chains()
    # EnvironmentBootstrapper().bootstrap_environment()

    # Generate static report
    try:
        generate_dashboard(settings.LOGS_DIR, settings.LOGS_DIR / "dashboard.html")
    except Exception:
        pass

    orchestrator = Orchestrator(dry_run=dry_run)

    # 2. Interactive Companion Workstation Mode (Default when no CLI targets are passed)
    if server_only or (not repo and not query and not issue):
        print("\n" + "=" * 62)
        print("🤖 OSS-AGENT INTERACTIVE COMPANION WORKSTATION ACTIVE")
        print(f"👉 UI Dashboard: http://localhost:{dashboard.port}")
        print("=" * 62)
        print("[Ready] Type instructions directly into the Web UI chat box.")
        print("Press Ctrl+C in this terminal to shut down.")
        try:
            while True:
                time.sleep(1)
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "IDLE"})
            print("\nShutting down OSS-Agent Companion.")
            return

    # 3. Headless GitHub Issue Resolution Mode
    if issue and not repo:
        raise click.UsageError("--issue requires --repo to also be set (e.g. --repo owner/name --issue 123)")

    dashboard.emit("STATE_CHANGE", {"state": "SCANNING_AST"})
    dashboard.emit("LOG_STREAM", {"message": f"Starting orchestrator pipeline (Repo: {repo or 'Search Query'})"})

    orchestrator.run(search_query=query, target_repo=repo, target_issue=issue)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

--- END FILE: main.py ---

from typing import List, Optional
from pydantic import BaseModel
from datetime import datetime

class RepositoryInfo(BaseModel):
    owner: str
    name: str
    full_name: str
    clone_url: str
    ssh_url: str
    default_branch: str
    description: Optional[str] = ""
    stargazers_count: int = 0
    language: Optional[str] = ""


class IssueInfo(BaseModel):
    number: int
    title: str
    body: str
    html_url: str
    repository_full_name: str
    labels: List[str] = []


class ImplementationPlan(BaseModel):
    summary: str
    files_to_modify: List[str] = []
    steps: List[str] = []

    def get(self, key: str, default=None):
        return getattr(self, key, default)

    def __getitem__(self, key: str):
        return getattr(self, key)


class ReviewResult(BaseModel):
    approved: bool
    feedback: str
    suggested_changes: Optional[str] = None
 

class ModelCallLog(BaseModel):
    task: str
    model_used: str
    prompt_tokens: int = 0
    completion_tokens: int = 0
    succeeded: bool = True
    error: Optional[str] = None
    was_dynamic: bool = False

class RunLog(BaseModel):
    run_id: str
    started_at: str
    finished_at: Optional[str] = None
    repository: Optional[str] = None
    issue_number: Optional[int] = None
    issue_title: Optional[str] = None
    outcome: str = "in_progress"  # in_progress | pr_created | pushed_no_pr | aborted | failed
    dry_run: bool = False
    model_calls: List[ModelCallLog] = []
    test_pass: Optional[bool] = None
    review_approved: Optional[bool] = None
    debug_retries_used: int = 0
    notes: List[str] = []

--- START FILE: repository/context.py ---
from pathlib import Path
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem
from repository.scanner import LocalScanner
from repository.relevance import RelevanceEngine


class RepositoryContext:
    def __init__(self, workspace_path: Path):
        self.fs = SafeFileSystem(workspace_path)
        self.scanner = LocalScanner(self.fs)
        self.relevance = RelevanceEngine(self.fs)

    def assemble_context(self, issue_title: str = "", issue_body: str = "") -> str:
        docs = self.scanner.get_core_docs()
        structure = self.scanner.scan_project_structure()
        ctx = [f"=== Project structure ===\n{structure}\n"]
        for name, content in docs.items():
            ctx.append(f"=== File: {name} ===\n{content}\n")

        if issue_title or issue_body:
            relevant = self.relevance.build_relevant_context(issue_title, issue_body)
            if relevant:
                ctx.append(relevant)

        return "\n".join(ctx)

--- END FILE: repository/context.py ---

--- START FILE: repository/relevance.py ---
import re
from typing import List, Dict
from tools.search import CodeSearcher
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem


class RelevanceEngine:
    """
    Finds files in the repo most likely relevant to an issue, using
    keyword search — not a full AST/semantic index, but enough to avoid
    sending the entire repo's context to every LLM call. Extracts
    candidate keywords from the issue title/body (error messages,
    quoted identifiers, function-like names) rather than requiring the
    caller to supply them manually.
    """

    def __init__(self, fs: SafeFileSystem):
        self.fs = fs
        self.searcher = CodeSearcher(fs)

    def extract_keywords(self, issue_title: str, issue_body: str) -> List[str]:
        text = f"{issue_title}\n{issue_body}"
        keywords = set()

        # Quoted strings/identifiers: `some_function`, "SomeClass", 'ERROR_CODE'
        for match in re.findall(r"[`'\"]([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{2,})[`'\"]", text):
            keywords.add(match)

        # CamelCase or snake_case tokens that look like real symbols, not prose
        for match in re.findall(r"\b([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{3,})\b", text):
            if "_" in match or (match[0].isupper() and any(c.islower() for c in match)):
                keywords.add(match)

        # Cap to avoid an excessive number of searches on a long issue body
        return list(keywords)[:10]

    def find_relevant_files(self, issue_title: str, issue_body: str, max_files: int = 5) -> List[str]:
        keywords = self.extract_keywords(issue_title, issue_body)
        if not keywords:
            return []

        matched: Dict[str, int] = {}  # file_path -> number of keyword hits, used to rank
        for kw in keywords:
            hits = self.searcher.search_symbol_or_text(kw, max_results=10)
            for hit in hits:
                file_path = hit["file"]
                if file_path.startswith(".git/"):
                    continue
                matched[file_path] = matched.get(file_path, 0) + 1

        ranked = sorted(matched.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
        return [path for path, _ in ranked[:max_files]]

    def build_relevant_context(self, issue_title: str, issue_body: str, max_files: int = 5,
                                max_chars_per_file: int = 1500) -> str:
        """
        Returns a formatted context string containing the most relevant
        files' content, capped per-file to control prompt size. Empty
        string if nothing relevant was found (caller should fall back to
        generic README/doc context in that case).
        """
        relevant_files = self.find_relevant_files(issue_title, issue_body, max_files)
        if not relevant_files:
            return ""

        sections = []
        for file_path in relevant_files:
            try:
                content = self.fs.read_file(file_path)
            except Exception:
                continue
            truncated = content[:max_chars_per_file]
            suffix = "\n... (truncated)" if len(content) > max_chars_per_file else ""
            sections.append(f"--- Relevant file: {file_path} ---\n{truncated}{suffix}")

        if not sections:
            return ""

        return "\n\n=== Files most relevant to this issue ===\n" + "\n\n".join(sections) + "\n"

--- END FILE: repository/relevance.py ---

--- START FILE: repository/scanner.py ---
from typing import Dict
from tools.filesystem import SafeFileSystem


class LocalScanner:
    def __init__(self, fs: SafeFileSystem):
        self.fs = fs

    def get_core_docs(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        docs = {}
        for t in ["README.md", "CONTRIBUTING.md", "setup.py", "pyproject.toml", "package.json"]:
            try:
                docs[t] = self.fs.read_file(t)[:3000]
            except Exception:
                continue
        return docs

    def scan_project_structure(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        files = self.fs.list_files(".")
        top_level = sorted(set(f.split("/")[0] for f in files))
        return {"file_count": str(len(files)), "top_level_entries": ", ".join(top_level)}

--- END FILE: repository/scanner.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_pc/cli.py ---
import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from filesystem_driver import FilesystemDriver
from local_client import LocalLLMClient
from system_driver import SystemDriver

SENTINEL_PC_PROMPT = """You are Sentinel-PC, a private, local-first PC/Laptop system automation agent.
MANDATES:
- You operate strictly locally. All data stays in process RAM (zero persistent logging).
- Return ONLY a single JSON object corresponding to the requested action.

ACTIONS SCHEMA:
1. Run terminal shell command:
   {"action": "run_shell", "command": "<shell_command>"}
2. Read file:
   {"action": "read_file", "path": "<relative_or_abs_path>"}
3. Write file:
   {"action": "write_file", "path": "<file_path>", "content": "<file_content>"}
4. Search files:
   {"action": "search_files", "pattern": "<glob_pattern>", "subpath": "<optional_subpath>"}
5. List processes:
   {"action": "list_processes", "filter": "<optional_name_filter>"}
6. Check hardware specs:
   {"action": "check_specs"}
7. Respond to user:
   {"action": "respond", "message": "<msg>"}
"""


def extract_json(text: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    m = re.search(r"```(?:json)?\s*(\{.*?\})\s*```", text, re.DOTALL) or re.search(r"\{.*?\}", text, re.DOTALL)
    if m:
        try:
            return json.loads(m.group(1) if "```" in text else m.group(0))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Sentinel-PC Local Laptop Runner")
    parser.add_argument("--root", default=".", help="Root workspace directory")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    system_driver = SystemDriver(workspace_root=args.root)
    fs_driver = FilesystemDriver(root_path=args.root)
    llm = LocalLLMClient()

    history: List[Dict[str, str]] = [{"role": "system", "content": SENTINEL_PC_PROMPT}]

    print("=" * 60)
    print("        SENTINEL-PC: LOCAL LAPTOP WORKSTATION AGENT")
    print("=" * 60)
    print("• Retention: Ephemeral RAM-only (No telemetry/cloud logging)")
    print(f"• Root Workspace: {fs_driver.root_path}\n")

    while True:
        try:
            query = input("pc-agent> ").strip()
        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
            print("\nShutting down. Session memory purged.")
            break

        if not query or query.lower() in ("exit", "quit"):
            break
        if query.lower() == "clear":
            history = [{"role": "system", "content": SENTINEL_PC_PROMPT}]
            print("[Context Cleared]\n")
            continue

        history.append({"role": "user", "content": query})

        try:
            raw = llm.query(history)
            act = extract_json(raw)

            if not act or "action" not in act:
                print(f"\n[Agent]: {raw}\n")
                history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": raw})
                continue

            action = act["action"]

            if action == "respond":
                print(f"\n[Agent]: {act.get('message', '')}\n")
                history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": json.dumps(act)})

            elif action == "run_shell":
                cmd = act.get("command", "")
                print(f"[*] Executing shell: {cmd}")
                res = system_driver.run_command(cmd)
                out = res["stdout"] if res["success"] else f"ERR: {res['stderr']}"
                print(f"[*] Output:\n{out}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {out}"})

            elif action == "read_file":
                path = act.get("path", "")
                res = fs_driver.read_file(path)
                out = res.get("content", f"ERR: {res.get('error')}")
                print(f"[*] Read {path} ({len(out)} chars)\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {out}"})

            elif action == "write_file":
                path = act.get("path", "")
                content = act.get("content", "")
                res = fs_driver.write_file(path, content)
                out = f"Wrote {res.get('bytes_written')} bytes to {path}" if res["success"] else f"ERR: {res.get('error')}"
                print(f"[*] {out}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {out}"})

            elif action == "search_files":
                pattern = act.get("pattern", "*")
                subpath = act.get("subpath", "")
                matches = fs_driver.search_files(pattern, subpath)
                out = f"Found {len(matches)} matches: " + ", ".join(matches[:10])
                print(f"[*] {out}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {out}"})

            elif action == "list_processes":
                filt = act.get("filter")
                procs = system_driver.list_processes(filt)
                out = f"Active processes: {len(procs)} found"
                print(f"[*] {out}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {procs}"})

            elif action == "check_specs":
                specs = system_driver.get_system_specs()
                out = f"OS: {specs['os']} ({specs['cpu_arch']}) | RAM: {specs['total_ram_mb']}MB | Disk Free: {specs['free_storage_mb']}MB"
                print(f"[*] {out}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {out}"})

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Error] {e}\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_pc/cli.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_pc/filesystem_driver.py ---
import fnmatch
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

RESTRICTED_DIRECTORIES = {".ssh", ".gnupg", "/etc/shadow", "/dev"}


class FilesystemDriver:
    """Manages file indexing, reading, writing, and directory searching."""

    def __init__(self, root_path: Optional[str] = None):
        self.root_path = Path(root_path or Path.home()).resolve()

    def _is_restricted(self, target: Path) -> bool:
        resolved = str(target.resolve())
        return any(blocked in resolved for blocked in RESTRICTED_DIRECTORIES)

    def read_file(self, file_path: str, max_bytes: int = 100_000) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        target = (self.root_path / file_path).resolve()
        if self._is_restricted(target):
            return {"success": False, "error": "Access denied: Restricted system path."}
        if not target.exists() or not target.is_file():
            return {"success": False, "error": f"File does not exist: {file_path}"}

        try:
            content = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")[:max_bytes]
            return {"success": True, "content": content, "truncated": len(content) >= max_bytes}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}

    def write_file(self, file_path: str, content: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        target = (self.root_path / file_path).resolve()
        if self._is_restricted(target):
            return {"success": False, "error": "Access denied: Restricted system path."}

        try:
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            target.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
            return {"success": True, "path": str(target), "bytes_written": len(content)}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"success": False, "error": str(e)}

    def search_files(self, pattern: str, root_subpath: str = "", max_results: int = 25) -> List[str]:
        search_root = (self.root_path / root_subpath).resolve()
        matches = []
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(search_root):
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if not d.startswith(".") and d not in RESTRICTED_DIRECTORIES]
            for filename in files:
                if fnmatch.fnmatch(filename, pattern):
                    rel = Path(root, filename).relative_to(self.root_path)
                    matches.append(str(rel))
                    if len(matches) >= max_results:
                        return matches
        return matches

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_pc/filesystem_driver.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_pc/local_client.py ---
import json
import os
import urllib.request
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional


class LocalLLMClient:
    """Air-gapped HTTP client targeting local Ollama or llama.cpp OpenAI-compatible endpoints."""

    def __init__(self, endpoint_url: Optional[str] = None, model: Optional[str] = None):
        self.endpoint_url = endpoint_url or os.environ.get(
            "LOCAL_LLM_URL", "http://localhost:11434/v1/chat/completions"
        )
        self.model = model or os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "llama3.2")

    def query(self, messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> str:
        payload = {
            "model": self.model,
            "messages": messages,
            "temperature": 0.1,
            "stream": False,
        }
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            self.endpoint_url,
            data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=90) as resp:
                data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                return data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()
        except urllib.error.URLError as e:
            raise ConnectionError(
                f"Cannot reach local inference server at {self.endpoint_url}. "
                "Ensure Ollama (`ollama run llama3.2`) or llama-server is active."
            ) from e

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_pc/system_driver.py ---
import os
import platform
import shutil
import subprocess
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

DANGEROUS_COMMAND_PATTERNS = [
    "rm -rf /",
    "mkfs",
    ":(){ :|:& };:",
    "> /dev/sda",
    "dd if=",
    "shutdown",
    "reboot",
]


class SystemDriver:
    """Manages local OS command execution, process monitoring, and system metrics."""

    def __init__(self, workspace_root: Optional[str] = None):
        self.workspace_root = os.path.abspath(workspace_root or os.getcwd())

    def get_system_specs(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Audits CPU, RAM, disk headroom, and OS details."""
        total_ram_mb = 0
        try:
            if os.path.exists("/proc/meminfo"):
                with open("/proc/meminfo", "r") as f:
                    for line in f:
                        if line.startswith("MemTotal:"):
                            total_ram_mb = int(line.split()[1]) // 1024
                            break
        except Exception:
            pass

        total_storage_mb, free_storage_mb = 0, 0
        try:
            total, used, free = shutil.disk_usage(self.workspace_root)
            total_storage_mb = total // (1024 * 1024)
            free_storage_mb = free // (1024 * 1024)
        except Exception:
            pass

        return {
            "os": platform.system(),
            "os_release": platform.release(),
            "cpu_arch": platform.machine(),
            "total_ram_mb": total_ram_mb,
            "free_storage_mb": free_storage_mb,
            "total_storage_mb": total_storage_mb,
        }

    def run_command(self, cmd: str, timeout: int = 45) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Runs a shell command and captures returncode, stdout, and stderr."""
        for pattern in DANGEROUS_COMMAND_PATTERNS:
            if pattern in cmd:
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "returncode": -1,
                    "stdout": "",
                    "stderr": f"BLOCKED: Command contains destructive pattern '{pattern}'",
                }

        try:
            res = subprocess.run(
                cmd,
                shell=True,
                cwd=self.workspace_root,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
                timeout=timeout,
            )
            return {
                "success": res.returncode == 0,
                "returncode": res.returncode,
                "stdout": res.stdout.strip(),
                "stderr": res.stderr.strip(),
            }
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "returncode": -1,
                "stdout": "",
                "stderr": f"Command timed out after {timeout} seconds.",
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {"success": False, "returncode": -1, "stdout": "", "stderr": str(e)}

    def list_processes(self, filter_name: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Queries running processes using standard ps utility."""
        res = subprocess.run(["ps", "-eo", "pid,comm,%cpu,%mem"], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if res.returncode != 0:
            return []

        lines = res.stdout.strip().splitlines()
        processes = []
        for line in lines[1:]:
            parts = line.split(None, 3)
            if len(parts) == 4:
                pid, comm, cpu, mem = parts
                if filter_name and filter_name.lower() not in comm.lower():
                    continue
                processes.append({"pid": pid, "command": comm, "cpu_pct": cpu, "mem_pct": mem})
        return processes[:25]

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_pc/system_driver.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/cli.py ---
import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import urllib.request
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from device_controller import EphemeralPersonalAgent

SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are Sentinel-Personal, a private local Android agent.
ZERO RETENTION: Volatile memory only. All private screen text is structurally stripped.
ACTIONS (JSON only):
{"action": "unlock", "pin": "<PIN>"}
{"action": "launch_app", "package": "<package_name>"}
{"action": "read_notifications"}
{"action": "inspect_screen"}
{"action": "tap_control", "label": "<label>"}
{"action": "tap", "x": <int>, "y": <int>}
{"action": "check_specs", "app_target": "<optional_app_name>"}
{"action": "audit_file", "file_path": "<path_to_file>"}
{"action": "setup_game", "game_name": "mario"}
{"action": "respond", "message": "<msg>"}
"""

class LocalLLMClient:
    def __init__(self, endpoint_url: Optional[str] = None, model: Optional[str] = None):
        self.endpoint_url = endpoint_url or os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_URL", "http://localhost:11434/v1/chat/completions")
        self.model = model or os.environ.get("LOCAL_LLM_MODEL", "llama3.2")

    def query(self, messages: List[Dict[str, str]]) -> str:
        payload = {"model": self.model, "messages": messages, "temperature": 0.1, "stream": False}
        req = urllib.request.Request(self.endpoint_url, data=json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8"), headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as resp:
            return json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))["choices"][0]["message"]["content"].strip()

def extract_json(text: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    m = re.search(r"```(?:json)?\s*(\{.*?\})\s*```", text, re.DOTALL) or re.search(r"\{.*?\}", text, re.DOTALL)
    if m:
        try:
            return json.loads(m.group(1) if "```" in text else m.group(0))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return None

def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Sentinel-Personal Standalone Runner")
    parser.add_argument("--adb-host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--adb-port", type=int, default=5555)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    agent = EphemeralPersonalAgent(adb_host=args.adb_host, adb_port=args.adb_port)
    llm = LocalLLMClient()
    history = [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT}]

    print("=" * 55)
    print("      SENTINEL-PERSONAL (STANDALONE AIR-GAPPED)")
    print("=" * 55)
    print("• Retention: Ephemeral RAM-only (No disk logs)")
    print("• Privacy: Structural field allow-listing\n")

    while True:
        try:
            query = input("personal> ").strip()
        except (KeyboardInterrupt, EOFError):
            agent.clear_memory()
            break
        if not query or query.lower() in ("exit", "quit"):
            agent.clear_memory()
            break
        if query.lower() == "clear":
            history = [{"role": "system", "content": SYSTEM_PROMPT}]
            agent.clear_memory()
            print("[Memory Cleared]")
            continue

        history.append({"role": "user", "content": query})
        try:
            raw = llm.query(history)
            act = extract_json(raw)
            if not act or "action" not in act:
                print(f"\n[Agent]: {raw}\n")
                history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": raw})
                continue

            action = act["action"]
            if action == "respond":
                print(f"\n[Agent]: {act.get('message', '')}\n")
            elif action == "unlock":
                ok = agent.unlock_device(act.get("pin", ""))
                res = "Device unlocked." if ok else "Unlock failed."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})
            elif action == "launch_app":
                ok = agent.launch_app(act.get("package", ""))
                res = f"Launched {act.get('package')}." if ok else "Launch failed."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})
            elif action == "inspect_screen":
                els = agent.get_screen_elements()
                preview = [f"{e['class']} (label: {e['control_label']}, center: {e['center']})" for e in els[:5]]
                summary = f"Found {len(els)} controls:\n" + "\n".join(preview)
                print(f"\n{summary}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {summary}"})
            elif action == "tap_control":
                ok = agent.tap_control(act.get("label", ""))
                res = f"Tapped {act.get('label')}." if ok else "Control not found."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})
            elif action == "tap":
                ok = agent.tap_coordinates(act.get("x", 0), act.get("y", 0))
                res = f"Tapped ({act.get('x')}, {act.get('y')})." if ok else "Tap failed."
                print(f"[*] {res}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})
            elif action == "read_notifications":
                notes = agent.get_unread_notifications()
                res = f"Found {len(notes)} notifications."
                print(f"\n{res}\n")
                history.append({"role": "user", "content": f"[Result]: {res}"})
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[Error] {e}\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/cli.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/device_controller.py ---
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from privacy_guard import StructuralPrivacyGuard
from system_auditor import SystemAuditor
from safety_scanner import SafetyScanner
from game_runner import GameRunner


class EphemeralPersonalAgent:
    """
    Zero-retention personal device agent with built-in hardware auditing,
    threat scanning, and autonomous game/dependency execution.
    """

    def __init__(self, adb_host: str = "127.0.0.1", adb_port: int = 5555):
        self.adb_target = f"{adb_host}:{adb_port}"
        self._session_context: List[Dict[str, str]] = []
        self._cached_dimensions: Optional[Tuple[int, int]] = None
        self.guard = StructuralPrivacyGuard()
        self.auditor = SystemAuditor(adb_target=self.adb_target)
        self.scanner = SafetyScanner()
        self.runner = GameRunner()

    def _run_adb(self, cmd: List[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        full_cmd = ["adb", "-s", self.adb_target] + cmd
        return subprocess.run(full_cmd, text=True, capture_output=True)

    def check_system_spec(self, target_app: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Audits specs against target app requirements (e.g. freefire, mario)."""
        specs = self.auditor.get_hardware_specs()
        if target_app:
            compatible, reason = self.auditor.verify_app_compatibility(target_app)
            specs["target_app"] = target_app
            specs["compatible"] = compatible
            specs["assessment"] = reason
        return specs

    def audit_download(self, file_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Scans downloaded APK or file for security risks before opening/installing."""
        return self.scanner.audit_apk_file(Path(file_path))

    def setup_and_play_game(self, game_name: str, steps: int = 500) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Prepares dependencies and executes game sessions (e.g. mario)."""
        if "mario" in game_name.lower():
            return self.runner.launch_mario_session(steps=steps)
        return {"status": "FAILED", "error": f"Game engine for '{game_name}' not supported yet."}

    def unlock_device(self, pin: str) -> bool:
        self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "keyevent", "26"])
        self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "swipe", "500", "1500", "500", "500", "250"])
        self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "text", pin])
        self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "keyevent", "66"])
        return True

    def launch_app(self, package_name: str, activity_name: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
        if activity_name:
            cmd = ["shell", "am", "start", "-n", f"{package_name}/{activity_name}"]
        else:
            cmd = ["shell", "monkey", "-p", package_name, "-c", "android.intent.category.LAUNCHER", "1"]
        return self._run_adb(cmd).returncode == 0

    def get_screen_elements(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        dump_path = "/sdcard/window_dump.xml"
        self._run_adb(["shell", "uiautomator", "dump", dump_path])
        cat_res = self._run_adb(["shell", "cat", dump_path])
        self._run_adb(["shell", "rm", "-f", dump_path])
        return self.guard.filter_ui_hierarchy(cat_res.stdout)

    def tap_coordinates(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:
        return self._run_adb(["shell", "input", "tap", str(x), str(y)]).returncode == 0

    def get_unread_notifications(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        res = subprocess.run(["termux-notification-list"], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if res.returncode != 0 or not res.stdout.strip():
            return []
        try:
            return self.guard.filter_notifications(json.loads(res.stdout))
        except Exception:
            return []

    def clear_memory(self):
        self._session_context.clear()

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/device_controller.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/game_runner.py ---
import importlib.util
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict


class GameRunner:
    """Manages emulator dependencies, assets, and execution for games like Super Mario."""

    @staticmethod
    def ensure_mario_dependencies() -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verifies and automatically installs NES emulator dependencies."""
        required_packages = ["gym_super_mario_bros", "nes_py"]
        installed = []
        missing = []

        for pkg in required_packages:
            if importlib.util.find_spec(pkg) is not None:
                installed.append(pkg)
            else:
                missing.append(pkg)

        if not missing:
            return {"status": "READY", "installed": installed}

        print(f"[GameRunner] Installing missing emulator packages: {missing}...")
        try:
            # Install packages into the local Python environment
            subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install"] + missing,
                check=True,
                capture_output=True,
                text=True,
            )
            return {"status": "INSTALLED", "packages": missing}
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
            return {"status": "FAILED", "error": e.stderr}

    @staticmethod
    def launch_mario_session(steps: int = 500, headless: bool = True) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Launches a Mario execution session."""
        dep_status = GameRunner.ensure_mario_dependencies()
        if dep_status["status"] == "FAILED":
            return dep_status

        # Launch script executing steps in the Mario NES environment
        run_code = f"""
import gym_super_mario_bros
from nes_py.wrappers import JoypadSpace
from gym_super_mario_bros.actions import SIMPLE_MOVEMENT

env = gym_super_mario_bros.make('SuperMarioBros-v0')
env = JoypadSpace(env, SIMPLE_MOVEMENT)

state = env.reset()
total_reward = 0
for step in range({steps}):
    action = env.action_space.sample()
    res = env.step(action)
    state, reward, done = res[0], res[1], res[2]
    total_reward += reward
    if done:
        break
env.close()
print(f"COMPLETED: Steps={steps}, FinalReward={{total_reward}}")
"""
        res = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", run_code], capture_output=True, text=True)
        if res.returncode == 0:
            return {"status": "SUCCESS", "output": res.stdout.strip()}
        return {"status": "FAILED", "error": res.stderr.strip()}

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/game_runner.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/privacy_guard.py ---
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Set

SAFE_CONTROL_LABELS: Set[str] = {
    "ok", "cancel", "submit", "next", "back", "done", "search", "settings",
    "save", "delete", "open", "close", "yes", "no", "continue", "confirm",
    "home", "menu", "send", "allow", "deny", "install", "update"
}
ALLOWED_NOTIFICATION_KEYS: Set[str] = {"id", "packageName", "postTime", "tag"}


class StructuralPrivacyGuard:
    """Enforces schema-driven allow-listing. Drops raw screen text and PII."""

    @staticmethod
    def filter_ui_hierarchy(raw_xml: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        if not raw_xml or not raw_xml.strip():
            return []
        try:
            root = ET.fromstring(raw_xml)
        except Exception:
            return []

        interactive_nodes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for node in root.iter("node"):
            attrib = node.attrib
            clickable = attrib.get("clickable") == "true"
            checkable = attrib.get("checkable") == "true"
            resource_id = attrib.get("resource-id", "")
            class_name = attrib.get("class", "").split(".")[-1]
            bounds = attrib.get("bounds", "")
            raw_text = attrib.get("text", "").strip()

            if clickable or checkable or "button" in class_name.lower() or "edittext" in class_name.lower():
                safe_text = raw_text if raw_text.lower() in SAFE_CONTROL_LABELS else "[CONTENT_HIDDEN]"
                center_coords = None
                coords = re.findall(r"\[(\d+),(\d+)\]", bounds)
                if len(coords) == 2:
                    x1, y1 = int(coords[0][0]), int(coords[0][1])
                    x2, y2 = int(coords[1][0]), int(coords[1][1])
                    center_coords = {"x": (x1 + x2) // 2, "y": (y1 + y2) // 2}

                interactive_nodes.append({
                    "class": class_name,
                    "resource_id": resource_id if resource_id else "none",
                    "control_label": safe_text,
                    "center": center_coords,
                    "clickable": clickable,
                })
        return interactive_nodes

    @staticmethod
    def filter_notifications(raw_notifications: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        sanitized = []
        for item in raw_notifications:
            filtered_item = {k: item[k] for k in ALLOWED_NOTIFICATION_KEYS if k in item}
            filtered_item["title"] = "[NOTIFICATION_RECEIVED]"
            sanitized.append(filtered_item)
        return sanitized

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/privacy_guard.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/safety_scanner.py ---
import os
import subprocess
import zipfile
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List

HIGH_RISK_PERMISSIONS = {
    "android.permission.BIND_ACCESSIBILITY_SERVICE": "Can intercept, monitor, and control screen interactions.",
    "android.permission.RECEIVE_SMS": "Can intercept 2FA verification codes and private text messages.",
    "android.permission.READ_SMS": "Can read private SMS communications.",
    "android.permission.READ_CALL_LOG": "Can monitor incoming and outgoing call records.",
    "android.permission.REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES": "Can silently download and install secondary malicious packages.",
    "android.permission.SYSTEM_ALERT_WINDOW": "Can draw invisible overlays over banking and login screens.",
}


class SafetyScanner:
    """Inspects downloaded artifacts and APK files for security threats and excessive permissions."""

    @staticmethod
    def audit_apk_file(apk_path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        if not apk_path.exists():
            return {"safe": False, "error": f"File not found: {apk_path}"}

        # 1. Check basic file integrity
        if not zipfile.is_zipfile(apk_path):
            return {"safe": False, "error": "Corrupt file: Not a valid Android package archive."}

        permissions_found: List[str] = []
        flagged_risks: Dict[str, str] = {}

        # 2. Try inspection via aapt/aapt2 if available on system
        try:
            res = subprocess.run(["aapt", "dump", "permissions", str(apk_path)], capture_output=True, text=True)
            if res.returncode == 0 and res.stdout:
                for line in res.stdout.splitlines():
                    if "uses-permission:" in line or "permission:" in line:
                        perm = line.split("name=")[-1].strip("'\" \t\r\n")
                        permissions_found.append(perm)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 3. Fallback: inspect raw binary AndroidManifest.xml strings
        if not permissions_found:
            try:
                with zipfile.ZipFile(apk_path, "r") as z:
                    manifest_data = z.read("AndroidManifest.xml")
                    for high_perm in HIGH_RISK_PERMISSIONS:
                        if high_perm.encode("utf-8") in manifest_data:
                            permissions_found.append(high_perm)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # 4. Evaluate against known threat vectors
        for p in permissions_found:
            for risk_perm, reason in HIGH_RISK_PERMISSIONS.items():
                if risk_perm.lower() in p.lower():
                    flagged_risks[risk_perm] = reason

        is_safe = len(flagged_risks) == 0
        return {
            "safe": is_safe,
            "permissions_detected": permissions_found,
            "high_risk_flags": flagged_risks,
            "verdict": "CLEAN" if is_safe else "HIGH_RISK_WARNING",
        }

--- END FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/safety_scanner.py ---

--- START FILE: templates/sentinel_personal/system_auditor.py ---
import os
import platform
import shutil
import subprocess
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional, Tuple

KNOWN_APP_REQUIREMENTS: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {
    "freefire": {
        "name": "Garena Free Fire",
        "min_storage_mb": 3500,
        "min_ram_mb": 2048,
        "supported_abi": ["arm64-v8a", "armeabi-v7a"],
        "target_platform": "android",
    },
    "mario": {
        "name": "Super Mario Bros (NES)",
        "min_storage_mb": 150,
        "min_ram_mb": 512,
        "supported_abi": ["any"],
        "target_platform": "any",
    },
}


class SystemAuditor:
    """Inspects device specs and evaluates whether target apps/games can run safely."""

    def __init__(self, adb_target: Optional[str] = None):
        self.adb_target = adb_target

    def _run_cmd(self, cmd: list) -> str:
        if self.adb_target:
            full_cmd = ["adb", "-s", self.adb_target, "shell"] + cmd
        else:
            full_cmd = cmd
        res = subprocess.run(full_cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)
        return res.stdout.strip()

    def get_hardware_specs(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns storage, RAM, and architecture specs."""
        # 1. Available Storage
        total_storage_mb, free_storage_mb = 0, 0
        try:
            total, used, free = shutil.disk_usage(os.getcwd())
            total_storage_mb = total // (1024 * 1024)
            free_storage_mb = free // (1024 * 1024)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 2. Available RAM
        total_ram_mb = 0
        try:
            if os.path.exists("/proc/meminfo"):
                with open("/proc/meminfo", "r") as f:
                    for line in f:
                        if line.startswith("MemTotal:"):
                            total_ram_mb = int(line.split()[1]) // 1024
                            break
        except Exception:
            pass

        # 3. CPU Architecture / ABI
        abi = "unknown"
        if self.adb_target:
            abi = self._run_cmd(["getprop", "ro.product.cpu.abi"])
        if not abi or abi == "unknown":
            abi = platform.machine()

        return {
            "platform": platform.system().lower(),
            "cpu_arch": abi,
            "total_ram_mb": total_ram_mb,
            "free_storage_mb": free_storage_mb,
            "total_storage_mb": total_storage_mb,
        }

    def verify_app_compatibility(self, app_key: str) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """Verifies if the device meets the hardware spec for an app/game."""
        specs = self.get_hardware_specs()
        reqs = KNOWN_APP_REQUIREMENTS.get(app_key.lower())

        if not reqs:
            # Fallback general safety check: minimum 500MB free storage
            if specs["free_storage_mb"] < 500:
                return False, f"Low disk space: Only {specs['free_storage_mb']}MB free. Aborting download."
            return True, "Standard requirements met."

        # Check Storage
        if specs["free_storage_mb"] < reqs["min_storage_mb"]:
            return False, (
                f"Storage Inadequate: '{reqs['name']}' requires at least {reqs['min_storage_mb']}MB, "
                f"but only {specs['free_storage_mb']}MB is available. Download would fail."
            )

        # Check RAM if available
        if specs["total_ram_mb"] > 0 and specs["total_ram_mb"] < reqs["min_ram_mb"]:
            return False, (
                f"RAM Inadequate: '{reqs['name']}' requires {reqs['min_ram_mb']}MB RAM, "
                f"device only has {specs['total_ram_mb']}MB."
            )

        return True, f"Hardware compatible with {reqs['name']}."

# Setup clean workspace

with open(valid_file, "w") as f:
    f.write("def calculate(x):\n    return x * 2\n")

# -------------------------------------------------------------
# Case 1: Bad PR (Syntax Error, Nonexistent file, Speculative Invariant)
# -------------------------------------------------------------
print("=== CASE 1: SUBMITTING DEFECTIVE WORKER PR ===")
bad_actions = [
    {
        "tool_call": {
            "name": "ledger_record_invariant",
            "args": {
                "invariant": "Maybe assume this database connection works",
                "rationale": "Testing"
            }
        }
    }
]

worker = swarm.get_or_create_worker("builder", "Build code")
worker.worker.touch_file("broken_code.py")
worker.worker.touch_file("ghost_file.py")

success = swarm.run_pipeline_step(
    role="builder",
    instructions="Write routines",
    simulated_actions=bad_actions
)
print(f"Result: Approved={success}")

# -------------------------------------------------------------
# Case 2: Clean PR (Valid Syntax, Real File, Sound Invariant)
# -------------------------------------------------------------
print("\n=== CASE 2: SUBMITTING VALID WORKER PR ===")
worker.worker.data.files_touched.clear()
worker.worker.data.candidate_invariants.clear()
worker.worker.touch_file("valid_code.py")

clean_actions = [
    {
        "tool_call": {
            "name": "ledger_record_invariant",
            "args": {
                "invariant": "All calculate functions must return numeric types",
                "rationale": "Prevents downstream type cast errors"
            }
        }
    }
]

success = swarm.run_pipeline_step(
    role="builder",
    instructions="Write routines",
    simulated_actions=clean_actions,
    verified_facts=["All unit tests passed for valid_code.py"]
)
print(f"Result: Approved={success}")

# Verify Root Ledger state
final_state = swarm.root_ledger.get_read_only_view()
print("\n=== FINAL ROOT LEDGER STATE ===")
print("Invariants:", final_state.invariants)
print("Modified Artifacts:", final_state.modified_artifacts)
